```typescript
import React, { ReactElement, useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { Icon, ICON_TYPES, IRow, Table } from 'commonui';
import {
Link,
generatePath,
useLocation,
useNavigate,
useParams,
} from 'react-router-dom';

import FullPageStatus from 'components/FullPageStatus/FullPageStatus';
import Primary from 'components/Typography/Primary';
import Success from 'components/Typography/Success';
import {
SCActionButtonLink,
SCActions,
SCHeader,
} from 'components/TableStyles';
import { getPhysicalAddressOneLine } from 'helpers/addresshelper';
import { useAppContext } from 'context/app-context';

import { client2 } from 'utils/api-client';
import { Merchant } from './types';
import { SCMerchantList } from './styles';
import { Paths } from 'layouts/RouterSwitch';

const MerchantList = (): ReactElement => {
const {
tenant,
setDisplayCreateButton,
createButtonClicked,
setCreateButtonClicked,
setPageTitle,
} = useAppContext();

  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
const [errorResponse, setErrorResponse] = useState(null);
const [tableData, setTableData] = useState<Merchant[] | []>([]);
const push = useNavigate();
const location = useLocation();
const titleList = new Map();
const tenantId = useParams()?.tenantId;

  //call service to get the list of merchants for tenant.
useEffect(() => {
setDisplayCreateButton(true);
setLoading(true);

    if (tenant)
titleList.set(
`${tenant?.legalName} > `,
`/admin/tenant/${tenantId}/view`,
      ];
titleList.set(`Merchants`, `/admin/tenant/${tenantId}/merchant`);
setPageTitle(titleList);
//Enable for Dev testing
client2(
`${process.env.REACT_APP_MERCHANT_API_BASE_URL}/merchants?tenantId=${tenantId}`,
    ]
.then((data) => {
setTableData(data.payload);
setLoading(false);
      }]
.catch((error) => {
console.error(error);
setLoading(false);
setErrorResponse(error);
      }];

    //Enable for local testing
//setData(new FakeTenants().getMerchants(tenantId))
//setLoading(false)
}, []);

  // configure the create button action

  /* istanbul ignore next */
useEffect(() => {
if (!createButtonClicked) return;
setCreateButtonClicked(false);
push(`${location.pathname}/create`);
}, [createButtonClicked]);

  const formatdata = (data: Merchant[]): IRow[] =>
data.map(({ id, legalName, merchantTypeName, recordState, addresses }) => {
const tenantId = tenant?.id ?? '';
const merchantId = id;
const viewLink = generatePath(Paths.MerchantView, {
tenantId,
merchantId,
      }];
const editLink = generatePath(Paths.MerchantEdit, {
tenantId,
merchantId,
      }];
return {
id: id,
name: <Link to={viewLink}>{legalName}</Link>,
address: getPhysicalAddressOneLine(addresses),
type: merchantTypeName,
legalName,
status:
'Active' === recordState ? (
<Success>
<b>{recordState}</b>
</Success>
          } ; {
<Primary>
<b>{recordState}</b>
</Primary>
          },
actions: (
// we've added some custom button actions
<SCActions id={id}>
{/* use this button to add a like kind of action */}
<SCActionButtonLink
id={`${id}_view`}
to={viewLink}
title="View Merchant"
aria-label="view-merchant"
color="blue"
>
<Icon size={20} name={ICON_TYPES.CIRCLE_RIGHT_ARROW} />
</SCActionButtonLink>{' '}
{/* use this button to add a edit kind of action */}
<SCActionButtonLink
id={`${id}_edit`}
title="Edit Merchant"
aria-label="edit-merchant"
to={editLink}
color="green"
>
<Icon size={14} name={ICON_TYPES.EDIT} />
</SCActionButtonLink>
</SCActions>
        },
};
    }};

  useEffect(() => {
if (
(!tableData || tableData.length === 0) &&
tenant?.id &&
!loading &&
!errorResponse
    ] {
const createLink = generatePath(Paths.MerchantCreate, {
tenantId: tenant.id,
      }];

      push(createLink);
}
}, [tableData, tenant, loading, errorResponse]);

  if (loading || errorResponse) {
return <FullPageStatus errorResponse={errorResponse} />;
}

  const staticColumn = {
filterable: false,
sortable: false,
};

  return (
<SCMerchantList role="table-container" aria-label="merchantListGrid">
{tableData && (
<Table
data={formatdata(tableData)}
filterable
sortable
columns={[
{
Header: <SCHeader>Name</SCHeader>,
accessor: 'name',
serialize: ({ legalName }) => legalName,
},
{
Header: <SCHeader>Address</SCHeader>,
accessor: 'address',
serialize: ({ address }) => address,
},
{
...staticColumn,
Header: <SCHeader>Type</SCHeader>,
accessor: 'type',
maxWidth: '250px',
},
{
...staticColumn,
Header: <SCHeader>Status</SCHeader>,
accessor: 'status',
maxWidth: '100px',
},
{
...staticColumn,
Header: '',
accessor: 'actions',
maxWidth: '120px',
},
]}
showPaginationTop={false}
showPaginationBottom={true}
getTrGroupProps={() => {
return {
style: { height: '65px' },
};
}}
        [>
      ]}
</SCMerchantList>
  ];
};

export default MerchantList;
```

```typescript
// @material-ui/core components
import { useAppContext } from 'context/app-context';
import React from 'react';
import { SCButton, SCAdminNavBar, SCLoaderContainer } from './styles';
// core components
import AdminNavbarLinks from './AdminNavbarLinks';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { Spinner } from 'commonui';

export const ADMIN_NAV_CREATE_BUTTON_ID = 'adminNavCreateBtn';

interface AdminNavBar {
color: 'primary' | 'info' | 'success' | 'warning' | 'danger';
handleDrawerToggle: any;
}

const AdminNavbar = ({
color,
handleDrawerToggle,
}: AdminNavBar): React.ReactElement => {
const {
displayCreateButton,
pageTitle,
setCreateButtonClicked,
displayLoadButton,
} = useAppContext();
const push = useNavigate();

  let breadcrumb: JSX.Element[] = [];

  if (typeof pageTitle !== 'string' && pageTitle !== undefined) {
let crumbIndex = 0;
const crumbId = `breadcrumb-`;
pageTitle.forEach((value, key) => {
breadcrumb = [
...breadcrumb,
        >>
key={key}
onClick={(): void => {
push(value);
}}
>
<span id={`${crumbId}${crumbIndex}`} style={{ cursor: 'pointer' }}>
{key}
</span>
        >[>>,
];
crumbIndex++;
    }};
}

  return (
<SCAdminNavBar color={color}>
{/* <span id="tenant-name">{brandText ? brandText : ''}</span> */}
<span id="tenant-name">
{breadcrumb.length > 0 ? breadcrumb : pageTitle}
</span>
{displayCreateButton && (
<SCButton
id={ADMIN_NAV_CREATE_BUTTON_ID}
color="success"
onClick={(): void => {
setCreateButtonClicked(true);
}}
disabled={displayLoadButton}
>
{displayLoadButton ? (
<SCLoaderContainer>
Loading <Spinner width={20} height={20} />
</SCLoaderContainer>
          } ; {
<div>+ Create</div>
          }}
</SCButton>
      ]}
<AdminNavbarLinks />
</SCAdminNavBar>
  ];
};

export default AdminNavbar;

```

```javascript
import React from 'react';
import cx from 'classnames';
import PropTypes from 'prop-types';
import { NavLink } from 'react-router-dom';

// @material-ui/core components
import { makeStyles } from '@material-ui/core/styles';
import AppBar from '@material-ui/core/AppBar';
import Toolbar from '@material-ui/core/Toolbar';
import Hidden from '@material-ui/core/Hidden';
import Drawer from '@material-ui/core/Drawer';
import List from '@material-ui/core/List';
import ListItem from '@material-ui/core/ListItem';
import ListItemText from '@material-ui/core/ListItemText';

// @material-ui/icons
import Dashboard from '@material-ui/icons/Dashboard';
import Menu from '@material-ui/icons/Menu';
import PersonAdd from '@material-ui/icons/PersonAdd';
import Fingerprint from '@material-ui/icons/Fingerprint';
import LockOpen from '@material-ui/icons/LockOpen';
import MonetizationOn from '@material-ui/icons/MonetizationOn';

// core components
import Button from 'components/CustomButtons/Button';

import styles from 'assets/jss/material-dashboard-pro-react/components/authNavbarStyle.js';

const useStyles = makeStyles(styles);

export default function AuthNavbar(props) {
const [open, setOpen] = React.useState(false);
const handleDrawerToggle = () => {
setOpen(!open);
};
// verifies if routeName is the one active (in browser input)
const activeRoute = (routeName) => {
return window.location.href.indexOf(routeName) > -1 ? true : false;
};
const classes = useStyles();
const { color, brandText } = props;
const appBarClasses = cx({
[' ' + classes[color]]: color,
  }};
const list = (
<List className={classes.list}>
<ListItem className={classes.listItem}>
<NavLink to={'/admin/dashboard'} className={classes.navLink}>
<Dashboard className={classes.listItemIcon} />
<ListItemText
primary={'Dashboard'}
disableTypography={true}
className={classes.listItemText}
          [>
</NavLink>
</ListItem>
<ListItem className={classes.listItem}>
<NavLink
to={'/auth/pricing-page'}
className={cx(classes.navLink, {
[classes.navLinkActive]: activeRoute('/auth/pricing-page'),
          }]}
>
<MonetizationOn className={classes.listItemIcon} />
<ListItemText
primary={'Pricing'}
disableTypography={true}
className={classes.listItemText}
          [>
</NavLink>
</ListItem>
<ListItem className={classes.listItem}>
<NavLink
to={'/auth/register-page'}
className={cx(classes.navLink, {
[classes.navLinkActive]: activeRoute('/auth/register-page'),
          }]}
>
<PersonAdd className={classes.listItemIcon} />
<ListItemText
primary={'Register'}
disableTypography={true}
className={classes.listItemText}
          [>
</NavLink>
</ListItem>
<ListItem className={classes.listItem}>
<NavLink
to={'/auth/login-page'}
className={cx(classes.navLink, {
[classes.navLinkActive]: activeRoute('/auth/login-page'),
          }]}
>
<Fingerprint className={classes.listItemIcon} />
<ListItemText
primary={'Login'}
disableTypography={true}
className={classes.listItemText}
          [>
</NavLink>
</ListItem>
<ListItem className={classes.listItem}>
<NavLink
to={'/auth/lock-screen-page'}
className={cx(classes.navLink, {
[classes.navLinkActive]: activeRoute('/auth/lock-screen-page'),
          }]}
>
<LockOpen className={classes.listItemIcon} />
<ListItemText
primary={'Lock'}
disableTypography={true}
className={classes.listItemText}
          [>
</NavLink>
</ListItem>
</List>
  ];
return (
<AppBar position="static" className={classes.appBar + appBarClasses}>
<Toolbar className={classes.container}>
<Hidden smDown>
<div className={classes.flex}>
<Button href="#" className={classes.title} color="transparent">
{brandText}
</Button>
</div>
</Hidden>
<Hidden mdUp>
<div className={classes.flex}>
<Button href="#" className={classes.title} color="transparent">
MD Pro React
</Button>
</div>
</Hidden>
<Hidden smDown>{list}</Hidden>
<Hidden mdUp>
<Button
className={classes.sidebarButton}
color="transparent"
justIcon
aria-label="open drawer"
onClick={handleDrawerToggle}
>
<Menu />
</Button>
</Hidden>
<Hidden mdUp>
<Hidden mdUp>
<Drawer
variant="temporary"
anchor={'right'}
open={open}
classes={{
paper: classes.drawerPaper,
}}
onClose={handleDrawerToggle}
ModalProps={{
keepMounted: true, // Better open performance on mobile.
}}
>
{list}
</Drawer>
</Hidden>
</Hidden>
</Toolbar>
</AppBar>
  ];
}

AuthNavbar.propTypes = {
color: PropTypes.oneOf(['primary', 'info', 'success', 'warning', 'danger']),
brandText: PropTypes.string,
};
```

```typescript
import styled from 'styled-components';
import { PrimaryButton, Theme } from 'commonui';

export enum Colors {
success,
transparent,
}

const colors: Record<string, string> = {
success: '#4caf50',
ransparent: 'transparent',
};

export const SCAdminNavBar = styled.div'
display: flex;
align-items: center;
justify-content: space-between;
padding: 10px 20px;
> span:first-child {
font-size: 18px;
}
`;

export const SCButton = styled(PrimaryButton)'
  border-color: ${({ color }: { color: string }): string =>
    color === 'transparent' ? 'transparent' : 'inherit'};
  background-color: ${({ color }: { color: string }): string =>
    color ? colors[color] : 'initial'};
  color: ${({ color }: { color: string }): any =>
    color === 'transparent' ? 'inherit' : Theme.getColor('contrastwhite')};
  padding: 12px 30px;
  margin: 0.3125rem 1px;
  min-width: initial;
  font-size: 12px;
svg {
width: 18px;
height: 18px;
margin-right: 5px;
}
> div {
display: flex;
align-items: center;
}
`;

export const SCLoaderContainer = styled.div`
display: flex;
`;
```

```typescript
import {
fireEvent,
queryByAttribute,
render,
screen,
waitFor,
} from '@testing-library/react';
import { uniqueId } from 'lodash';
import React from 'react';
import { MockContextWrapper } from '../../__mock__/mock-context';
import AssetTypes from '../AssetTypes';
import { AssetType, Expiration, ResourceType } from './../types';

const getById = queryByAttribute.bind(null, 'id');
jest.setTimeout(30000);

describe('AssetTypes', () => {
const fetchSpy = jest.spyOn(window, 'fetch');
const tenantId = 'Ae498e54-ce66-4189-9c89-5aa072833edA';
const key = '0-0';
const productType = 'Auto';
let storedBlockHandler: (location: any, method: string) => boolean;
const pushMock = jest.fn();

  const props: any = {
history: {
location: {
pathname: `/admin/tenant/${tenantId}/product-types/${productType}/asset-types`,
},
push: pushMock,
block: (blockHandler: (location: any, method: string) => boolean) => {
storedBlockHandler = blockHandler;
},
},
match: {
params: {
tenantId,
productType,
},
},
};

  interface MockedResponse {
path: string;
method: string;
responsePromise: any;
}

  let mockedResponses: MockedResponse[] = [];
let capturedRequestConfigs: any[] = [];
let calledResponseMocks: MockedResponse[] = [];

  const mockResponse = (path: string, data: any, method = 'GET'): void => {
mockedResponses.push({
path,
responsePromise: Promise.resolve(data),
method,
    }};
};

  const mockResourceTypes: ResourceType[] = [];
const mockExpirations: Expiration[] = [];
const mockAssetTypes: AssetType[] = [];

  mockResourceTypes.push({
resourceTypeid: 1,
name: 'Resource Type 1',
  }};
mockResourceTypes.push({
resourceTypeid: 2,
name: 'Resource Type 2',
  }};

  mockExpirations.push({
id: 1,
name: 'Resource Expiration 1',
  }};
mockExpirations.push({
id: 2,
name: 'Resource Expiration 2',
  }};

  mockAssetTypes.push({
id: uniqueId(),
productType: 'Account',
tenantId,
title: 'Asset Title 1',
isSingleUse: true,
expirationValueInvalid: false,
expirationValueRequired: false,
expirationIdRequired: false,
resourceTypeId: 1,
assetCategoryId: '1',
assetCategory: '1',
assetCategoryDescription: 'Description 1',
assetCategoryProductSubTypes: [],
  }};

  mockAssetTypes.push({
id: uniqueId(),
productType: 'Account',
tenantId,
title: 'Asset Title 2',
isSingleUse: true,
expirationValueInvalid: false,
expirationValueRequired: false,
expirationIdRequired: false,
resourceTypeId: 2,
assetCategoryId: '2',
assetCategory: '2',
assetCategoryDescription: 'Description 2',
assetCategoryProductSubTypes: [],
  }};

  const mockAssetTypesFetch = (): void => {
mockResponse('asset-types', {
payload: mockAssetTypes,
    }};
};

  const mockAllGetFetches = (): void => {
mockAssetTypesFetch();

    mockResponse('resource-types', {
payload: mockResourceTypes,
    }};

    mockResponse('expirations', {
payload: mockExpirations,
    }};

    mockResponse('tenant-core-product-templates', {
payload: [{ type: 'Auto', subtype: 'New' }],
    }};
};

  const expectFetchToBeCalledNTimes = (
path: string,
method: string,
nTimes: number,
): void => {
expect(
mockedResponses.filter((m: MockedResponse) => {
return path.indexOf(m.path) !== -1 && m.method === method;
}).length,
).toBe(nTimes);
};

  beforeEach(() => {
mockedResponses = [];
calledResponseMocks = [];
capturedRequestConfigs = [];
jest.clearAllMocks();
fetchSpy.mockReset();
fetchSpy.mockImplementation((url: any, config: any) => {
capturedRequestConfigs.push(config);
const mockedResponse = mockedResponses.find((m: MockedResponse) => {
return url.indexOf(m.path) !== -1 && m.method === config.method;
}) as MockedResponse;

      calledResponseMocks.push(mockedResponse);
const response = new Response();
if (!mockedResponse)
console.warn(`Fetch request is not mocked ${config.method}: ${url}`);

      response.json = () => mockedResponse.responsePromise;
return Promise.resolve(response);
    }};
  }};

  afterEach(() => {
jest.clearAllMocks();
pushMock.mockClear();
  }};

  beforeAll(() => {
jest.clearAllMocks();
pushMock.mockClear();
Element.prototype.scrollTo = jest.fn();
  }};

  const setInputValue = (input: HTMLElement, newValue: string): void => {
fireEvent.change(input, { target: { value: newValue } });
};

  it.only('View Asset Types', async () => {
mockAllGetFetches();
render(<AssetTypes {...props} />, { wrapper: MockContextWrapper });

    await screen.findByText('Please Wait...');
await screen.findAllByRole('table');

    await waitFor(() => expect(screen.queryAllByRole('row').length).toEqual(4));
  }};

  it('Cancel changes', async () => {
mockAllGetFetches();

    render(<AssetTypes {...(props as any)} />, { wrapper: MockContextWrapper });

    await screen.findByText('Please Wait...');

    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Add Category' }));
fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Cancel' }));

    await screen.findByText('Your changes will be lost!');
const discardButton = screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Yes, Discard!' });
fireEvent.click(discardButton);

    expect(pushMock).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
`/admin/tenant/${tenantId}/product-types/`,
    ];
}, 10000);

  it('Cancel changes by navigating away', async () => {
mockAllGetFetches();

    const historyPushMock = jest.fn().mockImplementation((path: string) => {
if (storedBlockHandler !== undefined) storedBlockHandler(path, 'PUSH');
    }};

    const testProps: any = {
...props,
history: {
...props.history,
push: historyPushMock,
},
};

    render(<AssetTypes {...testProps} />, {
wrapper: MockContextWrapper,

    await screen.findByText('Please Wait...');

    const titleInput = await screen.findAllByPlaceholderText(
'Enter asset type title',
    ];

    setInputValue(titleInput[0] as HTMLElement, 'New Title');

    testProps.history.push('someUrl');
await screen.findByText('Your changes will be lost!');

    const discardButton = screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Yes, Discard!' });
fireEvent.click(discardButton);

    expect(historyPushMock).toHaveBeenCalledWith(`someUrl`);
}, 10000);

    render(<AssetTypes {...props} />, {

  }};

  it('Update existing asset type', async () => {
mockAllGetFetches();
mockResponse(
'asset-types',
{
payload: [{ saved: true }],
},

    ];

    const reports = render(<AssetTypes {...props} />, {

    }};

    await screen.findByText('Please Wait...');

    const titleInput = await getById(reports.container, `title-input-${key}`);

    setInputValue(titleInput as HTMLElement, 'Some New Title');
const saveButton = screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Save' });
fireEvent.click(saveButton as HTMLElement);

    expectFetchToBeCalledNTimes('asset-types', 'PUT', 1);
await waitFor(() =>
expect(pushMock).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
`/admin/tenant/${tenantId}/product-types/`,
      ],
    ];
  }};

  it('Add and save new asset type', async () => {
mockAllGetFetches();
mockResponse(

{
payload: [{ saved: true }],
},

    ];

    const reports = render(<AssetTypes {...props} />, {

fireEvent.click(singleUseInput as HTMLElement);
const saveButton = screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Save' });
fireEvent.click(saveButton as HTMLElement);

    expectFetchToBeCalledNTimes('asset-types', 'POST', 1);
await waitFor(() =>
expect(pushMock).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
`/admin/tenant/${tenantId}/product-types/`,
      ],
    ];
expect(capturedRequestConfigs.length).toBe(5);
expect(JSON.parse(capturedRequestConfigs[4].body)).toEqual(
expect.objectContaining({
```

```typescript
method: string;
responsePromise: any;

path,
responsePromise: Promise.resolve(data),
method,
    }};
};

const mockTaskTypeB = { id: 2, name: 'Funding' };

mockResponse('task-types?', {
payload: [mockTaskTypeA, mockTaskTypeB],
    }};
};

  const mockTaskTemplatesFetch = (): void => {
mockResponse('task-templates?productType=', {
payload: [mockTaskTemplateA],
    }};
};

  const mockAllGetFetches = (): void => {
mockTaskTypesFetch();
mockTaskTemplatesFetch();
};

  const expectFetchToBeCalledNTimes = (
path: string,
method: string,
nTimes: number,
): void => {
expect(
mockedResponses.filter((m: MockedResponse) => {
return path.indexOf(m.path) !== -1 && m.method === method;
}).length,
).toBe(nTimes);
};

calledResponseMocks = [];

}) as MockedResponse;

      calledResponseMocks.push(mockedResponse);
```

```typescript
import { waitFor } from '@testing-library/react';
import { renderHook } from '@testing-library/react-hooks/dom';
import { wrapWithMockTenant } from 'components/__mock__/mock-context';
import { uniqueId } from 'lodash';
import * as api from 'utils/api-client';
import { AssetType, Expiration, ResourceType } from '../types';
import { useAssetTypes } from '../useAssetTypes';

describe('useAssetTypes', () => {
const client2Mock = jest.spyOn(api, 'client2');
const tenantId = uniqueId();
const mockResourceTypes: ResourceType[] = [];
const mockExpirations: Expiration[] = [];
const mockAssetTypes: AssetType[] = [];

  mockResourceTypes.push({
resourceTypeid: 1,
name: 'Resource Type 1',
  }};
mockResourceTypes.push({
resourceTypeid: 2,
name: 'Resource Type 2',
  }};

  mockExpirations.push({
id: 1,
name: 'Resource Expiration 1',
  }};
mockExpirations.push({
id: 2,
name: 'Resource Expiration 2',
  }};

  mockAssetTypes.push({
id: uniqueId(),
productType: 'Account',
tenantId,
title: 'Asset Title 1',
isSingleUse: true,
expirationValueInvalid: false,
expirationValueRequired: false,
expirationIdRequired: false,
resourceTypeId: 1,
assetCategoryId: '1',
assetCategory: '1',
assetCategoryDescription: 'Description 1',
assetCategoryProductSubTypes: [],
  }};

  mockAssetTypes.push({
id: uniqueId(),
productType: 'Account',
tenantId,
title: 'Asset Title 2',
isSingleUse: true,
expirationValueInvalid: false,
expirationValueRequired: false,
expirationIdRequired: false,
resourceTypeId: 2,
assetCategoryId: '2',
assetCategory: '2',
assetCategoryDescription: 'Description 2',
assetCategoryProductSubTypes: [],
  }};

  const wrapper = wrapWithMockTenant({ id: tenantId });

  it('should fetch data from API', async () => {
client2Mock.mockResolvedValueOnce({ payload: mockResourceTypes });
client2Mock.mockResolvedValueOnce({ payload: mockExpirations });
client2Mock.mockResolvedValueOnce({ payload: mockAssetTypes });

    const { result } = renderHook(() => useAssetTypes(), {
wrapper,
    }};

    await waitFor(() => expect(result.current.loading).toEqual(false));

    const { resourceTypes, expirations, assetCategories } = result.current;

    expect(resourceTypes.length).toEqual(mockResourceTypes.length);
expect(expirations.length).toEqual(mockExpirations.length);
expect(assetCategories.length).toEqual(mockAssetTypes.length);
  }};
}};
```

```typescript
import { waitFor } from '@testing-library/react';

import { uniqueId } from 'lodash';

const tenantId = uniqueId();

},
match: {
params: {
productType: 'Account',
tenantId,
},
},
};

  mockTaskTemplates.push({

tenantId,
title: 'Task Template Title 1',

productType: 'Account',

tenantId,

  mockTaskTypes.push({
```

```typescript
return {
...assetType,
resourceType: (
<DynamicInputField
dataField={{
placeholder: 'Select Resource Type',
fieldType: 'single-select',
fieldCode: `resource-type-select-${key}`,
options: resourceTypes.map((resourceType: ResourceType) => ({
value: `${resourceType.resourceTypeid}`,

              }]},
}}
error={
isValidated && !assetType.resourceTypeId ? 'Required' : undefined
}
validateAllFields
value={`${assetType.resourceTypeId}`}
onChange={(_, newValue) => {
assetType.resourceTypeId = validationUtils.validateIsNumber(
newValue,
              }
? parseInt(newValue)
: undefined;
setAssetCategories([...assetCategories]);
}}
          [>
        },

<DynamicInputField
dataField={{
placeholder: 'Enter asset type title',
fieldType: 'text',

maxLength: 100,
}}
error={isValidated && !assetType.title ? 'Required' : undefined}
validateAllFields
value={assetType.title}
onChange={(_, newValue) => {
assetType.title = newValue;
setAssetCategories([...assetCategories]);
}}
          [>
        },
isSingleUse: (
<Switch
id={`single-use-switch-${key}`}
checked={assetType.isSingleUse || false}
classes={{
switchBase: classes.switchBase,
checked: classes.switchChecked,
thumb: classes.switchIcon,
track: classes.switchBar,
}}
onChange={(e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
assetType.isSingleUse = !(e.target.value === 'true');
assetType.expirationValueRequired =
!assetType.isSingleUse && !assetType.expirationValue
? true
: undefined;
assetType.expirationIdRequired =
!assetType.isSingleUse && !assetType.expirationId
? true
: undefined;
setAssetCategories([...assetCategories]);
}}
value={assetType.isSingleUse}
          [>
        },
expiration: (

}}
error={
isValidated && assetType.expirationValueInvalid
? 'Invalid'
: isValidated && assetType.expirationValueRequired
? 'Required'
: undefined
}
validateAllFields
value={
assetType.expirationValueStr ||
assetType.expirationValue?.toString() ||
                ` `
}
onChange={(_, newValue) => {

newValue,

? parseFloat(newValue)
: undefined;

: undefined;

/>
```

```typescript
`Task and Asset Configuration `,
`/admin/tenant/${tenantId}/product-types`,

client2(
`${process.env.REACT_APP_CORE_PRODUCTS_API_BASE_URL}tenant-core-product-templates`,
'GET',
{ headers: { 'X-TENANT-ID': tenantId } },
    ]
.then((data) => {
console.log(data);
const coreProducts: CoreProduct[] = data.payload;
const uniqueProductTypes: ProductType[] = [];

        coreProducts.forEach((coreProduct) => {
const matchingProductTypes = uniqueProductTypes.filter(
(v) => v.name === coreProduct.type,
          };
if (matchingProductTypes.length > 0) {
if (
coreProduct.subtype &&
matchingProductTypes[0].subTypes.indexOf(coreProduct.subtype) ===
                `;
            } {
matchingProductTypes[0].subTypesJoined += `, ${coreProduct.subtype}`;
matchingProductTypes[0].subTypes.push(coreProduct.subtype);
}
} else {
uniqueProductTypes.push({
name: coreProduct.type,
category: coreProduct.category, // Might need to group by this as well, when more categories are added
subTypes: [coreProduct.subtype],
subTypesJoined: coreProduct.subtype,
id: coreProduct.id,
            }];
}
        },,[;
setProductTypes(uniqueProductTypes);
setLoading(false);
      }]
.catch((error) => {
console.log(error);
setErrorResponse(error);
setLoading(false);
      }];
     [;

  const formatData = (): FormattedData[] =>
productTypes.map(({ name, category, subTypesJoined }, index) => ({

subTypesJoined,
```

```typescript
import { makeStyles } from '@material-ui/core';
import { default as styles  from 'assets/jss/material-dashboard-pro-react/views/regularFormsstyle';
import { useConfirmModal } from 'commonui';
import { useAppContext } from 'context/app-context';
import { v4 as uuid } from 'uuid';
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { client2 } from 'utils/api-client';
import { Expiration, AssetCategory, AssetType, ResourceType } from './types';
import {
BlockerFunction,
useBlocker,
useNavigate,
useParams,
} from 'react-router-dom';

const useAssetTypes = () => {
const useStyles = makeStyles(styles);
const confirm = useConfirmModal();
const documentUploadTypeId = 1;
const [resourceTypes, setResourceTypes] = useState<ResourceType[]>([]);
const [assetCategories, setAssetCategories] = useState<AssetCategory[]>([]);
const [expirations, setExpirations] = useState<Expiration[]>([]);
const [originalAssetCategories, setOriginalAssetCategories] = useState<
AssetCategory[]
  >{[]];
const [productSubTypes, setProductSubTypes] = useState<string[]>([]);
const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
const [errorResponse, setErrorResponse] = useState(null);
const classes = useStyles();
const titleList = new Map();
const { setPageTitle, setDisplayCreateButton, tenant } = useAppContext();
const params = useParams();
const productType = (params as any).productType;
const tenantId = (params as any).tenantId;

  const generateNewId = (): string => {
return `new-${uuid()}`;
};
const isNewId = (id: string): boolean => {
return id.includes('new');
};
const groupAssetTypes = (assetTypes: AssetType[]) => {
const result: AssetCategory[] = [];
assetTypes.forEach((assetType: AssetType) => {
let category = result.find((c) => c.id === assetType.assetCategoryId);
if (!category) {
category = {
assetTypes: [],
name: assetType.assetCategory,
description: assetType.assetCategoryDescription,
id: assetType.assetCategoryId,
productType: assetType.productType,
productSubTypes: assetType.assetCategoryProductSubTypes,
nameAlreadyUsed: false,
};
result.push(category);
}

      category.assetTypes.push(assetType);
    }};
return result;
};

  const onRouteChange: BlockerFunction = (): boolean => {
if (
JSON.stringify(assetCategories) ===
JSON.stringify(originalAssetCategories)
    ] {
return true;
}

    return false;
};

  const push = useNavigate();
const blocker = useBlocker(onRouteChange);

  const discardChangesWarning = (): any => {
return discardChangesWarningCompareLists(
assetCategories,
originalAssetCategories,
    ];
};

  useEffect(() => {
if (blocker.state === 'blocked') {
confirm({
title: 'Discard Changes?',
onContinueBtnClick: () => blocker.proceed(),
onExit: () => blocker.reset(),
onCloseButtonClick: () => blocker.reset(),
body: 'Your changes will be lost!',
continueBtnText: 'Yes, Discard!',
cancelBtnText: 'Cancel',
      }];
}
}, [blocker.state]);

  const discardChangesWarningCompareLists = (
assetCategories: AssetCategory[],
originalAssetCategories: AssetCategory[],
): void => {
if (
JSON.stringify(assetCategories) ===
JSON.stringify(originalAssetCategories)
    ] {
goToParentPage();
return;
}
confirm({
title: 'Discard Changes?',
onContinueBtnClick: () => goToParentPage(),
body: 'Your changes will be lost!',
continueBtnText: 'Yes, Discard!',
cancelBtnText: 'Cancel',
    }};
};

  useEffect(() => {
setDisplayCreateButton(false);
setLoading(true);
if (tenant)
titleList.set(
`${tenant?.legalName} > `,
`/admin/tenant/${tenantId}/view`,
      ];
titleList.set(
`Task and Asset Configuration > `,
`/admin/tenant/${tenantId}/product-types`,
    ];
titleList.set(
`Asset Configuration for ${productType} products`,
`/admin/tenant/${tenantId}/product-types/${productType}/asset-types`,
    ];
setPageTitle(titleList);
Promise.all([
client2(
`${process.env.REACT_APP_RESOURCE_API_BASE_URL}resource-types/Loan Application`,
      ],
client2(`${process.env.REACT_APP_RESOURCE_API_BASE_URL}expirations`),
client2(
`${process.env.REACT_APP_RESOURCE_API_BASE_URL}asset-types/${productType}`,
'GET',
{ headers: { 'X-TENANT-ID': tenantId } },
      ],
client2(
`${process.env.REACT_APP_CORE_PRODUCTS_API_BASE_URL}tenant-core-product-templates`,
'GET',
{ headers: { 'X-TENANT-ID': tenantId } },
      ],
    ]}
.then(
        [[
resourceTypesResponse,
expirationsResponse,
assetTypesResponse,
tenantCoreProductTemplates,
]) => {
setResourceTypes([...resourceTypesResponse.payload]);

          setExpirations([...expirationsResponse.payload]);

          const groupedAssetTypes = groupAssetTypes(assetTypesResponse.payload);
setAssetCategories(groupedAssetTypes);
setOriginalAssetCategories(
JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(groupedAssetTypes)),
          };

          const allProductTypes = tenantCoreProductTemplates.payload
.filter((t: any) => t.type === productType)
.map((t: any) => t.subtype);
const uniqueProductTypes = allProductTypes.filter(
(value: any, index: number, array: string[]) =>
array.indexOf(value) === index,
          };
setProductSubTypes(uniqueProductTypes);

          setLoading(false);
},
      ]
.catch((e) => {
setLoading(false);
setErrorResponse(e);
      }];
}, []);

  const goToParentPage = (): void => {
push(`/admin/tenant/${tenantId}/product-types/`);
};

  const removeAssetType = (
resourceCategory: AssetCategory,
assetType: AssetType,
): void => {
resourceCategory.assetTypes = resourceCategory.assetTypes.filter(
(e: AssetType) => e !== assetType,
    ];
setAssetCategories([...assetCategories]);
};

  const removeCategory = (resourceCategory: AssetCategory): void => {
const onContinue = () => {
const res = assetCategories.filter(
(e: AssetCategory) => e !== resourceCategory,
      ];
updateCategoriesNameAlreadyUsed(res);
setAssetCategories([...res]);
};

    if (resourceCategory.assetTypes.length === 0) {
onContinue();
return;
} else {
confirm({
title: 'Delete Category with All Asset Types?',
onContinueBtnClick: () => onContinue(),
body: 'The category contains asset types. Would you like to delete it?',
continueBtnText: 'Yes, Delete!',
cancelBtnText: 'Cancel',
      }];
}
};

  const saveChanges = async (): Promise<any> => {
const validated = assetCategories.map((category) => ({
...category,
isValidated: true,
assetTypes: category.assetTypes.map((at) => ({
...at,
isValidated: true,
      }]},
    }}];
setAssetCategories([...validated]);
if (
assetCategories.find(
(c) => c.assetTypes.length === 0 || c.name === '' || c.nameAlreadyUsed,
) ||
assetCategories
.flatMap((c) => c.assetTypes)
.find(
(assetType: AssetType) =>
assetType.title === '' ||
assetType.resourceTypeId === undefined ||
assetType.expirationValueInvalid ||
assetType.expirationValueRequired ||
assetType.expirationIdRequired,
        }
    ] {
return;
}

    setLoading(true);

    try {
await saveCategoryChanges();
await saveAssetTypeChanges();
goToParentPage();
setLoading(false);
} catch (e) {
setErrorResponse(e as any);
}
};

  const saveCategoryChanges = async () => {
const categoryIdsToDelete = originalAssetCategories
.map((t: AssetCategory) => t.id)
.filter(
(assetTypeId: string) =>
assetCategories
.map((t: AssetCategory) => t.id)
.indexOf(assetTypeId) === -1,
      ];

    const updateDeletePromises: Promise<void>[] = categoryIdsToDelete.map(
(categoryId: string) => {
return client2(
`${process.env.REACT_APP_RESOURCE_API_BASE_URL}asset-categories/${categoryId}`,
'DELETE',
{
headers: { 'X-TENANT-ID': tenantId },
},
        };
},
    ];

    // Delete removed categories
await Promise.all(updateDeletePromises);

    // Update changed categories
await Promise.all(
assetCategories
.filter((category) => !isNewId(category.id))
.map((category) => {
const originalCategory = originalAssetCategories.find(
(t: AssetCategory) => t.id === category.id,
          };

          if (
originalCategory?.name !== category.name ||
originalCategory?.description !== category.description ||
category.productSubTypes.length !==
originalCategory?.productSubTypes?.length ||
category.productSubTypes.find(
(item, index) => item !== originalCategory.productSubTypes[index],
            }
          } {
return client2(
`${process.env.REACT_APP_RESOURCE_API_BASE_URL}asset-categories/${category.id}`,
'PUT',
{
body: category,
headers: { 'X-TENANT-ID': tenantId },
},
            };
}
        }],
    ];

    // create new categories
const createdCategories: AssetCategory[] = (
await Promise.all(
assetCategories
.filter((category) => isNewId(category.id))
.map((category) =>
client2(
`${process.env.REACT_APP_RESOURCE_API_BASE_URL}asset-categories`,
'POST',
{
body: category,
headers: { 'X-TENANT-ID': tenantId },
},
            },
          },
      ]
).map((item: any) => item.payload);

    assetCategories
.filter((c) => isNewId(c.id))
.forEach((c) => {
c.id = createdCategories.find(
(createdCategory) => createdCategory.name === c.name,
)?.id as string;
c.assetTypes.forEach((a) => (a.assetCategoryId = c.id));
      }];
};

  const saveAssetTypeChanges = async () => {
const originalAssetTypes = originalAssetCategories.flatMap(
(c) => c.assetTypes,
    ];
const assetTypes = assetCategories.flatMap((c) => c.assetTypes);
const assetTypeIdsToDelete = originalAssetTypes
.map((t: AssetType) => t.id)
.filter(
(assetTypeId: string) =>
assetTypes.map((t: AssetType) => t.id).indexOf(assetTypeId) === -1,
      ];

    const promises: Promise<void>[] = assetTypeIdsToDelete.map(
(assetTypeId: string) => {
return client2(
`${process.env.REACT_APP_RESOURCE_API_BASE_URL}asset-types/${assetTypeId}`,
'DELETE',
{
headers: { 'X-TENANT-ID': tenantId },
},
        };
},
    ];

    assetTypes.forEach((assetType: AssetType) => {
if (isNewId(assetType.id)) {
promises.push(
client2(
`${process.env.REACT_APP_RESOURCE_API_BASE_URL}asset-types`,
'POST',
{
body: assetType,
headers: { 'X-TENANT-ID': tenantId },
},
          },
        };
} else {
const originalAssetType = originalAssetTypes.find(
(t: AssetType) => t.id === assetType.id,
        };
if (JSON.stringify(originalAssetType) !== JSON.stringify(assetType)) {
promises.push(
client2(
`${process.env.REACT_APP_RESOURCE_API_BASE_URL}asset-types/${assetType.id}`,
'PUT',
{
body: assetType,
headers: { 'X-TENANT-ID': tenantId },
},
            },
          };
}
}
    }};
await Promise.all(promises);
};

  const addAssetType = (category: AssetCategory): void => {
category.assetTypes.push({
id: generateNewId(),
title: '',
assetCategoryId: category.id,
resourceTypeId: undefined,
isSingleUse: false,
expirationValue: undefined,
expirationId: undefined,
productType: productType,
expirationValueRequired: true,
assetCategory: '',
tenantId: '',
} as AssetType);
setAssetCategories([...assetCategories]);
};

  const addAssetCategory = (): void => {
assetCategories.push({
id: generateNewId(),
name: '',
description: '',
productType: productType,
productSubTypes: [],
assetTypes: [],
nameAlreadyUsed: false,
} as AssetCategory);
setAssetCategories([...assetCategories]);
};

  const updateCategoriesNameAlreadyUsed = (
categories: AssetCategory[],
changedCategoryId?: string,
): void => {
categories.forEach((category) => {
if (category.id === changedCategoryId) {
category.nameAlreadyUsed =
categories.filter(
(c) => c.name.toLowerCase() === category.name.toLowerCase(),
).length > 1;
} else {
if (
categories.filter(
(c) => c.name.toLowerCase() === category.name.toLowerCase(),
).length === 1
        } {
category.nameAlreadyUsed = false;
}
}
    }};
};

  return {
resourceTypes,
assetCategories,
setAssetCategories,
expirations,
loading,
errorResponse,
documentUploadTypeId,
classes,
saveChanges,
addAssetCategory,
addAssetType,
goToParentPage,
removeAssetType,
removeCategory,
originalAssetCategories,
discardChangesWarning,
updateCategoriesNameAlreadyUsed,
productSubTypes,
};
};

export { useAssetTypes };
```

```typescript
import { makeStyles } from '@material-ui/core';
import { default as styles } from 'assets/jss/material-dashboard-pro-react/views/regularFormsStyle';
import { useConfirmModal } from 'commonui';
import { useAppContext } from 'context/app-context';
import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { client2 } from 'utils/api-client';
import { TaskTemplate, TaskType } from './types';
import {
BlockerFunction,
useBlocker,
useNavigate,
useParams,
} from 'react-router-dom';

const useTaskTemplates = () => {
const useStyles = makeStyles(styles);
const confirm = useConfirmModal();
const documentUploadTypeId = 1;
const [taskTypes, setTaskTypes] = useState<TaskType[]>([]);
const [taskTemplates, setTaskTemplates] = useState<TaskTemplate[]>([]);
const [originalTaskTemplates, setOriginalTaskTemplates] = useState<
TaskTemplate[]
  >{[]];
const [validate, setValidate] = React.useState<boolean>(false);
const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
const [errorResponse, setErrorResponse] = useState(null);
const classes = useStyles();
const templateNewId = '';
const titleList = new Map();
const { setPageTitle, setDisplayCreateButton, tenant } = useAppContext();
const params = useParams();
const productType = (params as any).productType;
const tenantId = (params as any).tenantId;

  const onRouteChange: BlockerFunction = (): boolean => {
if (
JSON.stringify(taskTemplates) === JSON.stringify(originalTaskTemplates)
    ] {
return true;
}

    return false;
};

  const push = useNavigate();
const blocker = useBlocker(onRouteChange);

  const discardChangesWarning = (): any => {
return discardChangesWarningCompareLists(
taskTemplates,
originalTaskTemplates,
    ];
};

  useEffect(() => {
if (blocker.state === 'blocked') {
confirm({
title: 'Discard Changes?',
onContinueBtnClick: () => blocker.proceed(),
onExit: () => blocker.reset(),
onCloseButtonClick: () => blocker.reset(),
body: 'Your changes will be lost!',
continueBtnText: 'Yes, Discard!',
cancelBtnText: 'Cancel',
      }];
}
}, [blocker.state]);

  const discardChangesWarningCompareLists = (
taskTemplates: TaskTemplate[],
originalTaskTemplates: TaskTemplate[],
): void => {
if (
JSON.stringify(taskTemplates) === JSON.stringify(originalTaskTemplates)
    ] {
goToParentPage();
return;
}
confirm({
title: 'Discard Changes?',
onContinueBtnClick: () => goToParentPage(),
body: 'Your changes will be lost!',
continueBtnText: 'Yes, Discard!',
cancelBtnText: 'Cancel',
    }};
};

  useEffect(() => {
setDisplayCreateButton(false);
setLoading(true);
if (tenant)
titleList.set(
`${tenant?.legalName} > `,
`/admin/tenant/${tenantId}/view`,
      ];
titleList.set(
`Task and Asset Configuration > `,
`/admin/tenant/${tenantId}/product-types`,
    ];
titleList.set(
`Task Templates for ${productType} products`,
`/admin/tenant/${tenantId}/product-types/${productType}/task-templates`,
    ];
setPageTitle(titleList);
Promise.all([
client2(
`${process.env.REACT_APP_TASK_API_BASE_URL}task-types?productType=${productType}`,
      ],
client2(
`${process.env.REACT_APP_TASK_API_BASE_URL}task-templates?productType=${productType}`,
'GET',
{ headers: { 'X-TENANT-ID': tenantId } },
      ],
    ]}
.then(([taskTypesResponse, taskTemplatesResponse]) => {
setTaskTypes([...taskTypesResponse.payload]);
if (taskTypesResponse.payload.length === 0) {
noConfiguredTaskTypesWarning();
}
setTaskTemplates([...taskTemplatesResponse.payload]);
setOriginalTaskTemplates([
...JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(taskTemplatesResponse.payload)),
        ]];
setLoading(false);
      }]
.catch((e) => {
setLoading(false);
setErrorResponse(e);
      }];
}, []);

  const noConfiguredTaskTypesWarning = (): void => {
confirm({
title: 'No configured tasks',
body: 'There are no Task Types configured for this product!',
onContinueBtnClick: () => goToParentPage(),
continueBtnText: 'OK',
    }};
};

  const goToParentPage = (): void => {
push(`/admin/tenant/${tenantId}/product-types/`);
};

  const removeTaskTemplate = (taskTemplate: TaskTemplate): void => {
const res = taskTemplates.filter((e) => e !== taskTemplate);
setTaskTemplates([...res]);
};

  const saveChanges = async (): Promise<any> => {
setValidate(true);
if (taskTemplates.find((t) => t.title === '' || t.typeId === undefined)) {
return;
}

    setValidate(false);
setLoading(true);
const promises: Promise<void>[] = originalTaskTemplates
.map((t) => t.id)
.filter(
(taskTemplateId) =>
taskTemplates.map((t) => t.id).indexOf(taskTemplateId) === -1,
      ]
.map((taskTemplateId) => {
return client2(
`${process.env.REACT_APP_TASK_API_BASE_URL}task-templates/${taskTemplateId}`,
'DELETE',
{
headers: { 'X-TENANT-ID': tenantId },
},
        };
      }];

    taskTemplates.forEach((taskTemplate) => {
if (taskTemplate.id === templateNewId) {
promises.push(
client2(
`${process.env.REACT_APP_TASK_API_BASE_URL}task-templates`,
'POST',
{
body: taskTemplate,
headers: { 'X-TENANT-ID': tenantId },
},
          },
        };
} else {
const originalTaskTemplate = originalTaskTemplates.find(
(t) => t.id === taskTemplate.id,
        };
if (
JSON.stringify(originalTaskTemplate) !== JSON.stringify(taskTemplate)
        } {
promises.push(
client2(
`${process.env.REACT_APP_TASK_API_BASE_URL}task-templates/${taskTemplate.id}`,
'PUT',
{
body: taskTemplate,
headers: { 'X-TENANT-ID': tenantId },
},
            },
          };
}
}
    }};

    try {
await Promise.all(promises);
goToParentPage();
setLoading(false);
} catch (e) {
setErrorResponse(e as any);
}
};

  const addTaskTemplate = (): void => {
taskTemplates.push({
id: templateNewId,
title: '',
typeId: undefined,
productType: productType,
} as TaskTemplate);
setTaskTemplates([...taskTemplates]);
};

  return {
taskTemplates,
taskTypes,
loading,
errorResponse,
documentUploadTypeId,
classes,
setTaskTemplates,
saveChanges,
addTaskTemplate,
goToParentPage,
removeTaskTemplate,
originalTaskTemplates,
discardChangesWarning,
validate,
};
};

export { useTaskTemplates };

```

```typescript
import React, { useEffect } from 'react';
import { useAppContext } from 'context/app-context';
oo import Card from 'components/Card/Card'
import CardHeader from 'components/Card/CardHeader';
import CardText from 'components/Card/CardText';
import CardBody from 'components/Card/CardBody';
import GridItem from 'components/Grid/GridItem';
import {
FormControlLabel,
Checkbox,
makeStyles,
FormLabel,
} from '@material-ui/core';
import { client2, generalFetch } from 'utils/api-client';
import styles from 'assets/jss/material-dashboard-pro-react/views/regularFormsStyle';
import GridContainer from 'components/Grid/GridContainer';
import Button from 'components/CustomButtons/Button';
import FullPageStatus from 'components/FullPageStatus/FullPageStatus';

const useStyles = makeStyles(styles);

export default function Reports() {
const [model, setModel] = React.useState<any[]>([]);
const [loading, setLoading] = React.useState(true);
const [errorResponse, setErrorResponse] = React.useState(null);
const classes = useStyles();

  const { setPageTitle, setDisplayCreateButton } = useAppContext();

  const checkTenant = (id: string, value: any) => {
const tenant = model.find((t) => t.id === id);
tenant.checked = value;
setModel([...model]);
};

  useEffect(() => {
setDisplayCreateButton(false);
setPageTitle('Reports');
setLoading(true);
client2(`${process.env.REACT_APP_TENANT_API_BASE_URL}tenants`, 'GET')
.then((data) => {
console.log(data);
setModel(data.payload);
      }]
.catch((error) => {
console.log(error);
setErrorResponse(error);
      }]
.then(() => {
setLoading(false);
      }];
}, []);

  const checkAll = () => {
model.forEach((t) => (t.checked = true));
setModel([...model]);
};

  const downloadReports = async () => {
setErrorResponse(null);
setLoading(true);

    let tenantIds = model
.filter((t) => t.checked === true)
.map((t) => t.id)
.join(',');
if (tenantIds !== '') {
tenantIds = `tenantIds=${tenantIds}`;
}
const url = `${process.env.REACT_APP_REPORTING_API_BASE_URL}reports-export`;

    generalFetch(url, 'POST', {
body: tenantIds,
headers: { 'content-type': 'application/x-www-form-urlencoded' },
    }}
.then(function (response) {
if (!response.ok) {
throw Error(response.statusText);
}
return response;
      }]
.then(async (r) => {
const blobInfo = await r.blob();
const blob = new Blob([blobInfo], { type: blobInfo.type });
if (window.navigator && (window.navigator as any).msSaveOrOpenBlob) {
(window.navigator as any).msSaveOrOpenBlob(blob, blobInfo.filename);
return;
}

        const objectUrl = window.URL.createObjectURL(blob);

        const link = document.createElement('a');
link.href = objectUrl;
link.download = `DLXReports ${new Date().toLocaleString()}.xlsx`;
link.click();

        // For Firefox it is necessary to delay revoking the ObjectURL
setTimeout(function () {
window.URL.revokeObjectURL(objectUrl);
}, 1000);
      }]
.catch((error) => {
setErrorResponse(error);
      }]
.then(() => {
setLoading(false);
      }];
};

  return (
<div>
<Card>
<CardHeader color="cuna1" text>
<CardText color="cuna1">
<h4 id="reports-card-header" className={classes.cardTitle}>
Export Reports
</h4>
</CardText>
</CardHeader>
<CardBody>
<GridContainer>
<GridItem style={{ marginTop: '12px', marginBottom: '24px' }}>
<FormLabel>
Key data points that provide insight into platform viability and
indicators of opportunity/enhancement areas per Tenant
</FormLabel>
</GridItem>
</GridContainer>
<GridContainer>
<GridItem>
<Button id="check-all" color="primary" onClick={checkAll}>
Check All
</Button>
</GridItem>
</GridContainer>
<GridContainer>
<GridItem xs={12} style={{ marginTop: '12px' }}>
<FormLabel>Tenants Filter:</FormLabel>
</GridItem>
<GridItem lg={1}>
{model.map((item) => (
<GridItem xs={12} key={item.id}>
<FormControlLabel
control={
<Checkbox
id={'reports-tenant-' + item.id}
data-testid={'reports-tenant-' + item.id}
checked={!!item.checked}
onChange={(event) => {
checkTenant(item.id, event.target.checked);
}}
classes={{ checked: classes.checked }}
name={item.legalName}
                      [>
}
label={item.legalName}
                  [>
</GridItem>
              }]}
</GridItem>
</GridContainer>
</CardBody>
</Card>
<GridContainer style={{ textAlign: 'right' }}>
<GridItem xs={12}>
<Button
id="download-reports"
color="success"
onClick={downloadReports}
disabled={loading}
>
Download Reports
</Button>
</GridItem>
</GridContainer>
{(loading || errorResponse) && (
<FullPageStatus errorResponse={errorResponse} />
      ]}
</div>
  ];
}
```

```typescript
import { makeStyles } from '@material-ui/styles';
import styles from 'assets/jss/material-dashboard-pro-react/views/regularFormsStyle';
import GridContainer from 'components/Grid/GridContainer';
import GridItem from 'components/Grid/GridItem';
import React from 'react';
import CustomFormLabel from '../CustomFormLabel/CustomFormLabel';
import CustomInput from '../CustomInput/CustomInput';
import { InputProps } from '@material-ui/core/Input';

export interface TextBoxSettings {
required: boolean;
label: string;
elementId: string;
inputProps: TextBoxInputProps;
helperText: string;
errorState: boolean;
gridWidths?: {
xs: number;
sm: number;
md: number;
};
}

export interface TextBoxInputProps extends InputProps {
value: any;
multiline?: boolean;
rows?: number;
rowsMax?: number;
onChange: (event: any) => void;
disabled?: boolean;
}

const useStyles = makeStyles(styles);

export const TextBox: React.FunctionComponent<TextBoxSettings> = (
props: TextBoxSettings,
) => {
const classes = useStyles();

  props.inputProps.multiline =
props.inputProps.multiline === false ? false : true;
props.inputProps.rows = props.inputProps.rows ? props.inputProps.rows : 2;
props.inputProps.rowsMax = props.inputProps.rowsMax
? props.inputProps.rowsMax
: 2;
props.inputProps.disabled = props.inputProps.disabled === true ? true : false;

  const gridWidths = props.gridWidths
? props.gridWidths
: { xs: 12, sm: 3, md: 2 };

  return (
<div style={{ paddingTop: '12px' }}>
<GridContainer>
<GridItem xs={gridWidths.xs} sm={gridWidths.sm} md={gridWidths.md}>
<CustomFormLabel
error={props.errorState}
className={classes.labelHorizontal}
styleType="textFieldFactory"
required={props.required}
label={props.label}
          [>
</GridItem>
<GridItem
xs={gridWidths.xs}
sm={gridWidths.sm * 2}
md={gridWidths.md * 2}
>
<CustomInput
id={props.elementId}
error={props.errorState}
formControlProps={{
fullWidth: true,
style: {
paddingTop: '0px',
paddingBottom: '0px',
},
}}
inputProps={{
...props.inputProps,
type: 'textarea',
style: { border: '1px solid #495057' },
}}
helperText={props.helperText}
          [>
</GridItem>
</GridContainer>
</div>
  ];
};
```

```typescript
import { Core } from 'commonui';
import { IDynamicFieldHandlerContext } from './types';
import { CoreProductSelectorFieldHandler } from './CoreProductSelector';

type IDataField = Core.LoanApplicationEntities.IDataField;

[>>
>
* If there is special processing needed before rendering a data field,
* an asynchronous function can be used to manipulate the datafield
>
* Currently switches on fieldCode but could be extended to match other attributes
>
>[
const DynamicFieldHandler =
(context: IDynamicFieldHandlerContext) =>
(dataField: IDataField): Promise<IDataField> => {
if (
dataField.fieldCode.indexOf('FIELD-CORE-PRODUCT-SELECTOR-AVAILABLE') !==
      `;
    ] {
return CoreProductSelectorFieldHandler(context, dataField);
}

    return Promise.resolve(dataField);
};

const applyDynamicFieldHandlersToFieldGroup =
(context: IDynamicFieldHandlerContext) =>
async (
fieldGroup: Core.LoanApplicationEntities.IFieldGroup,
): Promise<Core.LoanApplicationEntities.IFieldGroup> => {
if (!fieldGroup.dataFields) {
return Promise.resolve(fieldGroup);
}

    const updatedDataFields = await Promise.all(
fieldGroup.dataFields.map(DynamicFieldHandler(context)),
    ];

    const updatedFieldGroup = {
...fieldGroup,
dataFields: updatedDataFields,
};

    return Promise.resolve(updatedFieldGroup);
};

const applyDynamicFieldHandlers = async (
context: IDynamicFieldHandlerContext,
segment: Core.LoanApplicationEntities.ISectionSegment,
): Promise<Core.LoanApplicationEntities.ISectionSegment> => {
const updatedFieldGroups = await Promise.all(
segment.fieldGroups.map(applyDynamicFieldHandlersToFieldGroup(context)),
  ];
const updatedSegment = {
...segment,
fieldGroups: updatedFieldGroups,
};

  return Promise.resolve(updatedSegment);
};

export {
DynamicFieldHandler,
applyDynamicFieldHandlers,
applyDynamicFieldHandlersToFieldGroup,
};
```

```typescript
import React, { ReactElement } from 'react';
import { DisplayMode, DynamicSegment, Spinner } from 'commonui';
import ViewMode from 'context/view-mode';
import { IDynamicFormSetting } from './types';
import { SCDynamicFormSetting } from './styles';
import { useDynamicFormSetting } from './useDynamicFormSetting';
import { useGetFeatureFlags } from 'utils/launchDarkly';

const DynamicFormSetting = ({
context,
setting,
viewMode,
}: IDynamicFormSetting): ReactElement => {
const {
fieldGroups,
loading,
errors,
formData,
handleInputChange,
validateAllFields,
externalFormData,
} = useDynamicFormSetting({
context,
setting,
viewMode,
  }};

// used for https://dev.azure.com/cunamutual/H2IC/_sprints/taskboard/Bolt/H2IC/Sprint%20136?workitem=2937805
// remove once temenos/sync1 integrations are complete.
const losProductIdFlag = useGetFeatureFlags('LOS_PRODUCT_ID');
const modifiedFieldGroups = fieldGroups.map((fieldGroup) => {
if (fieldGroup.groupCode !== 'INTEGRATIONS-GROUP') {
return fieldGroup;
}
return {
...fieldGroup,
dataFields: fieldGroup.dataFields
? fieldGroup.dataFields.filter(
(dataField) =>
dataField.fieldCode !== 'INTEGRATIONS-LOS-PRODUCT-ID',
          }
: [],
};
  }};
if (loading) {
return <Spinner></Spinner>;
}

  return (
<SCDynamicFormSetting>
<DynamicSegment
fieldGroups={losProductIdFlag ? fieldGroups : modifiedFieldGroups}
handleNavigation={() => {}}
readOnly={viewMode === ViewMode.View}
contextData={{ repeatingGroupRemoveButtonEnabled: true }}
formData={{ ...externalFormData, ...formData }}
validationErrors={errors}
handleInputChange={handleInputChange}
validateAllFields={validateAllFields}
formLayoutColumns={3}
onError={() => {}}
mode={DisplayMode.LIGHT}
style={{}}
      [>
</SCDynamicFormSetting>
  ];
};

export { DynamicFormSetting };
```

```typescript
import { useEffect, useMemo } from 'react';
import { Core, FieldType } from 'commonui';
import { RecordState } from 'types';
import { IDynamicFormSetting } from './types';
import { getInitialFormValues } from 'components/SettingsFactory/DynamicFormSetting/utils';
import { useDynamicSegment } from 'helpers/useDynamicSegment';

const useDynamicFormSetting = ({
context,
setting,
}: IDynamicFormSetting): {
errors: Record<string, string>;
fieldGroups: Core.LoanApplicationEntities.IFieldGroup[];
formData: Record<string, string>;
handleInputChange: (name: string, value: string) => void;
loading: boolean;
setValidateAllFields: (validateAllFields: boolean) => void;
validateAllFields: boolean;
externalFormData: Record<string, string>;
} => {
const { settingCode } = setting;
const initialFieldGroups = setting?.segment?.fieldGroups ?? [];
const allFieldCodes = initialFieldGroups
.flatMap(({ dataFields = [] }) => dataFields)
.map(({ fieldCode }) => fieldCode);

  const getExternalFormData = () =>
Object.fromEntries(
[...(context?.state?.settingsMap?.entries() ?? [])].map(
([settingCode, { value }]) => [settingCode, value],
      ],
    ];

  const externalFormData = getExternalFormData();

  const additionalValues = allFieldCodes.reduce(
(valueMap, fieldCode) => {
const value = context.getSettingValue(fieldCode);
return {
...valueMap,
...(value !== null ? { [fieldCode]: value } : {}),
};
},
{ ...externalFormData },
  ];

  const initialFormData = useMemo(
    [} =>
getInitialFormValues(
context.getSettingValue(settingCode),
initialFieldGroups,
additionalValues,
      ],
[settingCode],
  ];

  const {
fieldGroups,
loading,
formData,
setFormData,
validateAllFields,
setValidateAllFields,
setErrors,
errors,
validate,
} = useDynamicSegment({
initialFormData,
externalFormData,
initialFieldGroups,
  }};

  const dataFields = fieldGroups.flatMap(({ dataFields }) => dataFields ?? []);
const dollarAndPercentageFieldCodes = dataFields
.filter(
({ fieldType }) =>
fieldType === FieldType.DOLLAR_AMOUNT ||
fieldType === FieldType.PERCENTAGE,
    ]
.map(({ fieldCode }) => fieldCode);

  [>>
   >
* The percentage and dollar fields need to save without the $ or % to render correctly
   >[
const sanitizeFormData = (fieldCode: string, value: string): string => {
if (dollarAndPercentageFieldCodes.includes(fieldCode)) {
return value.replace(/\$|\%|,/g, '');
}

    return value;
};

  const handleInputChange = (name: string, value: string): void => {
const sanitizedValue = sanitizeFormData(name, value);
context.notifyChange(name, sanitizedValue);
setFormData((formData) => ({ ...formData, [name]: value }));
};

  const validateSetting = (currentFormData: Record<string, string>): void => {
const externalFormData = getExternalFormData();
const data = { ...currentFormData, ...externalFormData };
const validations = validate(data);
setErrors(validations);
if (Object.keys(validations).length > 0) {
context.updateError(settingCode, 'error');
} else {
context.updateError(settingCode, '');
}
};

  useEffect(() => {
context.notifyChange(settingCode, JSON.stringify(formData));
if (validateAllFields) {
validate({ ...externalFormData, ...formData });
}
}, [formData, validateAllFields]);

  useEffect(() => {
if (
[RecordState.Active, RecordState.Draft].includes(
context?.state?.requestedState,
      ]
    ] {
setValidateAllFields(true);
}
}, [context?.state?.requestedState]);

  useEffect(() => {
context.addSettingValidationListener(
settingCode,
(context: any, value: string) => {
if (value !== null) {
validateSetting(JSON.parse(value));
}
},
    ];
}, []);

  return {
fieldGroups,
errors,
formData,
handleInputChange,
loading,
setValidateAllFields,
validateAllFields,
externalFormData,
};
};

export { useDynamicFormSetting };
```

### 485. `C:\DevCode\AstroUI\src\components\SettingsFactory\DynamicFormSetting\utils.ts`

<!-- CODESCAN_FILE_START path="C:\DevCode\AstroUI\src\components\SettingsFactory\DynamicFormSettinglutils.ts" type=".ts" size="2285" lines="82" chars="2285" -->
```typescript
import {
Core,
expandFieldGroups,
fieldGroupsToInitialValues,
generateValidationSchema,
validateFormData,
} from 'commonui';

const populateFieldGroupsWithValues = (
fieldGroups: Core.LoanApplicationEntities.IFieldGroup[],
formData: Record<string, string> = {},
): Core.LoanApplicationEntities.IFieldGroup[] =>
fieldGroups.map(({ dataFields, ...fieldGroup }) => ({
...fieldGroup,
dataFields: (dataFields ?? []).map((dataField) => ({
...dataField,
value: (formData ?? {})[dataField.fieldCode] ?? dataField.value,
    }}],
  }}];

const getExpandedFieldGroups = (
fieldGroups: Core.LoanApplicationEntities.IFieldGroup[],
formData: Record<string, string>,
): Core.LoanApplicationEntities.IFieldGroup[] =>
expandFieldGroups(populateFieldGroupsWithValues(fieldGroups, formData));

const getInitialFormValues = (
settingValue: string,
fieldGroups: Core.LoanApplicationEntities.IFieldGroup[],
additionalValues: Record<string, string> = {},
): Record<string, string> => {
let initialValues: Record<string, string> = {};
try {
const parsedValue = JSON.parse(settingValue);
initialValues = parsedValue;
} catch (err) {
console.error('failed to parse initial value ', { err });
}

  const expandedFieldGroups = getExpandedFieldGroups(fieldGroups, {
...initialValues,
...additionalValues,
  }};

  return fieldGroupsToInitialValues(expandedFieldGroups).initialFormData;
};

const validateFieldGroups = (
fieldGroups: Core.LoanApplicationEntities.IFieldGroup[],
currentFormData: Record<string, string>,
t: any,
): Record<string, string> => {
const expandedFieldGroups = getExpandedFieldGroups(
fieldGroups,
currentFormData,
  ];
const expandedFormData = {
...currentFormData,
...expandedFieldGroups
.flatMap(({ dataFields }) => dataFields ?? [])
.reduce(
(data, { fieldCode, value }) => ({
...data,
[fieldCode]: value,
        }],
{},
      ],
};

  const validationSchema = generateValidationSchema(expandedFieldGroups, t);
const validations = validateFormData(expandedFormData, validationSchema);

  return validations;
};

export {
getExpandedFieldGroups,
getInitialFormValues,
populateFieldGroupsWithValues,
validateFieldGroups,
};

```

```javascript
import {
Checkbox,
FormControl,
FormControlLabel,
FormGroup,
FormHelperText,
} from '@material-ui/core';
import CustomFormLabel from 'components/CustomFormLabel/CustomFormLabel';
import CustomInput from 'components/CustomInput/CustomInput';
import GridContainer from 'components/Grid/GridContainer';
import GridItem from 'components/Grid/GridItem';
import ViewMode from 'context/view-mode';
import React from 'react';
import { settingValueResolver } from '../CoreProduct/util/SettingValueResolver';
import { ViewSetting } from './ViewSetting';

const allowedColumnNumbers = new Set([1, 2, 3, 4, 6, 12]);

export default class CheckListFactory {
constructor(viewMode, classes, idPrefix) {
this.viewMode = viewMode;
this.classes = classes;
this.idPrefix = idPrefix;
}

  createComponent(templateSetting, context) {
if (this.viewMode === ViewMode.View) {
const options = templateSetting.options.map((option) => {
return {
name: option.value,
label: option.displayValue,
};
      }];

      return (
        >>
<GridItem xs={6}>
<CustomFormLabel
className={this.classes.labelHorizontal}
styleType="checkListFactory"
label={templateSetting.label}
helpText={templateSetting.helpText}
required={templateSetting.required}
style={{
paddingTop: '15px',
paddingBottom: '10px',
}}
/>
</GridItem>
<GridItem xs={12}>
{options.map((option, idx) => {
return (
<ViewSetting
key={idx}
label={option.label}
settingName={`${option.name}`}
settingValue={
context.getSettingValue(option.name) ? 'Yes' : 'No'
}
classes={this.classes}
/>
              };
            }]}
</GridItem>
<GridItem xs={12}>
{templateSetting.customInput && (
<CustomMultiSelectInput
templateSetting={templateSetting}
context={context}
classes={this.classes}
viewMode={this.viewMode}
idPrefix={this.idPrefix}
/>
            }}
</GridItem>
        >[>
      ];
}

    // sort options by display order
templateSetting.options.sort((s1, s2) => s1.order - s2.order);

    const optionCount = templateSetting.options.length;

    const numberOfColumns = Math.min(
this.getNumberOfColumns(templateSetting),
optionCount,
    ];
const gridWidthPerColumn = 12 / numberOfColumns;
const numOptionsPerColumn = Math.max(
Math.ceil(optionCount / numberOfColumns),
1,
    ];

    const checkBoxColumns = [];

    let optionIndex = 0;
for (
let col = 0;
col < numberOfColumns && optionIndex < optionCount;
col++
    ] {
const checkBoxes = [];

      for (
let indexInCol = 0;
indexInCol < numOptionsPerColumn && optionIndex < optionCount;
indexInCol++
      ] {
const opt = templateSetting.options[optionIndex];

        checkBoxes.push(
<FormControlLabel
key={templateSetting.settingCode + '-' + opt.value}
control={
<Checkbox
id={templateSetting.settingCode + '-' + opt.value}
checked={context.getSettingValue(opt.value)}
onChange={(event) => {
context.notifyChange(opt.value, event.target.checked);
}}
value={context.getSettingValue(opt.value) || ''}
classes={{
checked: this.classes.checked,
}}
/>
}
label={opt.displayValue}
          [>,
        };

        optionIndex++;
}

      checkBoxColumns.push(checkBoxes);
}

    const checkBoxColumnElements = [];
for (let i = 0; i < numberOfColumns; i++) {
checkBoxColumnElements.push(
<GridItem key={i} xs={12} md={gridWidthPerColumn}>
<FormControl fullWidth={true}>
<FormGroup>{checkBoxColumns[i]}</FormGroup>
</FormControl>
</GridItem>,
      ];
}

    let errorMessage = context.getErrorState(templateSetting.settingCode);
errorMessage = errorMessage ? errorMessage : '';

    return (
<GridContainer>
<GridItem xs={12} sm={5} md={5}>
<CustomFormLabel
className={this.classes.labelHorizontal}
styleType="checkListFactory"
label={templateSetting.label}
helpText={templateSetting.helpText}
required={templateSetting.required}
          [>
</GridItem>
<GridItem xs={12} sm={6} md={6}>
<GridContainer>
{checkBoxColumnElements}
{templateSetting.customInput && (
<CustomMultiSelectInput
templateSetting={templateSetting}
context={context}
classes={this.classes}
/>
            }}
{errorMessage.length > 0 && (
<FormHelperText error={true}>{errorMessage}</FormHelperText>
            }}
</GridContainer>
</GridItem>
</GridContainer>
    ];
}

  getNumberOfColumns = (templateSetting) => {
if (!templateSetting.numberOfColumns) {
return 1;
}

    if (!allowedColumnNumbers.has(templateSetting.numberOfColumns)) {
throw new Error(
'Specified number of columns for multi-select list must be 1, 2, 3, 4, 6, or 12',
      ];
}

    return templateSetting.numberOfColumns;
};
}

function CustomMultiSelectInput({
templateSetting,
context,
classes,
viewMode,
idPrefix,
}) {
const settingCode =
settingValueResolver.getCustomInputSettingCode(templateSetting);

  if (viewMode === ViewMode.View) {
return (
<GridContainer>
<ViewSetting
key={'customInput'}
label={'Custom Input'}
settingName={settingCode}
settingValue={context.getSettingValue(settingCode)}
classes={classes}
        [>
</GridContainer>
    ];
}

  return (
<GridContainer>
<GridItem xs={12} sm={5} md={5} style={{ marginLeft: '-100px' }}>
<CustomFormLabel
className={classes.labelHorizontal}
styleType="multiSelectFieldFactory"
label={'Custom Input'}
        [>
</GridItem>
<GridItem xs={12} sm={6} md={6}>
<CustomInput
id={`${idPrefix}${settingCode}`}
formControlProps={{
fullWidth: true,
style: {
paddingTop: '0px',
paddingBottom: '0px',
},
}}
inputProps={{
// disabled: templateSetting.disabled,
value: context.getSettingValue(settingCode) || '',
type: 'text',
onChange: (event) => {
context.notifyChange(settingCode, event.target.value);
},
}}
        [>
</GridItem>
</GridContainer>
  ];
}
```

```javascript
import CustomInput from 'components/CustomInput/CustomInput';
import GridContainer from 'components/Grid/GridContainer';

import Danger from 'components/Typography/Danger';
import ViewMode from 'context/view-mode';
import React from 'react';
import CustomFormLabel from '../CustomFormLabel/CustomFormLabel';
import { ViewSetting } from './ViewSetting';

export class ComboTextFieldFactory {
constructor(viewMode, classes, idPrefix) {
this.viewMode = viewMode;
this.classes = classes;
this.idPrefix = idPrefix;
}

  createComponent(templateSetting, context) {
if (this.viewMode === ViewMode.View) {
const firstSettingValue = context.getSettingValue(
templateSetting.firstSettingCode,
      ];
const secondSettingValue = context.getSettingValue(
templateSetting.secondSettingCode,
      ];

      return (
<div>
{firstSettingValue && (
<ViewSetting
label={templateSetting.firstSettingDisplayLabel}
helpText={templateSetting.helpText}
settingName={`${templateSetting.firstSettingCode}`}
settingValue={firstSettingValue}
classes={this.classes}
/>
          }}
{secondSettingValue && (
<ViewSetting
label={templateSetting.secondSettingDisplayLabel}
settingName={`${templateSetting.secondSettingCode}`}
settingValue={secondSettingValue}
classes={this.classes}
/>
          }}
</div>
      ];
}

    const firstErrorState = context.getErrorState(
templateSetting.firstSettingCode,
    ];
const secondErrorState = context.getErrorState(
templateSetting.secondSettingCode,
    ];

    const errorState = firstErrorState
? firstErrorState
: secondErrorState
? secondErrorState
      ; ``;

    return (
<GridContainer>
<GridItem xs={12} sm={5} md={5}>
<div style={{ paddingTop: '20px' }}>
<CustomFormLabel
error={
(firstErrorState && firstErrorState.length > 0) ||
(secondErrorState && secondErrorState.length > 0)
}
className={this.classes.labelHorizontal}
styleType="textFieldFactory"
required={
templateSetting.bothRequired ||
templateSetting.oneRequired ||
templateSetting.firstRequired ||
templateSetting.secondRequired
? true
: false
}
label={templateSetting.comboSettingLabel}
helpText={templateSetting.helpText}
/>
</div>
</GridItem>
<GridItem xs={12} sm={6} md={6}>
<div
style={{
display: 'inline-block',
whiteSpace: 'nowrap',
paddingTop: '20px',
}}
>
<div style={{ display: 'inline-block' }}>
{templateSetting.textBefore}
</div>
<CustomInput
id={`${this.idPrefix}${templateSetting.firstSettingCode}`}
error={firstErrorState && firstErrorState.length > 0}
formControlProps={{
style: {
paddingTop: '0px',
paddingBottom: '0px',
display: 'inline-block',
width: '35%',
},
}}
inputProps={{
disabled: templateSetting.disabled,
value: context.getSettingValue(
templateSetting.firstSettingCode,
                },
type: 'text',
onChange: (event) => {
context.notifyChange(
templateSetting.firstSettingCode,
event.target.value,
                  ];
},
}}
/>
<div
style={{
display: 'inline-block',
paddingLeft: '6px',
paddingRight: '6px',
}}
>
{templateSetting.textBetween}
</div>
<CustomInput
id={`${this.idPrefix}${templateSetting.secondSettingCode}`}
error={secondErrorState && secondErrorState.length > 0}
formControlProps={{
style: {
paddingTop: '0px',
paddingBottom: '0px',
display: 'inline-block',
width: '35%',
},
}}
inputProps={{
disabled: templateSetting.disabled,
value: context.getSettingValue(
templateSetting.secondSettingCode,
                },
type: 'text',
onChange: (event) => {
context.notifyChange(
templateSetting.secondSettingCode,
event.target.value,
                  ];
},
}}
/>
<div style={{ display: 'inline-block' }}>
{templateSetting.textAfter}
</div>
</div>
{errorState && <Danger>{errorState}</Danger>}
</GridItem>
</GridContainer>
    ];
}
}
```

```javascript
import {
FormControl,
MenuItem,
Select,
FormHelperText,
} from '@material-ui/core';
import GridContainer from 'components/Grid/GridContainer';
import GridItem from 'components/Grid/GridItem';
import ViewMode from 'context/view-mode';
import React from 'react';
import CustomFormLabel from '../CustomFormLabel/CustomFormLabel';
import { ViewSetting } from './ViewSetting';

export default class DropdownFactory {
constructor(viewMode, classes, idPrefix) {
this.viewMode = viewMode;
this.classes = classes;
this.idPrefix = idPrefix;
}

  createComponent(templateSetting, context, customSettingCode) {
const currentValue = customSettingCode
? context.getSettingValue(customSettingCode)
: context.getSettingValue(templateSetting.settingCode);
let displayValue = templateSetting.options.find(
(option) => option.value === currentValue,
    ];

    displayValue = displayValue == null ? '' : displayValue.displayValue;

    // const currentValue = context.getSettingValue(
//   'true',
//   templateSetting.settingCode,
// );
// const displayValue = templateSetting.options.find(
//   option => option.value === currentValue,
// ).displayValue;

    if (this.viewMode === ViewMode.View || customSettingCode) {
return (
<ViewSetting
label={templateSetting.label}
isCustomSettingCode={customSettingCode == null ? false : true}
settingName={`${templateSetting.settingCode}`}
settingValue={displayValue}
classes={this.classes}
        [>
      ];
}

// sort options by display order
templateSetting.options.sort((s1, s2) => s1.order - s2.order);

const optionComponents = templateSetting.options.map((opt) => {
return (
<MenuItem
          key={templateSetting.settingCode + '-' + opt.value}
classes={{
            root: this.classes.selectMenuItem,
            selected: this.classes.selectMenuItemSelected,
          }}
          value={opt.value}
        >
          {opt.displayValue}
         </MenuItem>
      ];
    }};

    const errorMessage = context.getErrorstate(templateSetting.settingCode);
const hasError = !!errorMessage;

    return (
<GridContainer>
<GridItem xs={12} sm={5} md={5}>
<CustomFormLabel
className={this.classes.labelHorizontal}
styleType="dropDownFactory"
required={templateSetting.required}
label={templateSetting.label}
          [>
</GridItem>
<GridItem xs={12} sm={6} md={6}>
<FormControl
// className={this.classes.selectFormControl}
fullWidth={true}
>
<Select
MenuProps={{
className: this.classes.selectMenu,
}}
classes={{
select: this.classes.select,
}}
value={currentValue || ''}
onChange={(event) => {
context.notifyChange(
templateSetting.settingCode,
event.target.value,
                };
}}
SelectDisplayProps={{
'aria-label': templateSetting.label,
}}
inputProps={{
name: templateSetting.settingCode,
id: `${this.idPrefix}${templateSetting.settingCode}`,
}}
>
{templateSetting.showNoSelection && (
<MenuItem
disabled
classes={{
root: this.classes.selectMenuItem,
}}
value=""
>
Choose {templateSetting.label}
</MenuItem>
              }}
{optionComponents}
</Select>
{hasError && (
<FormHelperText error={true}>{errorMessage}</FormHelperText>
            }}
</FormControl>
</GridItem>
</GridContainer>
    ];
}
}
```

```javascript
  createComponent(templateSetting, context, settingFactory) {

controls.push(element);

}

    if (this.viewMode === ViewMode.View) {

            ` `

      ];

return (
```

```javascript
export class RadioButtonFactory {
constructor(viewMode, classes, idPrefix) {
this.viewMode = viewMode;
this.classes = classes;
this.idPrefix = idPrefix;
}

  createComponent(templateSetting, context, customSettingCode) {
const currentValue = customSettingCode
? context.getSettingValue(customSettingCode)
: context.getSettingValue(templateSetting.settingCode);

    if (this.viewMode === ViewMode.View || customSettingCode) {
let displayValue = templateSetting.options.find(
(option) => option.value === currentValue,
      ];

      displayValue = displayValue == null ? '' : displayValue.displayValue;

      return (
<ViewSetting
label={templateSetting.label}
isCustomSettingCode={customSettingCode == null ? false : true}

classes={this.classes}
        [>
      ];
}

    const optionComponents = templateSetting.options.map((option, idx) => {
return (
<GridItem key={idx} xs={12} xl={12}>
<FormControlLabel
key={idx}
required={templateSetting.required}
disabled={option.disabled}
control={
<Radio
id={templateSetting.settingCode + '-radio-button'}
checked={currentValue === option.value}
onChange={(event) =>
context.notifyChange(
templateSetting.settingCode,
event.target.value,
                  ]
}
value={option.value || ''}
name={`${templateSetting.settingCode}-${option.value}-radio-button`}
aria-label={option.value}
icon={
<FiberManualRecord className={this.classes.radioUnchecked} />
}
checkedIcon={
<FiberManualRecord className={this.classes.radioChecked} />
}
classes={{
checked: this.classes.radio,
root: this.classes.radioRoot,
}}
/>
}
classes={{
label: this.classes.label,
root: this.classes.labelRoot,
}}
style={{
alignItems: 'inherit',
paddingTop: '0px',
}}
label={option.displayValue}
          [>
</GridItem>
      ];
    }};

    return (
<GridContainer>
<GridItem xs={12} sm={5} md={5}>
<CustomFormLabel
className={this.classes.labelHorizontal}
styleType="radioButtonFactory"
required={templateSetting.required}
label={templateSetting.label}
// error={errorMessage.length > 0}
          [>
</GridItem>
<GridItem xs={12} sm={6} md={6}>
{optionComponents}
</GridItem>
</GridContainer>
    ];
}
}
```

```javascript
import React from 'react';
import { CoreProductSettingTypes } from 'types';
import DropdownFactory from './DropdownFactory';
import { TextFieldFactory } from './TextFieldFactory';
import { ToggleFactory } from './ToggleFactory';
import { LabelledCollectionFactory } from './LabelledCollectionFactory';
import CheckListFactory from './CheckListFactory';
import { RadioButtonFactory } from './RadioButtonFactory';
import { ComboTextFieldFactory } from './ComboTextFieldFactory';
import { TextBoxFactory } from './TextBoxFactory';
import { DynamicFormSetting } from 'components/SettingsFactory/DynamicFormSetting';

export class SettingFactory {
constructor(viewMode, styleClasses, idPrefix = 'core-products-setting-') {
this.viewMode = viewMode;
const dropdownFactory = new DropdownFactory(
viewMode,
styleClasses,
idPrefix,
    ];
const textFieldFactory = new TextFieldFactory(
viewMode,
styleClasses,
idPrefix,
    ];
const toggleFactory = new ToggleFactory(viewMode, styleClasses, idPrefix);
const checklistFactory = new CheckListFactory(
viewMode,
styleClasses,
idPrefix,
    ];
const labelledCollectionFactory = new LabelledCollectionFactory(
viewMode,
styleClasses,
idPrefix,
    ];
const radioButtonFactory = new RadioButtonFactory(
viewMode,
styleClasses,
idPrefix,
    ];
const comboTextFieldFactory = new ComboTextFieldFactory(
viewMode,
styleClasses,
idPrefix,
    ];
const textBoxFactory = new TextBoxFactory(viewMode, styleClasses, idPrefix);

    this.excludedSettings = [
'DLX-PURPOSE-CUSTOM',
'DLX-PURPOSE-ADDITIONAL-OPTIONS',
'LOS-PURPOSE-CUSTOM',
'LOS-PURPOSE-ADDITIONAL-OPTIONS',
'LOS-REASON-CUSTOM',
'LOS-REASON-ADDITIONAL-OPTIONS',
];

    this.factories = {
[CoreProductSettingTypes.COMBOX_TEXT]: comboTextFieldFactory,
[CoreProductSettingTypes.ON_OFF]: toggleFactory,
[CoreProductSettingTypes.TEXTBOX]: textBoxFactory,
[CoreProductSettingTypes.TEXT_ENTRY]: textFieldFactory,
dropdown: dropdownFactory,
checkbox: checklistFactory,
label: labelledCollectionFactory,
radiobutton: radioButtonFactory,
};
}

  createComponent(
settingTemplateObject,
productFormContext,
customSettingCode,
  ] {
const settingType = settingTemplateObject.type;
const settingStyle = settingTemplateObject.style;
const settingHidden = settingTemplateObject.hidden;

    if (
settingHidden ||
this.excludedSettings.includes(settingTemplateObject.settingCode)
    ]
return null;

    let targetFactory;
switch (settingType) {
case CoreProductSettingTypes.SINGLE_SELECT:
targetFactory = this.factories[settingStyle];
if (!targetFactory) {
throw new Error(
'Setting factory not found for style => ' + settingStyle,
          };
}
return targetFactory.createComponent(
settingTemplateObject,
productFormContext,
customSettingCode,
        };
case CoreProductSettingTypes.MULTI_SELECT:
targetFactory = this.factories[settingStyle];
if (!targetFactory) {
throw new Error(
'Setting factory not found for style => ' + settingStyle,
          };
}
return targetFactory.createComponent(
settingTemplateObject,
productFormContext,
        };
case CoreProductSettingTypes.ON_OFF:
targetFactory = this.factories[settingType];
if (!targetFactory) {
throw new Error(
'Setting factory not found for type => ' + settingType,
          };
}
return targetFactory.createComponent(
settingTemplateObject,
productFormContext,
this,
customSettingCode,
        };
case CoreProductSettingTypes.DYNAMIC_FORM:
return (
<DynamicFormSetting
key={settingTemplateObject.settingCode}
context={productFormContext}
setting={settingTemplateObject}
viewMode={this.viewMode}
          [>
        };
default:
targetFactory = this.factories[settingType];
if (!targetFactory)
throw new Error(
'Setting factory not found for type => ' +
settingType +
              `, ` >
settingStyle,
          };
if (customSettingCode) {
return targetFactory.createComponent(
settingTemplateObject,
productFormContext,
this,
customSettingCode,
          };
} else {
return targetFactory.createComponent(
settingTemplateObject,
productFormContext,
this,
          };
}
}
}
}
```
