import { makeStyles } from '@material-ui/core/styles';
import styles from 'assets/jss/material-dashboard-pro-react/views/regularFormsStyle';
import Card from 'components/Card/Card';
import CardBody from 'components/Card/CardBody';
import CardHeader from 'components/Card/CardHeader';
import CardText from 'components/Card/CardText';
import FullPageStatus from 'components/FullPageStatus/FullPageStatus';
import GridContainer from 'components/Grid/GridContainer';
import GridItem from 'components/Grid/GridItem';
import { useAppContext } from 'context/app-context';
import { getFormattedAddresses } from 'helpers/addresshelper';
import { DateTime } from 'luxon';
import React from 'react';
import { client2 } from 'utils/api-client';
import { isEmptyString } from '../../helpers/validations';
import CustomFormLabel from '../CustomFormLabel/CustomFormLabel';
import { useParams } from 'react-router-dom';

const useStyles = makeStyles(styles);

const fixDateString = async (dateString, setter) => {
const formattedDate = (await isEmptyString.isValid(dateString))
    ?--
: DateTime.fromISO(dateString).toFormat('MM/dd/yyyy');

  setter(formattedDate);
};

const fixMerchantType = (type) => {
switch (type) {
case 1:
return 'Credit Union';
case 2:
return 'Auto Dealer';
default:
return type;
}
};

export default function ViewMerchant() {
const params = useParams();
const merchantId = params.merchantId;
const { tenant, setDisplayCreateButton, setPageTitle } = useAppContext();
const [data, setData] = React.useState();
const [loading, setLoading] = React.useState(true);
const [errorResponse, setErrorResponse] = React.useState(null);
const classes = useStyles();

  const [formattedStartDate, setFormattedStartDate] = React.useState(null);
const [formattedEndDate, setFormattedEndDate] = React.useState(null);
const titleList = new Map();

  React.useEffect(() => {
setLoading(true);
//Remove this and make actual service call...
//const [merchant] = new FakeMerchants().findMerchant(merchantId)
//setData(merchant)
//setLoading(false);
client2(
`${process.env.REACT_APP_MERCHANT_API_BASE_URL}/merchants/${merchantId}`,
    ]
.then((data) => {
console.log(data);
const merchantName = data.payload.legalName;
if (tenant)
titleList.set(
`${tenant?.legalName} > `,
`/admin/tenant/${tenant?.id}/view`,
          };
titleList.set(`Merchants > `, `/admin/tenant/${tenant?.id}/merchant`);
titleList.set(
merchantName,
`/admin/tenant/${tenant?.id}/merchant/${merchantId}/view`,
        };
setPageTitle(titleList);
setData(data.payload);
setLoading(false);

        if (data.payload.startDate) {
fixDateString(data.payload.startDate, setFormattedStartDate);
}

        if (data.payload.endDate) {
fixDateString(data.payload.endDate, setFormattedEndDate);
}
      }]
.catch((error) => {
setLoading(false);
setErrorResponse(error);
      }];
}, []);

  setDisplayCreateButton(false);

  if (loading || errorResponse || !data) {
return <FullPageStatus errorResponse={errorResponse} />;
}

  return (
<GridContainer>

### 549. `C:\DevCode\AstroUI\src\components\ViewTenant\ViewTenant.tsx`

<!-- CODESCAN_FILE_START path="C:\DevCode\AstroUI\src\componentsIViewTenantIViewTenant.tsx" type=".tsx" size="4183" lines="134" chars="4183" -->
```typescript
import FullPageStatus from 'components/FullPageStatus/FullPageStatus';
import GridItem from 'components/Grid/GridItem';
import GridContainer from 'components/Grid/GridContainer';
import { client2 } from 'utils/api-client';
import SnackbarContent from 'components/Snackbar/SnackbarContent';
import { useAppContext } from 'context/app-context';
import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import ViewApiClient from './ViewTenantTypes/ViewApiClient';
import ViewCreditUnionTenant from './ViewTenantTypes/ViewCreditUnionTenant';
import { Tenant } from 'components/ManageTenant/base-tenant';
import { TenantTypeCodes } from '../../types';
import { getAuthorizationAPIRequest } from 'API';
import { useParams } from 'react-router-dom';

export default function ViewTenant(): JSX.Element {
const tenantId = useParams().tenantId;

  const {
tenant: originalTenant,
setPageTitle,
setDisplayCreateButton,
loadTenant,
hasExternalApiAuthorizationLoaded,
setExternalApiAuthorizationLoaded,
isAuthorizedToSubmitToLOS,
setIsAuthorizedToSubmitToLOS,
} = useAppContext();

  const tenant = originalTenant as Tenant;
const [isFicusApiClient, setIsFicusApiClient] = useState(false);
const [merchantName, setMerchantName] = useState('');
const [checkForUpdate, setCheckForUpdate] = useState<boolean | null>(false);
const [errorResponse, setErrorResponse] = useState(null);
const loadExitApiAuth = async () => {
const result = await getAuthorizationAPIRequest(tenant.contractNumber);
tenant.isAuthorizeToSubmitToLOS = result;
setIsAuthorizedToSubmitToLOS(result);
setExternalApiAuthorizationLoaded(true);
};

  useEffect(() => {
const titleList = new Map();
titleList.set(tenant.legalName, `/admin/tenant/${tenantId}/view`);
setPageTitle(titleList);
setDisplayCreateButton(false); // remove navbar create button
}, []);

  // Call the service and get the tenant.
useEffect(() => {
let timeoutId: number;
if (tenant.creationPending) {
timeoutId = window.setTimeout(() => setCheckForUpdate(true), 10000);
}

    return () => {
if (timeoutId) {
clearTimeout(timeoutId);
}
};
}, [tenant.creationPending]);

  // effect hook to see if tenant pending status has changed
useEffect(() => {
if (!checkForUpdate) return;
if (!tenantId) return;
let timeoutId: number;

    loadTenant(tenantId)
.then((tenant) => {
if (tenant && tenant.creationPending) {
setCheckForUpdate(null);
timeoutId = window.setTimeout(() => setCheckForUpdate(true), 10000);
}
      }]
.catch((error) => {
setCheckForUpdate(null);
setErrorResponse(error);
timeoutId = window.setTimeout(() => setCheckForUpdate(true), 10000);
      }];

    return () => {
if (timeoutId) {
clearTimeout(timeoutId);
}
};
}, [checkForUpdate]);

  useEffect(() => {
if (tenant.tenantTypeCode === TenantTypeCodes.APIClient) {
setIsFicusApiClient(true);
}
if (tenant.defaultMerchantId) {
client2(
`${process.env.REACT_APP_MERCHANT_API_BASE_URL}/merchants/${tenant.defaultMerchantId}`,
).then((data) => {
setMerchantName(data.payload.legalName || 'None');
      }];
    else
setMerchantName('None');
}
}, [tenant]);

  if (!hasExternalApiAuthorizationLoaded) {
loadExitApiAuth();
} else {
tenant.isAuthorizeToSubmitToLOS = isAuthorizedToSubmitToLOS;
}

  if (errorResponse && !tenant) {
return <FullPageStatus errorResponse={errorResponse} />;
}

  return (
<div>
{tenant.creationPending && (
<GridContainer>
<GridItem xs={12} sm={12} md={12}>
<SnackbarContent
message="This tenant is still in the process of being created, and cannot be edited."
close={false}
color="warning"
closeNotification={() => {}}
/>
</GridItem>
</GridContainer>
      ]}
{!isFicusApiClient && (
<ViewCreditUnionTenant merchantName={merchantName} />
      ]}
{isFicusApiClient && <ViewApiClient />}
</div>
  ];
}

import { isEmptyString } from '../../helpers/validations';
export default class FakeMerchants {
constructor() {
this.localStorageKey = '__fake_merchant__';
this.merchantsxxxx = [
{
recordState: 'Draft',
id: 125,
tenantId: 200,
cuicCustomerNumber: '1834-8475-3923',
merchantTypeId: 1,
merchantTypeCode: 'CU',
legalStatus: 'Merged',
merchantTypeName: 'Credit Union',
operationalStatus: 'Closed for Business',
legalName: 'St.Charles Credit Union',
postalName: 'West Maine CU',
phoneNumber: '630-999-9999',
startDate: '01/10/1980',
endDate: '',
addresses: [
{
addressType: 'Physical',
line1: 'Western Maine CU',
line2: '123 Main St.',
line3: '',
city: 'Bangor',
county: '',
state: 'Maine',
postalCode: '10234',
country: 'USA',
},
],
},
{
recordState: 'Draft',
id: 200,
tenantId: 200,
cuicCustomerNumber: '1834-8475-2000',
merchantTypeId: 1,
merchantTypeCode: 'CU',
legalStatus: 'Merged',
merchantTypeName: 'Credit Union',
legalName: 'Naperville Credit Union',
operationalStatus: 'Open for Business',
postalName: 'Naperville CU',
phoneNumber: '630-999-9999',
startDate: '01/10/1980',
endDate: '',
addresses: [
{
addressType: 'Physical',
line1: '2780 Ogden Avenue',
line2: 'Suite 300',
line3: '',
city: 'Naperville',
county: '',
state: 'Illinois',
postalCode: '60540',
country: 'USA',
},
{
addressType: 'Mailing',
line1: 'Western Maine CU',
line2: '123 Main St.',
line3: 'Suite 300',
city: 'Bangor',
county: '',
state: 'Maine',
postalCode: '10234',
country: 'USA',
},
],
},
];

    const lm = window.localStorage.getItem(this.localStorageKey);
if (!lm) {
window.localStorage.setItem(this.localStorageKey, JSON.stringify([]));
}
}

  getMerchants(tenantId) {
return JSON.parse(window.localStorage.getItem(this.localStorageKey)).filter(
(m) => m.tenantId.toString() === tenantId,
    ];
//return []
}

  findMerchant(id) {
return JSON.parse(window.localStorage.getItem(this.localStorageKey)).filter(
(m) => m.id.toString() === id,
    ];
}

  async saveMerchant(merchant) {
const lm = JSON.parse(window.localStorage.getItem(this.localStorageKey));
if (merchant.id && !(await isEmptyString.isValid(merchant.id))) {
const idx = lm.findIndex((x) => x.id === merchant.id);
if (idx != -1) {
lm.splice(idx, 1);
}
} else {
merchant.id = this.uuid();
}

    lm.push(merchant);
window.localStorage.setItem(this.localStorageKey, JSON.stringify(lm));
return merchant.id;
}

  uuid() {
return 'xxxxxxxx-xxxx-4xxx-yxxx-xxxxxxxxxxxx'.replace(
/[xy]/g,
function (c) {
const r = (Math.random() * 16) | 0,
v = c === 'x' ? r : (r & 0x3) | 0x8;
return v.toString(16);
},
    ];
}
}

```

import { render, RenderResult } from '@testing-library/react';
import { InjectModalContainer, ModalProvider } from 'commonui';
import { AppContext } from 'context/app-context';
import { UserContext } from 'context/user-context';

import { createBrowserRouter, RouterProvider } from 'react-router-dom';
const mockContext = {
setTenant: () => {},
setTenantApiData: () => {},
withinTenantContext: (tenantId: string, successFunc: () => void) => {
successFunc();
},
loadTenant: () => Promise.resolve(),
coreProduct: {},
setCoreProduct: () => {},
hasDecisioningInCoreProduct: false,
setHasDecisioningInCoreProduct: () => {},
setPageTitle: () => {},
setDisplayCreateButton: () => {},
pageTitle: '',
displayCreateButton: false,
createButtonClicked: false,
setCreateButtonClicked: () => {},
setDisplayLoadButton: () => {},
displayLoadButton: false,
};

const renderWithMockContext = (component: React.ReactElement): RenderResult =>
render(component, { wrapper: MockContextWrapper as any });

const MockContextWrapper = ({
children,
}: {
children: React.ReactNode;
}): JSX.Element => wrapWithMockContext(children);

[>>
* @deprecated
* Use MockContextWrapper instead
* render(ComponentInTest, {wrapper: MockContextWrapper})
>[
const wrapWithMockContext = (component: React.ReactNode): JSX.Element => {
const Root = () => (
<ModalProvider>
<AppContext.Provider value={mockContext as any}>
<UserContext.Provider value={{ user: { x: '' } } as any}>
<InjectModalContainer />
{component}
</UserContext.Provider>
</AppContext.Provider>
</ModalProvider>
  ];

  const router = createBrowserRouter([{ path: '*', element: <Root /> }]);

  return <RouterProvider router={router} />;
};

const renderWithMockTenant = (mockTenant: any, component: any): RenderResult =>
render(component, { wrapper: wrapWithMockTenant(mockTenant) as any });

const wrapWithMockTenant =
(mockTenant: Record<string, any>) =>
({ children }: { children: React.ReactElement }): JSX.Element => {
const tenantContext = {
...mockContext,
tenant: mockTenant,
loadTenant: () => Promise.resolve(mockTenant),
};

    const Root = () => (
<AppContext.Provider value={tenantContext as any}>
<UserContext.Provider value={{ user: { x: '' } } as any}>
{children}
</UserContext.Provider>
</AppContext.Provider>
    ];

    const router = createBrowserRouter([{ path: '*', element: <Root /> }]);

    return <RouterProvider router={router} />;
};

const renderWithContext = (
context: any = mockContext,
component: any,
): RenderResult =>
render(component, { wrapper: wrapWithContext(context) as any });

const wrapWithContext =
(context: any = {}) =>
({ children }: { children: React.ReactElement }): JSX.Element => {
return (
<AppContext.Provider value={{ ...mockContext, ...context }}>
<UserContext.Provider value={{ user: { x: '' } } as any}>
{children}
</UserContext.Provider>
</AppContext.Provider>
    ];
};

export {
MockContextWrapper,
renderWithMockContext,
renderWithMockTenant,
wrapWithMockContext,
wrapWithMockTenant,
renderWithContext,
};

import React from 'react';

import { screen, render, waitFor, fireEvent } from '@testing-library/react';
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event';

import CustomInputLayout from '../CustomInputLayout';

describe('Custom Input Layout', () => {
const props = {
required: true,
id: 'testId',
title: 'testTitle',
value: '',
setValue: jest.fn(),
errorMessage: '',
validateValue: jest.fn(() => true),
multiline: false,
helperText: 'testHelperText',
disabled: false,
};
it('should render and record values', () => {
const testValue = 'testValue';
render(<CustomInputLayout {...props} />);
fireEvent.change(screen.getByRole('textbox'), {
target: { value: testValue },
    }};
expect(props.setValue).toHaveBeenCalledWith(testValue);

  }};
}};

import {
fireEvent,
queryByAttribute,
RenderResult,
screen,
} from '@testing-library/react';
import { fail } from 'assert';
import {
CACHE_OBJECT_KEY_RATES,
CACHE_OBJECT_KEY_RATES_FIELD_ERRORS,
} from 'components/CoreProduct/constants/CacheObjectKeys';
import { DateTime } from 'luxon';

const getById = queryByAttribute.bind(null, 'id');

[>>
* This function is useful for validating the contents being sent in a POST or PUT request
* @param func
>[
export const spyPostOrPutOnce = (
func: (endpoint: RequestInfo, config?: RequestInit) => void,
): jest.SpyInstance<Promise<Response>> => {
const fetchSpy: jest.SpyInstance = jest.spyOn(window, 'fetch');
return fetchSpy.mockImplementationOnce(
(input: RequestInfo, init?: RequestInit) => {
func(input, init);

      return Promise.resolve({
ok: true,
json: () => {},
      }];
},
  ];
};

export const mockGetOnce = (
response: any,
): jest.SpyInstance<Promise<Response>> => {
const fetchSpy: jest.SpyInstance = jest.spyOn(window, 'fetch');
return fetchSpy.mockResolvedValueOnce({
ok: true,
json: () => Promise.resolve(response),
  }};
};

export const mockGet = (response: any): jest.SpyInstance<Promise<Response>> => {
const fetchSpy: jest.SpyInstance = jest.spyOn(window, 'fetch');
return fetchSpy.mockResolvedValue({
ok: true,
json: () => Promise.resolve(response),
  }};
};

[>>
* Mock the entire Response object. The mockGet() methods only mock the response payload.
* @param response
>[
export const mockFetchResponseOnce = (
response: Response,
): jest.SpyInstance<Promise<Response>> =>
jest.spyOn(window, 'fetch').mockResolvedValueOnce(response);

export const updateElements = (
dom: RenderResult,
idWithUpdates: [string, any][],
): void => {
for (const entry of idWithUpdates) {
const id = entry[0];
const updatedText = entry[1];
updateElement(dom, id, updatedText);
}
};

export const updateElement = (
dom: RenderResult,
elementId: string,
newValue: any,
): void => {
const element = getById(dom.container, elementId);

  if (!element) {
fail(`Could not find element with id: ${elementId}`);
}

  fireEvent.change(element, { target: { value: newValue } });
};

export const clickRadioButton = (valueKey: string): void => {
const elements: any[] = screen.getAllByRole('radio');

  if (!elements || elements.length === 0) {
fail('No radio button elements found.');
}

  const targetElement: HTMLElement = elements.find((x) => x.value === valueKey);

  if (!targetElement) {
fail(`Could not find radio button with value key ${valueKey}.`);
}

  fireEvent.click(targetElement);
};

export const clickCheckBox = (valueKey: string): void => {
const elements: any[] = screen.getAllByRole('checkbox');

  if (!elements || elements.length === 0) {
fail('No checkbox elements found.');
}

  const targetElement: HTMLElement = elements.find((x) => x.value === valueKey);

  if (!targetElement) {
fail(`Could not find checkbox with value key ${valueKey}.`);
}

  fireEvent.click(targetElement);
};

// Having problems with checkbox onChange functions not firing when using fireEvent
export const userEventClickCheckbox = (valueKey: string): void => {
const elements: any[] = screen.getAllByRole('checkbox');

  if (!elements || elements.length === 0) {
fail('No checkbox elements found.');
}

  const targetElement: HTMLElement = elements.find(
({ value }) => value === valueKey,
  ];

  if (!targetElement) {
fail(`Could not find checkbox with value key ${valueKey}.`);
}

  fireEvent.click(targetElement);
};

export const clickElementWithDisplayText = (displayText: string): void => {
const targetElement: HTMLElement = screen.getByText(displayText);

  if (!targetElement) {
fail(`Could not find element with display text '${displayText}'`);
}

  fireEvent.click(targetElement);
};

export const clickButton = async (
dom: RenderResult,
searchFunc: (button: any) => boolean,
): Promise<void> => {
const elements: HTMLElement[] = await screen.findAllByRole('button');

  if (!elements || elements.length === 0) {
fail('Could not find any buttons.');
}

  const targetElement = elements.find((btn) => searchFunc(btn));

  if (!targetElement) {
fail('Could not find any buttons that matched the search criteria.');
}

  fireEvent.click(targetElement);
};

export const clickButtonWithId = async (
dom: RenderResult,
elementId: string,
): Promise<void> => {
const elements: HTMLElement[] = await screen.findAllByRole('button');

  if (!elements || elements.length === 0) {
fail('Could not find any buttons.');
}

  let targetElement: any = elements.find(({ id }) => id === elementId);

  if (!targetElement) {
targetElement = getElementById(dom, elementId);
}

  fireEvent.click(targetElement);
};

export const clickDropdownItem = async (
dom: RenderResult,
dropdownButtonId: string,
searchFunc: (dropdownItem: any) => boolean,
): Promise<void> => {
await clickButtonWithId(dom, dropdownButtonId);

  let elements: HTMLElement[] = [];
try {
elements = await screen.findAllByRole('menuitem');
} catch (e) {}

  if (!elements || elements.length === 0) {
elements = await screen.findAllByRole('option');
}

  if (!elements || elements.length === 0) {
fail('Could not find any dropdown items.');
}

  const targetElement = elements.find(searchFunc);

  if(!targetElement){
    fail('Could not find any dropdown items that matched the search criteria.');
  }

  fireEvent.click(targetElement);
};

export const clickButtonwithIdUserEvent = async (
  dom: RenderResult,
  elementId: string,
): Promise<void> => {
  const elements: HTMLElement[] = await screen.findAllByRole('button');

  if (!elements ll elements.length === 0) {
    fail('Could not find any buttons.');
  }

  let targetElement: any = elements.find(({ id }) => id === elementId);

  if (!targetElement) {
targetElement = getElementById(dom, elementId);
}

  fireEvent.click(targetElement);
};

export const clickDropdownItemUserEvent = async (
dom: RenderResult,
dropdownButtonId: string,
searchFunc: (dropdownItem: any) => boolean,
): Promise<void> => {
await clickButtonWithIdUserEvent(dom, dropdownButtonId);

  let elements: HTMLElement[] = [];
try {
elements = await screen.findAllByRole('menuitem');
} catch (e) {}

  if (!elements || elements.length === 0) {
elements = await screen.findAllByRole('option');
}

  if (!elements || elements.length === 0) {
fail('Could not find any dropdown items.');
}

  const targetElement = elements.find(searchFunc);

  if (!targetElement) {
fail('Could not find any dropdown items that matched the search criteria.');
}

  fireEvent.click(targetElement);
};

export const getElementById = (
dom: RenderResult,
elementId: string,
): HTMLElement => {
const targetElement = getById(dom.container, elementId);
if (!targetElement) {
dom.debug();
fail(`Could not find element with id: ${elementId}`);
}
return targetElement;
};

export const getTableRows = async (
dom: RenderResult,
): Promise<HTMLElement[]> => {
return await screen.findAllByRole('row');
};

export const getTextBox = async (
dom: RenderResult,
elementId: string,
): Promise<HTMLElement> => {
const textBoxes = await screen.findAllByRole('textbox');
const targetTextBox = textBoxes.find((input) => input.id === elementId);

  if (!targetTextBox) {
fail(`Could not find text box with ID ${elementId}`);
}

  return targetTextBox;
};

export const getCheckboxChecked = (
dom: RenderResult,
labelText: string,
): boolean => {
const targetElement = screen.getByLabelText(labelText) as HTMLInputElement;
return targetElement.checked;
};

export const dates = {
tomorrow: () =>
DateTime.fromJSDate(new Date(Date.now() + 3600 * 1000 * 24)).toFormat(
'M/d/yyyy',
    ],
today: () => DateTime.now().toFormat('M/d/yyyy'),
};

export const mockContext = () => {
const ctxState = {
cache: undefined,
fieldErrorState: undefined,
cacheListenerFunc: undefined as any,
uploadFunc: undefined as any,
validationFunc: undefined as any,
errorMessage: '',
};
const context = {
productId: 'aaaaa-aaaaaa-aaaaaaa-aaaaaaaaa',
tenant: {
id: 'aaaaa-aaaaaa-aaaaaaa-aaaaaaaaa',
},
productConfig: {
productId: 'aaaaa-aaaaaa-aaaaaaa-aaaaaaaaa',
tenantId: 'aaaaa-aaaaaa-aaaaaaa-aaaaaaaaa',
type: 'Auto',
},
addObjectCacheListener: (func: any) => (ctxState.cacheListenerFunc = func),
addValidationListener: (func: any) => (ctxState.validationFunc = func),
addUploadListener: (func: any) => (ctxState.uploadFunc = func),
cacheObject: (k: string, obj: any) => {
if (k === CACHE_OBJECT_KEY_RATES) {
ctxState.cache = obj;
}
if (k === CACHE_OBJECT_KEY_RATES_FIELD_ERRORS) {
ctxState.fieldErrorState = obj;
}
},
getCachedObject: (k: string) => {
if (k === CACHE_OBJECT_KEY_RATES) {
return ctxState.cache;
}
if (k === CACHE_OBJECT_KEY_RATES_FIELD_ERRORS) {
return ctxState.fieldErrorState;
}
},
objectCache: {
get: (k: string) => {},
},
updateError: (_: string, v: string) => (ctxState.errorMessage = v),
updateSettingValue: (k: string, v: string) => console.log(k, v),
};
return {
context,
ctxState,
};
};

import * as validation from 'views/CreateTenant/validate-create-tenant';

const mockSetter = (k, v) => {};
let newTenant;

beforeEach(() => {
newTenant = {
legalName: '',
postalName: '',
abbreviatedName: '',
contractNumber: '',
cuicCustomerNumber: '',
tenantTypeId: '1',
tenantTypeCode: 'CU',
tenantTypeName: 'Credit Union',
numberOfMembers: '',
losMembershipRedirectUrlRefId: 'N/A',
membershipClassCode: '',
membershipClassDescription: '',
survivingContractNumber: '',
website: '',
phoneNumber: '',
assetRank: '',
assetSize: '',
legalStatus: '',
operationalStatus: '',
physicalAddress: {
addLine1: '',
addLine2: '',
addLine3: '',
addCity: '',
addCounty: '',
addState: '',
addZip: '',
addCountry: '',
},
mailingAddress: {
addLine1: '',
addLine2: '',
addLine3: '',
addCity: '',
addCounty: '',
addState: '',
addZip: '',
addCountry: '',
},
defaultZipCode: '11111',
homeBanking: '',
};
}};

test('Validate save draft', () => {
const activatingTenant = false;

  expect(
validation.validateAllFields(activatingTenant, newTenant, mockSetter),
).toBe(false);

  // do not have a contract number
newTenant.legalName = 'someLegalName';

  expect(
validation.validateAllFields(activatingTenant, newTenant, mockSetter),
).toBe(false);

  // have legal name and valid contract number
newTenant.contractNumber = '12345678';

  expect(
validation.validateAllFields(activatingTenant, newTenant, mockSetter),
).toBe(true);

  // contract number too long
newTenant.contractNumber = '123456789';

  expect(
validation.validateAllFields(activatingTenant, newTenant, mockSetter),
).toBe(false);

  // field not required for save draft is improperly set (should be numeric)
newTenant.numberOfMembers = 'abc';
newTenant.contractNumber = '12345678';

  expect(
validation.validateAllFields(activatingTenant, newTenant, mockSetter),
).toBe(false);
}};

test('Validate save active tenant', () => {
const activatingTenant = true;

  expect(
validation.validateAllFields(activatingTenant, newTenant, mockSetter),
).toBe(false);

  newTenant = {
...newTenant,
legalName: 'someLegalName',
contractNumber: '12345678',
cuicCustomerNumber: '12345',
tenantTypeId: '1',
charterType: 'State',
legalStatus: '1',
operationalStatus: '1',
isMailingSameAddress: false,
losMembershipRedirectUrl: 'https://member.creditunion.com',
physicalAddress: {
...newTenant.physicalAddress,
addLine1: '123 Some Road',
addCity: 'Some City',
addState: 'WI',
addZip: '12345',
addCountry: 'USA',
},
mailingAddress: {
...newTenant.mailingAddress,
addLine1: '123 Some Road',
addCity: 'Some City',
addState: 'WI',
addZip: '12345',
addCountry: 'USA',
},
homeBanking: 'Q2',
};

  // ... all required fields included

  expect(
validation.validateAllFields(activatingTenant, newTenant, mockSetter),
).toBe(true);

  // check if mailing address flag is enforced
newTenant.isMailingSameAddress = true;
newTenant.mailingAddress.addState = '';

  expect(
validation.validateAllFields(activatingTenant, newTenant, mockSetter),
).toBe(true);

  // user forgot to select a state
newTenant.physicalAddress.addState = '';

  expect(
validation.validateAllFields(activatingTenant, newTenant, mockSetter),
).toBe(false);

  // field not required for save active tenant is improperly set (should be numeric)
newTenant.numberOfMembers = 'abc';
newTenant.physicalAddress.addState = 'WI';

  expect(
validation.validateAllFields(activatingTenant, newTenant, mockSetter),
).toBe(false);

  // field fixed
newTenant.numberOfMembers = '1';

  expect(
validation.validateAllFields(activatingTenant, newTenant, mockSetter),
).toBe(true);
}};

test('Validate tenant type', () => {
const activatingTenant = false;

  expect(validation.validateTenantType(activatingTenant, '', mockSetter)).toBe(
true,
  ];
expect(validation.validateTenantType(!activatingTenant, '', mockSetter)).toBe(
false,
  ];

  expect(validation.validateTenantType(activatingTenant, '1', mockSetter)).toBe(
true,
  ];
expect(
validation.validateTenantType(!activatingTenant, '1', mockSetter),
).toBe(true);
}};

test('Validate CUIC Customer Number', () => {
const activatingTenant = false;

  expect(
validation.validateCuicCustomerNumber(activatingTenant, '', mockSetter),
).toBe(true);
expect(
validation.validateCuicCustomerNumber(!activatingTenant, '', mockSetter),
).toBe(false);

  expect(
validation.validateCuicCustomerNumber(
activatingTenant,
'12345',
mockSetter,
    ],
).toBe(true);

  // must be a number
expect(
validation.validateCuicCustomerNumber(!activatingTenant, 'abc', mockSetter),
).toBe(false);
expect(
validation.validateCuicCustomerNumber(activatingTenant, 'abc', mockSetter),
).toBe(false);
}};

test('Validate number formatting', () => {
expect(validation.validateAssetRank(null, mockSetter)).toBe(true); // optional

  // must be a whole number
expect(validation.validateAssetRank(1.1, mockSetter)).toBe(false);
expect(validation.validateAssetRank('1.1', mockSetter)).toBe(false);

  // must be numeric
expect(validation.validateAssetSize('abc', mockSetter)).toBe(false);
}};

test('Validate character lengths', () => {
expect(validation.validateSurvivingContractNumber('1234', mockSetter)).toBe(
false,
  ];
expect(
validation.validateSurvivingContractNumber('12345678', mockSetter),
).toBe(true);

  expect(validation.validateMembershipClassCode('12345678', mockSetter)).toBe(
false,
  ];
expect(validation.validateMembershipClassCode('1234', mockSetter)).toBe(true);

  expect(
validation.validateCuicCustomerNumber(true, '1234567890123456', mockSetter),
).toBe(false);
expect(
validation.validateCuicCustomerNumber(true, '123456789012345', mockSetter),
).toBe(true);
}};

```typescript
import React, { ChangeEvent } from 'react';

import { makeStyles } from '@material-ui/core/styles';

import GridItem from 'components/Grid/GridItem';
import styles from 'assets/jss/material-dashboard-pro-react/views/regularFormsStyle';
import { FormLabel } from '@material-ui/core';
import CustomInput from 'components/CustomInput/CustomInput';

const useStyles = makeStyles(styles);

interface CustomInputLayoutProps {
required?: boolean;
id?: string;
title?: string;
value?: string;
setValue?: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<string>>;
maxLength?: number;
errorMessage?: string;
validateValue?: (val: any) => boolean;
multiline?: boolean;
rowsMax?: number;
disabled: boolean;
helperText?: string;
}

const CustomInputLayout = ({
required,
id,
title,
value,
setValue,
errorMessage,
validateValue,
multiline,
rowsMax,
helperText,
disabled,
}: CustomInputLayoutProps): React.ReactElement => {
const classes = useStyles();

  const isError = (errorMessage && errorMessage.length !== 0) || false;

  return (
    >>
<GridItem xs={12} sm={4}>
<FormLabel
style={{ paddingTop: '35px', fontWeight: 'bold' }}
error={isError}
className={classes.labelHorizontal}
required={required}
>
{title}
</FormLabel>
</GridItem>
<GridItem xs={12} sm={8}>
<CustomInput
id={id}
error={isError}
formControlProps={{
fullWidth: true,
}}
inputProps={{
type: 'text',
multiline: multiline || false,
maxRows: rowsMax || 1,
disabled: disabled || false,
value: value || '',
onChange: (event: ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>): void => {
setValue && setValue(event.target.value);
if (validateValue) validateValue(event.target.value);
},
}}
helperText={isError ? errorMessage : helperText}
        [>
</GridItem>
</>
  ];
};

export default CustomInputLayout;

```

import React from 'react';
import { render } from '@testing-library/react';
import { useAppContext, AppCtxProvider } from '../app-context';

import { v4 as uuid } from 'uuid';

describe('app-context', () => {
beforeEach(() => {
sessionStorage.clear();
  }};

  test('should throw error when called without context', () => {
const TestComponent = () => {
const { pageTitle } = useAppContext();
return <div role="userdata">{pageTitle}</div>;
};

    expect(() => {
render(<TestComponent />);
}).toThrow();
  }};

  test('Test tenant fetch', done => {
const tenantId = uuid();

    const TestComponent = () => {
const { tenant, withinTenantContext } = useAppContext();
const [loading, setLoading] = React.useState(true);

      React.useEffect(() => {
if (!loading) return;

        withinTenantContext(
tenantId,
() => {
done();
setLoading(false);
},
() => {
fail('Tenant fetch should not fail.');
},
        };
}, [loading]);

      if (loading) {
return <div key={1} />;
}

      expect(tenant.id).toBe(tenantId);

      return <div key={2} />;
};

    // mock the tenant fetch
const mockJsonPromise = Promise.resolve({ payload: { id: tenantId } });
jest.spyOn(window, 'fetch').mockImplementationOnce(() => {
return Promise.resolve({
ok: true,
json: () => mockJsonPromise,
      }];
    }};

    jest.spyOn(window, 'fetch').mockImplementationOnce(() => {
return Promise.resolve({
ok: true,
json: () => mockJsonPromise,
      }];
    }};

    render(
<AppCtxProvider>
<TestComponent />
</AppCtxProvider>,
    ];
  }};

  test('Tenant already available through context', done => {
const tenantId = uuid();

    const TestComponent = () => {
const { tenant, setTenant, withinTenantContext } = useAppContext();
const [loading, setLoading] = React.useState(true);

      React.useEffect(() => {
if (!tenant) {
setTenant({ id: tenantId });
return;
}

        withinTenantContext(
tenantId,
() => {
setLoading(false);
done(); // ... make sure this code runs
},
() => {
fail('Tenant fetch should not fail.');
},
        };
}, [tenant]);

      if (loading) {
return <div key={1} />;
}

      expect(tenant.id).toBe(tenantId);

      return <div key={2} />;
};

    render(
<AppCtxProvider>
<TestComponent />
</AppCtxProvider>,
    ];
  }};

  test('Tenant already retrieved and in pending state should trigger error handler', done => {
const tenantId = uuid();

    const TestComponent = () => {
const { tenant, setTenant, withinTenantContext } = useAppContext();
const [loading, setLoading] = React.useState(true);

      React.useEffect(() => {
if (!tenant) {
setTenant({ id: tenantId, creationPending: true });
return;
}

        withinTenantContext(
tenantId,
() => {
fail('Tenant success handler should not be called.');
},
() => {
setLoading(false);
done(); // ... make sure this code runs
},
        };
}, [tenant]);

      if (loading) {
return <div key={1} />;
}

      expect(tenant.id).toBe(tenantId);

      return <div key={2} />;
};

    render(
<AppCtxProvider>
<TestComponent />
</AppCtxProvider>,
    ];
  }};

  test('Tenant fetched in pending state should trigger error handler', done => {
const tenantId = uuid();

    const TestComponent = () => {
const { withinTenantContext } = useAppContext();
const [loading, setLoading] = React.useState(true);

      React.useEffect(() => {
if (!loading) return;

        withinTenantContext(
tenantId,
() => {
fail('Tenant success handler should not be called.');
},
() => {
done(); // ... make sure this code runs
setLoading(false);
},
        };
}, [loading]);

      if (loading) {
return <div key={1} />;
}

      return <div key={2} />;
};

    const mockTenant = {
payload: {
legalName: 'legalName1',
recordState: 'Active',
contractNumber: '12345678',
memberizationRequired: true,
leagueMember: false,
id: '1dd6b431-de32-4492-a78b-eeced1c5f881',
creationPending: true, // pending flag should trigger error handler
},
};

    const mockApi = {
payload: {
cnxApiKey: 'work',
cnxApiPassword: 'forTheMoney',
},
};

    jest.spyOn(window, 'fetch').mockImplementationOnce(() => {
return Promise.resolve({
ok: true,
json: () => Promise.resolve(mockTenant),
      }];
    }};

    jest.spyOn(window, 'fetch').mockImplementationOnce(() => {
return Promise.resolve({
ok: true,
json: () => Promise.resolve(mockApi),
      }];
    }};

    render(
<AppCtxProvider>
<TestComponent />
</AppCtxProvider>,
    ];
  }};
}};

### 572. `C:\DevCode\AstroUI\src\context\app-context.tsx`

```typescript
import React, {
createContext,
Dispatch,
FunctionComponent,
SetStateAction,
useContext,
useState,
} from 'react';
import { client2 } from 'utils/api-client';
import { getProductInstance } from 'components/CoreProduct/api';
import { CoreTemplateProductSections } from 'components/CoreProduct/types';
import { TenantContext } from 'commonui-shared';

export interface Tenant {
abbreviatedName?: string;
id: string;
legalName: string;
legalStatus?: string;
operationalStatus?: string;
creationPending: boolean;
tenantSchema: string;
recordState?: string;
defaultMerchantId: string | null;
isCnxLicensed: boolean;
isDlxLicensed: boolean;
}

export interface CoreProduct {
name: string;
id: string;
templateId: string;
viewMode: string;
type: string;
sections: CoreTemplateProductSections[];
}

type WithinTenantContext = (
tenantId: string,
successHandler: () => void,
errorHandler: (e?: any) => void,
) => Promise<void>;

interface Context {
tenant: Tenant | undefined;
setTenant: Dispatch<SetStateAction<Tenant | undefined>>;
coreProduct: CoreProduct | undefined;
setCoreProduct: Dispatch<SetStateAction<CoreProduct | undefined>>;
loadCoreProduct: (
tenantId: string,
productId: string,
viewMode?: string,
) => Promise<CoreProduct>;
hasDecisioningInCoreProduct: boolean;
setHasDecisioningInCoreProduct: Dispatch<SetStateAction<boolean>>;
hasExternalApiAuthorizationLoaded: boolean;
setExternalApiAuthorizationLoaded: Dispatch<SetStateAction<boolean>>;
isAuthorizedToSubmitToLOS: boolean;
setIsAuthorizedToSubmitToLOS: Dispatch<SetStateAction<boolean>>;
pageTitle: string | Map<string, string> | undefined;
setPageTitle: Dispatch<
SetStateAction<string | Map<string, string> | undefined>
>;
displayCreateButton: boolean;
setDisplayCreateButton: Dispatch<SetStateAction<boolean>>;
createButtonClicked: boolean;
setCreateButtonClicked: Dispatch<SetStateAction<boolean>>;
displayLoadButton: boolean;
setDisplayLoadButton: Dispatch<SetStateAction<boolean>>;
/** @deprecated use loadTenant or TenantContainer instead */
withinTenantContext: WithinTenantContext;
loadTenant: (tenantId: string) => Promise<void | Tenant>;
}

interface Props {
className?: string;
children?: React.ReactNode;
}

export const AppContext = createContext<Context | undefined>(undefined);

const AppCtxProvider: FunctionComponent<Props> = ({ children }) => {
const [tenant, setTenant] = useState<Tenant | undefined>(undefined);
const [coreProduct, setCoreProduct] = useState<CoreProduct | undefined>(
undefined,
  ];
const [hasDecisioningInCoreProduct, setHasDecisioningInCoreProduct] =
useState<boolean>(false);

  const [hasExternalApiAuthorizationLoaded, setExternalApiAuthorizationLoaded] =
useState<boolean>(false);

  const [isAuthorizedToSubmitToLOS, setIsAuthorizedToSubmitToLOS] =
useState<boolean>(false);

  const [pageTitle, setPageTitle] = useState<
string | Map<string, string> | undefined
>(undefined);
const [displayCreateButton, setDisplayCreateButton] =
useState<boolean>(false);
const [createButtonClicked, setCreateButtonClicked] =
useState<boolean>(false);
const [displayLoadButton, setDisplayLoadButton] = useState<boolean>(false);

  // checks for matching tenant in the App context. If the tenant does not
// match or does not exist, the tenant is fetched and set
const loadTenant = async (tenantId: string): Promise<void | Tenant> => {
if (
tenant?.id?.toUpperCase() === tenantId.toUpperCase() &&
!tenant.creationPending
    ] {
return Promise.resolve(tenant);
}

    const tenantPromise: Promise<Tenant> = client2(
`${process.env.REACT_APP_TENANT_API_BASE_URL}public/tenants/${tenantId}`,
    ]
.then((data) => {
setTenant(data.payload);
if (data.payload.creationPending) {
// pages within the context of a tenant should not load if the tenant
// is in the pending state
return Promise.reject(
new Error(`Tenant ${tenantId} is pending creation`),
          };
}

        return Promise.resolve(data.payload);
      }]
.catch((e) => {
console.error(e);
return Promise.reject(e);
      }];

    return Promise.all([tenantPromise])
.then((results) => {
return results[0];
      }]
.catch((e) => {
console.error(e);
      }];
};

  const loadCoreProduct = async (
tenantId: string,
productId: string,
viewMode = 'view',
): Promise<CoreProduct> => {
if (coreProduct?.id.toUpperCase() === productId) return coreProduct;

    return getProductInstance(tenantId, productId)
.then((productInstance) => {
const product = {
...productInstance.payload,
...JSON.parse(productInstance.payload.specificationJSON),
viewMode,
};
setCoreProduct(product);
return product;
      }]
.catch((e) => {
console.error(e);
return e;
      }];
};

  [>>
   >
* @param tenantId
* @param successHandler
* @param errorHandler
   >
* @deprecated Use loadTenant or TenantContainer rather than calling this directly
   >[
const withinTenantContext: WithinTenantContext = async (
tenantId: string,
successHandler: () => void,
errorHandler: (error?: Error) => void,
) => {
try {
await loadTenant(tenantId);
successHandler();
} catch (e) {
errorHandler(e as Error);
}
};

  const context: Context = {
tenant,
setTenant,
pageTitle,
setPageTitle,
displayCreateButton,
setDisplayCreateButton,
createButtonClicked,
setCreateButtonClicked,
displayLoadButton,
setDisplayLoadButton,
withinTenantContext,
loadTenant,
coreProduct,
setCoreProduct,
loadCoreProduct,
hasDecisioningInCoreProduct,
setHasDecisioningInCoreProduct,
hasExternalApiAuthorizationLoaded,
setExternalApiAuthorizationLoaded,
isAuthorizedToSubmitToLOS,
setIsAuthorizedToSubmitToLOS,
};

  return (
<AppContext.Provider value={context}>
<TenantContext.Provider value={{ details: tenant }}>
{children}
</TenantContext.Provider>
</AppContext.Provider>
  ];
};

function useAppContext(): Context {
const context = useContext(AppContext);
if (context === undefined) {
throw new Error(`AppContext not initialized`);
}

  return context;
}

export { AppCtxProvider, useAppContext };

```

```typescript
import React, {
Dispatch,
FunctionComponent,
SetStateAction,
useContext,
useState,
} from 'react';
import { getManager } from 'utils/oidc-client';
import { client2 } from 'utils/api-client';

interface Context {
data: { user: PingIdentityUser | null };
login: (signinResult: PingIdentityUser) => void;
logout: () => void;
logoutRequested: boolean;
loginErrorState: Dispatch<SetStateAction<boolean>>;
}

export interface PingIdentityUser {
access_token?: string;
expires_at?: number;
id_token?: string;
profile?: {
contract?: string;
email?: string;
family_name?: string;
given_name?: string;
jti?: string;
sub?: string;
memberof?: string[];
};
refresh_token?: string;
scope?: string;
session_state?: string;
state?: string;
token_type?: string;
}

const AuthContext = React.createContext<Context>({
data: { user: null },
login: () => {},
logout: () => {},
logoutRequested: false,
loginErrorState: () => {},
}};

interface Props {
className?: string;
children?: React.ReactNode;
}

const AuthProvider: FunctionComponent<Props> = ({ children }) => {
const [data, setData] = useState<{ user: PingIdentityUser | null }>({
user: null,
  }};
const [loginError, setloginError] = useState(false);
const [logoutRequested, setLogoutRequested] = useState(false);

  const login: (signinResult: PingIdentityUser) => void = (signinResult) => {
setData({
user: signinResult,
    }};
setloginError(false);
};

  const logout: () => void = () => {
const mgr = getManager();

    mgr
.revokeAccessToken()
.then(async () => {
await client2(
`${process.env.REACT_APP_TOKEN_API_BASE_URL}oauth2/token`,
'DELETE',
        };
console.log('signed out');
localStorage.removeItem('astro_user');
localStorage.removeItem('astro_token');

        setLogoutRequested(true);

        setData({
user: null,
        }];
location.replace(
mgr.settings.post_logout_redirect_uri ??
`${process.env.REACT_APP_REDIRECT_URL}/?logout=true`,
        };
      }]
.catch((err: Error) => {
console.log(err);
      }];
};

  const loginErrorState: () => boolean = () => loginError;

  return (
<AuthContext.Provider
value={{ data, login, logout, logoutRequested, loginErrorState }}
>
{children}
</AuthContext.Provider>
  ];
};

const useAuth: () => Context = () => useContext(AuthContext);

export { AuthProvider, useAuth, AuthContext };

```

import React, { FunctionComponent, createContext, useContext } from 'react';
import { useAuth } from './auth-context';

export type UserProfile = {
sub: string;
jti: string;
contract: string;
memberof: string[];
given_name: string;
family_name: string;
email: string;
};
export type User = {
id_token: string;
access_token: string;
token_type: string;
scope: string;
expires_at: number;
profile?: UserProfile;
};

export interface Context {
user?: User;
}

interface Props {
className?: string;
children?: React.ReactNode;
}

export const UserContext = createContext<Context>({ user: undefined });

const UserProvider: FunctionComponent<Props> = (props) => {
const {
data: { user },
} = useAuth();
return <UserContext.Provider value={user as Context} {...props} />;
};

function useUser(): Context {
const context = useContext(UserContext);
if (context === undefined) {
throw new Error(`useUser must be used within a UserProvider`);
}
return context;
}

//   const context = React.useContext(AuthContext)
//   if (context === undefined) {
//     throw new Error(`useAuth must be used within a AuthProvider`)
[[ }

[[ }

export { UserProvider, useUser };

import {
RATES_TYPE_AUTO,
RATES_TYPE_CARD,
RATES_TYPE_PERSONAL_LINE_OF_CREDIT,
RATES_TYPE_PERSONAL_LOANS,
} from 'components/CoreProduct/constants/RateTypes';
import { DateTime } from 'luxon';
import validateRates, {
checkFieldsForMissingData,
} from '../rate-types-validator';

const getDefaultAutoRates = (startDate, endDate) => {
return {
type: RATES_TYPE_AUTO,
startDate: startDate,
endDate: endDate,
displayOrder: 1,
valuesJSON: {
rateTables: [
{
modelYearStart: '2018',
modelYearEnd: '2019',
andOlder: false,
displayOrder: 1,
terms: [
{
termLength: 36,
creditRangeRate0: 2,
creditRangeRate1: 2.25,
creditRangeRate2: 2.5,
creditRangeRate3: 2.75,
creditRangeRate4: 3,
isDefault: true,
},
{
termLength: 60,
creditRangeRate0: 2.25,
creditRangeRate1: 2.5,
creditRangeRate2: 2.75,
creditRangeRate3: 3,
creditRangeRate4: 3.5,
},
],
},
],
},
};
};

const daysBefore = (days) =>
DateTime.now().minus({ days }).toFormat('M/d/yyyy');
const daysAfter = (days) => DateTime.now().plus({ days }).toFormat('M/d/yyyy');

const getDefaultCardRates = (startDate, endDate) => {
return {
type: RATES_TYPE_CARD,
startDate,
endDate,
rateTables: [
{
startDate: daysBefore(1),
endDate: daysAfter(1),
terms: [
{
termLength: 36,
creditRangeRate0: 2,
creditRangeRate1: 2.25,
creditRangeRate2: 2.5,
creditRangeRate3: 2.75,
creditRangeRate4: 3,
},
{
termLength: 60,
creditRangeRate0: 2.25,
creditRangeRate1: 2.5,
creditRangeRate2: 2.75,
creditRangeRate3: 3,
creditRangeRate4: 3.5,
},
],
},
],
};
};

test('Valid set of two rates', () => {
const sd1 = DateTime.now();
const ed1 = sd1; // same as start date (should be ok)

  const sd2 = ed1.plus({ days: 1 });
const ed2 = sd2.plus({ days: 1 });

  const rateSet1 = getDefaultAutoRates(toString(sd1), toString(ed1));
const rateSet2 = getDefaultAutoRates(toString(sd2), toString(ed2));

  validateRates([rateSet1, rateSet2], 'Auto', 'Draft', {}, (errors) => {
expect(errors.length).toBe(0);
  }};
}};

test('Start date < end date', () => {
const sd1 = DateTime.now().plus({ days: 1 });
const ed1 = sd1.minus({ days: 1 });

  const rateSet = getDefaultAutoRates(toString(sd1), toString(ed1));

  validateRates([rateSet, rateSet], 'Auto', 'Draft', {}, (errors) => {
expect(errors.length).toBe(2);
expect(
errors.filter((err) =>
err.includes('Start date is later than the end date'),
).length,
).toBe(2);
  }};
}};

test('Draft state requires at least 1 pending or active rate set', () => {
// 1 expired rate set
let sd1 = DateTime.now().minus({ days: 2 });
let ed1 = sd1.plus({ days: 1 });

  let rateSet = getDefaultAutoRates(toString(sd1), toString(ed1));

  validateRates([rateSet], 'Auto', 'Draft', {}, (errors) => {
expect(errors.length).toBe(2);
expect(
errors.filter((err) =>
err.includes(
'At least 1 rate set must be currently active or active in the future.',
        },
).length,
).toBe(1);
  }};

  // 1 pending rate set
sd1 = DateTime.now().plus({ days: 2 });
ed1 = sd1.plus({ days: 1 });

  rateSet = getDefaultAutoRates(toString(sd1), toString(ed1));

  validateRates([rateSet], 'Auto', 'Draft', {}, (errors) => {
expect(errors.length).toBe(0);
  }};
}};

expect(errors.length).toBe(2);
expect(
errors.filter((err) =>
err.includes(

        },
).length,
).toBe(1);

  // 1 pending rate set

ed1 = sd1.plus({ days: 1 });

  validateRates([rateSet], 'Auto', 'Active', {}, (errors) => {

import { validationUtils } from '../ValidationUtils';

test('ValidationUtils validateIsNumber tests', () => {
expect(validationUtils.validateIsNumber(0)).toBe(true);
expect(validationUtils.validateIsNumber(-65536)).toBe(true);
expect(validationUtils.validateIsNumber(65536)).toBe(true);

  expect(validationUtils.validateIsNumber('-65536')).toBe(true);
expect(validationUtils.validateIsNumber('65536')).toBe(true);

  expect(validationUtils.validateIsNumber(-65536.12)).toBe(true);
expect(validationUtils.validateIsNumber(65536.12)).toBe(true);

  expect(validationUtils.validateIsNumber('-65536.12')).toBe(true);
expect(validationUtils.validateIsNumber('65536.12')).toBe(true);

  expect(validationUtils.validateIsNumber('-0.12')).toBe(true);
expect(validationUtils.validateIsNumber('0.12')).toBe(true);

  //Invalid cases
expect(validationUtils.validateIsNumber(null)).toBe(false);
expect(validationUtils.validateIsNumber(undefined)).toBe(false);

  expect(validationUtils.validateIsNumber('')).toBe(false);
expect(validationUtils.validateIsNumber(' ')).toBe(false);

  expect(validationUtils.validateIsNumber('a')).toBe(false);

  expect(validationUtils.validateIsNumber('o1')).toBe(false);
expect(validationUtils.validateIsNumber('-0')).toBe(false);
}};

test('ValidationUtils validateIsInteger tests', () => {
expect(validationUtils.validateIsInteger(0)).toBe(true);
expect(validationUtils.validateIsInteger(-65536)).toBe(true);
expect(validationUtils.validateIsInteger(65536)).toBe(true);

  expect(validationUtils.validateIsInteger('-65536')).toBe(true);
expect(validationUtils.validateIsInteger('65536')).toBe(true);

  //Invalid cases
expect(validationUtils.validateIsInteger(null)).toBe(false);
expect(validationUtils.validateIsInteger(undefined)).toBe(false);

  expect(validationUtils.validateIsInteger(-65536.12)).toBe(false);
expect(validationUtils.validateIsInteger(65536.12)).toBe(false);

  expect(validationUtils.validateIsInteger('-65536.12')).toBe(false);
expect(validationUtils.validateIsInteger('65536.12')).toBe(false);

  expect(validationUtils.validateIsInteger('-0.12')).toBe(false);
expect(validationUtils.validateIsInteger('0.12')).toBe(false);

  expect(validationUtils.validateIsInteger('')).toBe(false);
expect(validationUtils.validateIsInteger(' ')).toBe(false);

  expect(validationUtils.validateIsInteger('a')).toBe(false);

  expect(validationutils.validateIsInteger('o1')).toBe(false);
expect(validationUtils.validateIsInteger('-0')).toBe(false);
}};

### 579. `C:\DevCode\AstroUI\src\helpers\validation\ComboTextFieldValidationFactory.ts`

```typescript
import { validationUtils } from './ValidationUtils';

export default function createComboTextFieldValidators(
productTemplate: any,
context: any,
] {
// filter template sections that use pre-built components
const sectionsWithSettings = productTemplate.sections.filter(
(section: { settings: any }) => section.settings,
  ];

  for (const section of sectionsWithSettings) {
const comboFieldSettings = section.settings.filter(
(setting: any) => setting.type === 'combo-text-field',
    ];

    if (!comboFieldSettings || comboFieldSettings.length === 0) {
continue;
}

    comboFieldSettings.forEach((setting: any) => {
const firstSettingName = setting.firstSettingCode;
const firstValueType = setting.firstSettingType;

      const secondSettingName = setting.secondSettingCode;
const secondValueType = setting.secondSettingType;

      // adding configuration settings to context
context.addTemplateSetting(firstSettingName, setting);
context.addTemplateSetting(secondSettingName, setting);
context.addSectionReference(firstSettingName, section.sectionTitle);
context.addSectionReference(secondSettingName, section.sectionTitle);

      context.addSettingValidationListener(
firstSettingName,
(context: any, value: string) => {
const secondSettingValue = context.getSettingValue(secondSettingName);

          const requiredFieldsOk: boolean = validateRequiredFields(
context,
setting,
firstSettingName,
value,
secondSettingValue,
          };

          if (!requiredFieldsOk) {
return;
}

          if (secondSettingValue) {
evaluateValue(
context,
secondSettingName,
secondValueType,
secondSettingValue,
            };
}

          if (!value) {
context.updateError(firstSettingName, '');
return;
}

          evaluateValue(context, firstSettingName, firstValueType, value);
},
      ];

      context.addSettingValidationListener(
secondSettingName,
(context: any, value: any) => {
const firstSettingValue = context.getSettingValue(firstSettingName);

          const requiredFieldsOk: boolean = validateRequiredFields(
context,
setting,
firstSettingName,
firstSettingValue,
value,
          };

          if (!requiredFieldsOk) {
return;
}

          if (firstSettingValue) {
evaluateValue(
context,
firstSettingName,
firstValueType,
firstSettingValue,
            };
}

          if (!value) {
context.updateError(secondSettingName, '');
return;
}

          evaluateValue(context, secondSettingName, secondValueType, value);
},
      ];
    }};
}
}

const validateRequiredFields = (
context: any,
setting: any,
firstSettingName: string,
firstValue: string,
secondValue: string,
): boolean => {
if (setting.bothRequired && (!firstValue || !secondValue)) {
context.updateError(
firstSettingName,
'${setting.firstSettingDisplayLabel} and ${setting.secondSettingDisplayLabel} must be provided',
    ];
return false;
}

  if (setting.oneRequired && !firstValue && !secondValue) {
context.updateError(
firstSettingName,
'Either ${setting.firstSettingDisplayLabel} or $setting.secondSettingDisplayLabel} mustbe provided',
    ];
return false;
}

  context.updateError(firstSettingName, '');
return true;
};

const evaluateValue = (
context: any,
settingName: string,
valueType: string,
value: string,
): void => {
switch (valueType) {
case 'number':
context.updateError(
settingName,
validationUtils.validateIsNumber(value)
? ''
: 'Value is not a valid number',
      ];
break;
case 'integer':
context.updateError(
settingName,
validationUtils.validateIsInteger(value)
? ''
: 'Value is not a valid integer',
      ];
break;
case 'date':
context.updateError(
settingName,
validationUtils.validateIsDate(value, 'M/d/yyyy')
? ''
: 'Value is not a valid date',
      ];
break;
case 'url':
context.updateError(
settingName,
validationUtils.validateIsUrl(value) ? '' : 'Value is not a valid URL',
      ];
break;
default:
break;
}
};

import { CoreProductSettingTypes } from 'types';
import { settingValueResolver } from '../../components/CoreProduct/util/SettingValueResolver';

export default function createMultiSelectValidators(productTemplate, context) {
// filter template sections that use pre-built components
const sectionsWithSettings = productTemplate.sections.filter(
(section) => section.settings,
  ];

  let multiSelectSettings = [];
sectionsWithSettings.forEach((section) => {
const multiSelects = section.settings.filter(
({ type }) => type === CoreProductSettingTypes.MULTI_SELECT,
    ];
if (multiSelects.length === 0) return;
multiSelectSettings = multiSelectSettings.concat(multiSelects);
  }};

  const customInputPopulated = (value, delimiter) => {
if (!value) {
return false;
}

    const regex = new RegExp(delimiter, 'g');
const textWithoutDelimiters = value.replace(regex, '');

    return textWithoutDelimiters.trim() ? true : false;
};

  multiSelectSettings.forEach((setting) => {
// if a selection is NOT required, no validation needed
if (!setting.required) return;

    const settingCode = setting.settingCode;

    // check boolean value of ALL options, whenever one of them is changed
const optionIsSelected = (context, updatedSetting) => {
return setting.options.some((option) => {
const settingName = option.value;

        // the state of the updated checkbox is stale ... don't check
if (settingName === updatedSetting) return false;

        const isChecked = context.getSettingValue(settingName);

        return isChecked;
      }];
};

    setting.options.forEach((option) => {
const settingName = option.value;

      // add a validation listener that checks the boolean value of ALL options,
// and sets an error accordingly
context.addSettingValidationListener(
settingName,
(context, isChecked) => {
if (isChecked) {
context.updateError(settingCode, '');
return;
}

          if (setting.customInput) {
const customSettingCode =
settingValueResolver.getCustomInputSettingCode(setting);
const customInputDelimiter = setting.customInputDelimiter
? setting.customInputDelimiter
              ; `,`;
const customIsPopulated = customInputPopulated(
context.getSettingValue(customSettingCode),
customInputDelimiter,
            };

            if (customIsPopulated) {
context.updateError(settingCode, '');
return;
}
}

          // ... if an option was just unselected, check to see if at least 1 option is selected
if (!optionIsSelected(context, settingName)) {
context.updateError(settingCode, 'An option must be selected.');
return;
}

          context.updateError(settingCode, '');
},
      ];
    }};

    if (setting.customInput) {
const customSettingCode =
settingValueResolver.getCustomInputSettingCode(setting);
context.addSettingValidationListener(
customSettingCode,
(context, value) => {
const customInputDelimiter = setting.customInputDelimiter
? setting.customInputDelimiter
            ; `,`;
const customIsPopulated = customInputPopulated(
value,
customInputDelimiter,
          };

          if (customIsPopulated) {
context.updateError(settingCode, '');
return;
}

          // ... if an option was just unselected, check to see if at least 1 option is selected
if (!optionIsSelected(context, '')) {
context.updateError(settingCode, 'An option must be selected.');
return;
}

          context.updateError(settingCode, '');
},
      ];
}
  }};
}

### 581. `C:\DevCode\AstroUI\src\helpers\validation\numeric-comparison-factory.js`

<!-- CODESCAN_FILE_START path="C:\DevCode\AstroUI\src\helpers\validation\numeric-comparison-factory.js" type=".js" size="2267" lines="92" chars="2267" -->
```javascript
const operationMap = {
'<': (x, y) => x < y,
'>': (x, y) => x > y,
'=': (x, y) => x === y,
'<=': (x, y) => x <= y,
'>=': (x, y) => x >= y,
};

class NumericComparisonRuleFactory {
createValidator = (setting, rule, context) => {
const settingName = setting.settingCode;
const definition = rule.definition;

    const operation = operationMap[definition.operator];
if (!operation) {
return;
}

    const otherSetting = definition.otherSetting;
if (!otherSetting) {
return;
}

    const violationMessage = definition.violationMessage;
if (!violationMessage) {
return;
}

    context.addSettingValidationListener(settingName, (context, value) => {
const v1 = extractNumber(value);
const v2 = extractNumber(context.getSettingValue(otherSetting));

      if (!v1 || !v2) {
return;
}

      const violationExists = !operation(v1, v2);

      if (!violationExists) {
return;
}

      context.updateError(settingName, violationMessage);
    }};

    // add it to both settings in the comparison, so that this rule is run
// if either of the setting values change
context.addSettingValidationListener(otherSetting, (context, value) => {
const v1 = extractNumber(context.getSettingValue(settingName));
const v2 = extractNumber(value);

      if (!v1 || !v2) {
// if the values cannot be evaluated, then reset any previous error state
if (context.getErrorState(settingName) === violationMessage) {
context.updateError(settingName, '');
}
return;
}

      const violationExists = !operation(v1, v2);

      if (!violationExists) {
if (context.getErrorState(settingName) === violationMessage) {
context.updateError(settingName, '');
}
return;
}

      context.updateError(settingName, violationMessage);
    }};
};
}

const extractNumber = (value) => {
if (!value) {
return null;
}

  if (typeof value === 'number') {
return value;
}

  if (typeof value === 'string' && !isNaN(value)) {
return Number(value);
}

  return null;
};

const numericComparisonRuleFactory = new NumericComparisonRuleFactory();
export default numericComparisonRuleFactory;

```

```typescript
import {
emptyArrayCheck,
isEndDateInPast,
isValidDate,
isValidTwoDecimalNumber,
isValidYear,
maxLengthCheck,
} from 'helpers/validations';
import { DateTime } from 'luxon';
import FormValidator from 'utils/FormValidator';
import { regexUtils } from '../../utils/RegexUtils';
import {
RATES_TYPE_AUTO,
RATES_TYPE_CARD,
RATES_TYPE_NON_AUTO,
RATES_TYPE_PERSONAL_LINE_OF_CREDIT,
RATES_TYPE_PERSONAL_LOANS,
RATES_TYPE_PERSONAL_UNSECURED,
} from '../../components/CoreProduct/constants/RateTypes';
import { AUTO_RATE_FIELDS } from '../../components/CoreProduct/Rates/constants/AutoRateFields';
import { CARD_RATE_FIELDS } from '../../components/CoreProduct/Rates/constants/CardRateFields';
import { Rates } from '../../components/CoreProduct/Rates/types/RateTypes';
import { indexOfCreditRangeGap } from '../../components/CoreProduct/Rates/utils/plocGapUtils';
import { decimalUtils } from '../../components/CoreProduct/util/DecimalUtils';

const categoryVariables = [
  'creditRangeRateo',
'creditRangeRate1',
'creditRangeRate2',
'creditRangeRate3',
'creditRangeRate4',
'creditRangeRate5',
'creditRangeRate6',
'creditRangeRate7',
'creditRangeRate8',
'creditRangeRate9',
];

export default function validateRates(
rates: Rates[],
ratesType: string,
requestedState: string,
fieldErrorState: any,
setErrors: any,
): void {
let errors = []; // accumulate errors

  if (
ratesType !== RATES_TYPE_CARD &&
rates &&
rates.some(
      [>} =>
        !r !
        !r.valuesJSON II
        !r.valuesJSON.rateTables Il
r.valuesJSON.rateTables.length === 0,
    ]
  ] {
errors.push('- Missing data for at least 1 rate set');
}

  if (ratesType === RATES_TYPE_PERSONAL_LINE_OF_CREDIT && rates) {
rates.some((r) => {
const tables = r?.valuesJSON?.rateTables || [];
if (tables.some((t) => t.creditRanges.length === 0)) {
errors.push('- Missing balance ranges for at least one rate set');
}
    }};

    rates.forEach((r) => {
const tables = r?.valuesJSON?.rateTables || [];
tables.forEach((t) => {
if (t.isSingleValue) return;
const ranges = t.creditRanges.map((cr) => ({
low: decimalUtils.toDec(cr.low),
high: decimalUtils.toDec(cr.high),
        }]};
if (indexOfCreditRangeGap(t.creditRanges) !== undefined) {
errors.push(
`${t.rateTableName}: Gaps are present between balance ranges`,
          };
}
      }];
    }};
}

  const hasInvalidData = checkForInvalidData(fieldErrorState);
if (hasInvalidData && ratesType !== RATES_TYPE_CARD) {
errors.push('- Fix the highlighted errors.');
}

  const missingDataErrors = checkForMissingData(ratesType, rates);
if (missingDataErrors.length > 0) {
errors = errors.concat(missingDataErrors);
}

  const hasGreaterMaxCreditThanMin = checkForMaxCreditGreaterThanMin(rates);
if (hasGreaterMaxCreditThanMin) {
errors.push(
'- Max Credit Limit(s) must be greater than the Min Credit Limit(s).',
    ];
}

  if (
ratesType === RATES_TYPE_AUTO ||
ratesType === RATES_TYPE_NON_AUTO ||
ratesType === RATES_TYPE_PERSONAL_UNSECURED
  ] {
checkRateTypeDefaultIsSet(rates, errors);
}

  const dateRanges = rates.map((rateSet) => {
return { startDate: rateSet.startDate, endDate: rateSet.endDate };
  }};

  const emptyEndDates = dateRanges.filter(
(range) => !range.endDate || range.endDate === '',
  ];

  if (ratesType !== RATES_TYPE_CARD && emptyEndDates.length > 1) {
// ... only one rates set can have an unspecified end date
errors.push('- Only 1 rate set can have an unspecified end date.');
}

  checkForRangeOverlaps(dateRanges, errors);

  if (requestedState === 'Active') {
checkForActiveDateRange(
dateRanges,
errors,
'- At least 1 rate set must include the current date in its range.',
    ];
} else {
checkForActiveOrPendingDateRange(dateRanges, errors);
}

  if (ratesType === RATES_TYPE_CARD) {
checkCCRateTableDateRanges(rates, errors);
}

  setErrors(errors);
}

const checkRateTypeDefaultIsSet = (rates: Rates[], errors: string[]) => {
rates.forEach((rate) => {
const tables = rate.valuesJSON?.rateTables ?? [];
tables.forEach((table) => {
const { terms } = table;
const defaults = terms.filter((t) => t.isDefault);
if (defaults.length < 1)
errors.push(
'Rate Set ${rate.displayOrder}, Table ${table.displayorder}: No default term length set',
        };
// if (defaults.length > 1) errors.push('Rate Set ${rate.id), Table${table.displayorder}: Can only choose one default term length');
    }};
  }};
};

const checkCCRateTableDateRanges = (rates: Rates[], errors: string[]) => {
rates.forEach((rate) => {
if (!rate.rateTables) {
return;
}
const rtRanges = rate.rateTables.map((rt) => {
return { startDate: rt.startDate || '', endDate: rt.endDate || '' };
    }};

    const emptyEndDates = rtRanges.filter(
(range) => !range.endDate || range.endDate === '',
    ];

    if (emptyEndDates.length > 1) {
// ... only one rates set can have an unspecified end date
errors.push('- Only 1 card type table can have an unspecified end date.');
}

    checkForActiveDateRange(
rtRanges,
errors,
'- At least 1 table for each card type must include the current date in its range.',
    ];

    checkForRangeOverlaps(rtRanges, errors, 'Card Type Table');
  }};
};

function checkForActiveDateRange(
dateRanges: {
startDate: string;
endDate: string;
}[],
errors: any,
errorText: string,
): void {
// ... if the product is being saved in the Active state, at least
// 1 set of rates must include the current date

  const currentTime = DateTime.now().toUnixInteger();

  const rateSetIsActive = dateRanges.some((range) => {
const sd1 = DateTime.fromFormat(
range.startDate,
'M/d/yyyy',
).toUnixInteger();

    const endDate1Valid = range.endDate && range.endDate !== '';
// end date runs through date specified - bump by (24 hrs in seconds)-1
const ed1 = endDate1Valid
? DateTime.fromFormat(range.endDate, 'M/d/yyyy').toUnixInteger() + 86399
: Number.MAX_VALUE;

    return currentTime >= sd1 && currentTime < ed1;
  }};

  if (!rateSetIsActive) {
errors.push(errorText);
}
}

function checkForActiveOrPendingDateRange(
dateRanges: {
startDate: string;
endDate: string;
}[],
errors?: any,
): void {
// at least 1 set of rates must be pending or active

  const currentTime = DateTime.now().toUnixInteger();

  const rateSetIsActiveOrPending = dateRanges.some((range) => {
const sd1 = DateTime.fromFormat(
range.startDate,
'M/d/yyyy',
).toUnixInteger();

    const endDate1Valid = range.endDate && range.endDate !== '';
const ed1 = endDate1Valid
? DateTime.fromFormat(range.endDate, 'M/d/yyyy').toUnixInteger() + 86399
: Number.MAX_VALUE;

    return currentTime <= sd1 || (currentTime >= sd1 && currentTime < ed1);
  }};

  if (!rateSetIsActiveOrPending) {
errors.push(
'- At least 1 rate set must be currently active or active in the future.',
    ];
}
}

function checkForRangeOverlaps(
dateRanges: {
startDate: string;
endDate: string;
}[],
errors: any,
errorTitle?: string,
): void {
const overlapsSet = new Set();
const currentDate = DateTime.now();
errorTitle = errorTitle ? errorTitle + ': ' : '';

  dateRanges.forEach((range1, idx1) => {
const sd1 = DateTime.fromFormat(
range1.startDate,
'M/d/yyyy',
).toUnixInteger();

    const endDate1Valid = range1.endDate && range1.endDate !== '';
const ed1 = endDate1Valid
? DateTime.fromFormat(range1.endDate, 'M/d/yyyy').toUnixInteger() + 86399
: Number.MAX_VALUE;

    if (
currentDate.startOf('day') >
DateTime.fromFormat(range1.endDate, 'M/d/yyyy').startOf('day')
    ] {
// ... end date is before the current day's date
errors.push(
`- ${
errorTitle + ': '
}End date cannot be in past => (current date: ${currentDate.toFormat(
'M/d/yyyy',
)}, end date: ${range1.endDate})`,
      ];
return;
}

    if (sd1 >= ed1) {
// ... start date is >= end date
errors.push(
'- ${errorTitle}start date is later than the end date => (start date: ${range1.startDate}, end date: ${range1.endDate})',
      ];
return;
}

    dateRanges.forEach((range2, idx2) => {
if (idx1 === idx2) return;
if (!range2.endDate || range2.endDate === '') return;

      if (overlapsSet.has(idx1 + ',' + idx2)) {
return;
}

      const sd2 = DateTime.fromFormat(
range2.startDate,
'M/d/yyyy',
).toUnixInteger();

      const endDate2Valid = range2.endDate && range2.endDate !== '';
const ed2 = endDate2Valid
? DateTime.fromFormat(range2.endDate, 'M/d/yyyy').toUnixInteger() +
86399
: Number.MAX_VALUE;

      if (Math.max(sd1, sd2) <= Math.min(ed1, ed2)) {
errors.push(
`- ${errorTitle}Date ranges overlap: (${range1.startDate}, ${
endDate1Valid ? range1.endDate : 'Indefinite'
}) and (${range2.startDate}, ${
endDate2Valid ? range2.endDate : 'Indefinite'
})`,
        };
overlapsSet.add(idx1 + ',' + idx2);
overlapsSet.add(idx2 + ',' + idx1);
}
    }};
  }};
}

export function checkFieldsForMissingData(

): { [key: string]: { isInvalid: boolean; message: string } } {
const fieldsWithMissingData: {
[key: string]: { isInvalid: boolean; message: string };
} = {};

  [>>

: ratesType === RATES_TYPE_CARD
? CARD_RATE_FIELDS
: CARD_RATE_FIELDS;

(Array.isArray(value) && value.length === 0 && key !== 'dlxPurpose')
      ] {

// determine if any match the field values input

};

      ] {

};

key === 'minMonthlyPaymentDollarsString' &&

      ] {

] = {

const checkForMissingDataCC = (rateSetsAsArray: Rates[]): string[] => {
const errors: string[] = [];
rateSetsAsArray.forEach((rateSet, i) => {
const cardName = !rateSet.dlxCardName
? `Unnamed Card, Position ${i + 1}`
: rateSet.dlxCardName;
const errorFields: string[] = [];
if (!rateSet.dlxCardName) {
errorFields.push('dlxCardName');
}
if (!rateSet.productDisplayName) {
errorFields.push('productDisplayName');
}
if (!rateSet.losCardName) {
errorFields.push('losCardName');
}
if (!rateSet.startDate) {
errorFields.push('startDate');
}
if (!rateSet.cardNetwork) {
errorFields.push('cardNetwork');
}
if (
!rateSet.minMonthlyPaymentDollarsString &&
!rateSet.minMonthlyPaymentPercentString
    ] {
errorFields.push('minMonthlyPaymentDollarsString');
}
if (!rateSet.minCreditLimitString) {
errorFields.push('minCreditLimit');
}
if (!rateSet.annualFeeString) {
errorFields.push('annualFee');
}
if (!rateSet.cardDescription) {
errorFields.push('cardDescription');
}
if (!rateSet.cardDetails) {
errorFields.push('cardDetails');
}
if (!rateSet.source) {
errorFields.push('source');
}
if (!rateSet.externalLinkKey) {
errorFields.push('externalLinkKey');
}
if (!rateSet.applicationDisclosure) {
errorFields.push('applicationDisclosure');
}
if (rateSet.dlxPurpose?.length < 1 && !rateSet.dlxCustomPurpose) {
errorFields.push('dlxPurpose');
}
if (!rateSet.losPurpose) {
errorFields.push('losPurpose');
}
if (rateSet.discounted === true && !rateSet.rateDisclaimerText) {
errorFields.push('rateDisclaimer');
}

    if (errorFields.length > 0) {
const errorFieldLabels = errorFields.map((ef) => {
const field = CARD_RATE_FIELDS.find((crf) => crf.value === ef);
return field?.label ?? ef;
      }];
errors.push(
`${cardName}: Required Fields Missing - ${errorFieldLabels.join(', ')}`,
      ];
}
  }};
return errors;
};

function checkForMissingData(
ratesType: string,
rateSetsAsArray: Rates[],
): string[] {
let result = false;
const defaultError = ['- Required field(s) highlighted missing.'];

  if (ratesType === RATES_TYPE_CARD) {
return checkForMissingDataCC(rateSetsAsArray);
}

  for (const rates of rateSetsAsArray) {
if (
ratesType === RATES_TYPE_AUTO &&
rates.valuesJSON &&
rates.valuesJSON.rateTables
    ] {
if (!rates.startDate) return defaultError;

      result = rates.valuesJSON.rateTables.some((rateTable) => {
if (!rateTable.modelYearStart) return defaultError;
if (!rateTable.modelYearEnd && !rateTable.andOlder) return defaultError;
return rateTable.terms.some((term) => {
return categoryVariables.some((category) => {
const value = term[category];
if (value === null || value === '') {
return defaultError;
}
          }];
        }];
      }];
if (result) return defaultError;
}

    if (
ratesType === RATES_TYPE_PERSONAL_LOANS &&
rates.valuesJSON &&
rates.valuesJSON.rateTables
    ] {
if (!rates.startDate) return defaultError;
if (
!rates.valuesJSON.discountDescription &&
rates.valuesJSON.discountedApr
      ]
return defaultError;
result = rates.valuesJSON.rateTables.some((rateTable) => {
return rateTable.terms.some((term) => {
return categoryVariables.some((category) => {
const value = term[category];
if (value === null || value === '') {
return defaultError;
}
          }];
        }];
      }];
if (result) return defaultError;
}

    if (ratesType === RATES_TYPE_PERSONAL_LINE_OF_CREDIT && rates.valuesJSON) {
if (!rates.startDate) return defaultError;
if (
!rates.valuesJSON.discountDescription &&
rates.valuesJSON.discountedApr
      ] {
return defaultError;
}
result = rates.valuesJSON.rateTables.some((rateTable) => {
return rateTable.creditRanges.some((creditRange) => {
return categoryVariables.some((category) => {
const value = creditRange[category];
if (value === null || value === '') {
return defaultError;
}
          }];
        }];
      }];
if (result) return defaultError;
}
}
return [];
}

function checkForInvalidData(fieldErrorState: {
[key: string]: { isInvalid: boolean; message: string };
}): boolean {
let foundInvalidData = false;
for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(fieldErrorState)) {
if (value.isInvalid && value.message !== 'Required')
foundInvalidData = true;
}

  return foundInvalidData;
}

function checkForMaxCreditGreaterThanMin(rateSetsAsArray: Rates[]): boolean {
return rateSetsAsArray.some((rates) => {
if (
!isNaN(rates.maxCreditLimit) &&
rates.maxCreditLimit <= rates.minCreditLimit
    ]
return true;
  }};
}

```javascript
import { CoreProductSettingTypes } from 'types';

export default function createTextLengthValidators(productTemplate, context) {
// filter template sections that use pre-built components
const sectionsWithSettings = productTemplate?.sections?.filter(
(section) => section.settings,
  ];

  let maxLengthSettings = [];
sectionsWithSettings?.forEach((section) => {
const targetSettings = section.settings.filter(
(setting) =>
(setting.type === CoreProductSettingTypes.TEXT_ENTRY ||
setting.type === CoreProductSettingTypes.TEXTBOX) &&
setting.maxLength &&
typeof setting.maxLength === 'number',
    ];
if (targetSettings.length === 0) return;
maxLengthSettings = maxLengthSettings.concat(targetSettings);
  }};

  maxLengthSettings.forEach((setting) => {
const settingName = setting.settingCode;
const maxLength = setting.maxLength;

    context.addSettingValidationListener(settingName, (context, value) => {
if (!value || value.length <= maxLength) {
return;
}

      context.updateError(
settingName,
'Value cannot be more than ' + maxLength + ' characters.',
      ];
    }};
  }};
}

```

import { CoreProductSettingTypes, CoreProductSettingValueTypes } from 'types';
import { validationUtils } from './ValidationUtils';

// TODO -> this should create functions that respond with an error message.
// The current implementation adds the validation listeners directly. Ideally,
// the validator-configuration function should do that.
export default function createTypeValidators(productTemplate, context) {
// filter template sections that use pre-built components
const sectionsWithSettings = productTemplate.sections?.filter(
(section) => section.settings,
  ];

  let textBasedSettings = [];
sectionsWithSettings.forEach((section) => {
let textSettings = section.settings.filter(
({ type, settingCode }) =>
settingCode &&
(type === CoreProductSettingTypes.TEXT_ENTRY ||
type === CoreProductSettingTypes.TEXTBOX),
    ];
section.settings.forEach((Collsection) => {
if (Collsection.collectionSettings) {
const colltextSettings = Collsection.collectionSettings.filter(
({ type, settingCode }) =>
settingCode &&
(type === CoreProductSettingTypes.TEXT_ENTRY ||
type === CoreProductSettingTypes.TEXTBOX),
        };
textSettings = textSettings.concat(colltextSettings);
}
    }};

    if (textSettings.length === 0) return;
textBasedSettings = textBasedSettings.concat(textSettings);
  }};

  textBasedSettings.forEach((setting) => {
const settingName = setting.settingCode;
const valueType = setting.valueType;
const settingRequired = setting.required;

    context.addSettingValidationListener(settingName, (context, value) => {
if (!value || value === '') {
context.updateError(
settingName,
settingRequired ? `${setting.label} is a required field` : '',
        };
return;
}

      if (valueType === CoreProductSettingValueTypes.NUMBER) {
context.updateError(
settingName,
validationUtils.validateIsNumber(value)
            ] ` `
: 'Value is not a valid number',
        };
return;
}

      if (valueType === CoreProductSettingValueTypes.INTEGER) {
context.updateError(
settingName,
validationUtils.validateIsInteger(value)
            ] ` `
: 'Value is not a valid integer',
        };
return;
}

      if (valueType === CoreProductSettingValueTypes.DATE) {
context.updateError(
settingName,
validationUtils.validateIsDate(value, 'M/d/yyyy')
            ] ` `
: 'Value is not a valid date',
        };
}

      if (valueType === CoreProductSettingValueTypes.URL) {
context.updateError(
settingName,
validationUtils.validateIsUrl(value)
            ] ` `
: 'Value is not a valid URL',
        };
}
    }};
  }};
}

```javascript
import numericComparisonRuleFactory from './numeric-comparison-factory';

const factories = {
'numeric comparison': numericComparisonRuleFactory,
};

export default function configureUserRules(productTemplate, context) {
// filter template sections that use pre-built components
const sectionsWithSettings = productTemplate.sections.filter(
(section) => section.settings,
  ];

  sectionsWithSettings.forEach((section) => {
section.settings.forEach((setting) => {
if (!setting.rules) {
return;
}

      setting.rules.forEach((rule) => {
if (!rule.type) {
return;
}

        const factory = factories[rule.type];

        if (!factory) {
return;
}

        factory.createValidator(setting, rule, context);
      }];
    }};
  }};
}

```

```typescript
import { DateTime } from 'luxon';
import { isValidURL } from 'helpers/validations';

class ValidationUtils {
validateIsNumber = (value: any) => {
return (
!isNaN(value) &&
!isNaN(parseFloat(value)) &&
parseFloat(value).toString() === value.toString()
    ];
};

  validateIsInteger = (value: any) => {
if (isNaN(value)) return false;

    if (typeof value === 'number') {
return Number.isInteger(value);
}

    if (typeof value !== 'string') return false;

    if (/^-?\d+$/.test(value) == false) return false;

    return Number(value).toString() === value.toString();
};

  validateIsDate = (value: any, dateFormat: string) => {
if (typeof value !== 'string') return false;
return DateTime.fromFormat(value, dateFormat, {
locale: 'en',
}).isValid;
};

  validateIsUrl = isValidURL;
}

export const validationUtils = new ValidationUtils();

```

```javascript
import createTypeValidators from './type-validation-factory';
import createMultiSelectValidators from './multi-select-validation-factory';
import createTextLengthValidators from './text-length-validation-factory';
import configureUserRules from './user-rules-configuration';
import createComboTextFieldValidators from './ComboTextFieldValidationFactory';

const defaultProductTemplate = { sections: [] };

export default function configureValidators(
productTemplate = defaultProductTemplate,
productConfig,
productFormContext,
] {
const productTemplateWithDefault = {
...defaultProductTemplate,
...productTemplate,
};
createTypeValidators(productTemplateWithDefault, productFormContext);
createTextLengthValidators(productTemplateWithDefault, productFormContext);
createMultiSelectValidators(productTemplateWithDefault, productFormContext);
configureUserRules(productTemplateWithDefault, productFormContext);
createComboTextFieldValidators(
productTemplateWithDefault,
productFormContext,
  ];
}
