import {
isValidTwoDecimalNumber,
isValidDate,
isValidYear,
isValidURL,
} from '../validations';

describe('validations', () => {
describe('isValidDate', () => {
it('should return valid', async () => {
const isValid = await isValidDate('10/03/2021');
expect(isValid).toEqual(true);
    }};
it('should return valid for empty string', async () => {
const isValid = await isValidDate('');
expect(isValid).toEqual(true);
    }};
it('should return invalid', async () => {
const isValid = await isValidDate('invalid date');
expect(isValid).toEqual(false);
    }};
  }};
describe('isValidTwoDecimalNumber', () => {
it('should return valid', async () => {
const isValid = await isValidTwoDecimalNumber('2.12');
expect(isValid).toEqual(true);
    }};
it('should return valid for empty string', async () => {
const isValid = await isValidTwoDecimalNumber('');
expect(isValid).toEqual(true);
    }};
it('should return invalid', async () => {
const isValid = await isValidTwoDecimalNumber('2.1234');
expect(isValid).toEqual(false);
    }};
  }};
describe('isValidYear', () => {
it('should return valid', async () => {
const isValid = await isValidYear('2021');
expect(isValid).toEqual(true);
    }};
it('should return valid for empty string', async () => {
const isValid = await isValidYear('');
expect(isValid).toEqual(true);
    }};
it('should return invalid', async () => {
const isValid = await isValidYear('invalidYear');
expect(isValid).toEqual(false);
    }};
  }};
describe('URL validation', () => {
it('should return true for a valid url', () => {
expect(isValidURL('https://www.google.com')).toBe(true);
    }};
it('should return true for a url without a protocol', () => {
expect(isValidURL('www.google.com')).toBe(true);
    }};
it('should return false for an invalid url', () => {
expect(isValidURL('google')).toBe(false);
    }};
it('should return false for an invalid type', () => {
expect(isValidURL(12)).toBe(false);
    }};
  }};
}};

```javascript
import React from 'react';
import Address from './address';

const getPhysicalAddressOneLine = (addresses) => {
const address = addresses.find((x) => x.addressType === 'Physical');

  if (!isValidAddress(address)) {
return '';
}

  let rval = address
? `${address.line1}${
address.line2 && address.line2 !== '' ? `, ${address.line2}` : ''
}, ${address.city} ${address.state} ${address.postalCode}`
    ; ` `;
rval = rval.replace(', ,', '');
return rval;
};

const getFormattedAddresses = (addresses, classes) => {
if (!addresses) {
return <div></div>;
}

  const pa = addresses.find((x) => x.addressType === 'Physical');
const ma = addresses.find((x) => x.addressType === 'Mailing');
const temp = [];

  if (isValidAddress(pa)) {
temp.push(
<Address key="pa" address={pa} type="Physical" classes={classes} />,
    ];
}
if (isValidAddress(ma)) {
temp.push(
<Address key="ma" address={ma} type="Mailing" classes={classes} />,
    ];
}

  return <div style={{ paddingLeft: '24px' }}>{temp}</div>;
};

const isValidAddress = (address) => {
if (!address) return false;

  if (!address.line1 || address.line1 === '') return false;
if (!address.city || address.city === '') return false;
if (!address.state || address.state === '') return false;
if (!address.postalCode || address.postalCode === '') return false;

  return true;
};

const addressSame = (add1, add2) => {
return (
add1.line1 === add2.line1 &&
add1.line2 === add2.line2 &&
add1.line3 === add2.line3 &&
add1.city === add2.city &&
add1.county === add2.county &&
add1.state === add2.state &&
add1.postalCode === add2.postalCode &&
add1.country === add2.country
  ];
};

const copyAddresses = (data) => {
data.Mailing_line1 = data.Physical_line1;
data.Mailing_line2 = data.Physical_line2;
data.Mailing_line3 = data.Physical_line3;
data.Mailing_city = data.Physical_city;
data.Mailing_county = data.Physical_county;
data.Mailing_state = data.Physical_state;
data.Mailing_postalCode = data.Physical_postalCode;
data.Mailing_country = data.Physical_country;
return data;
};

export {
getPhysicalAddressOneLine,
getFormattedAddresses,
addressSame,
copyAddresses,
};

segment.fieldGroups,
{ ...externalFormData, ...formData },
  ];

  const validate = (
currentFormData: Record<string, string>,
): Record<string, string> => {
const groups = applyPresentationRulesToGroupsAndFilterHiddenGroups(
segment.fieldGroups,
{ ...externalFormData, ...currentFormData },
    ];

    const validations = validateFieldGroups(
groups,
{ ...externalFormData, ...currentFormData },
t,
    ];

    setErrors(validations);

    return validations;
};

  useEffect(() => {
if (validateAllFields) {
validate({ ...formData, ...externalFormData });
}
}, [formData, validateAllFields]);

  const updateSegment = async (): Promise<void> => {
const updatedSegment = await applyDynamicFieldHandlers(
{ tenantId: tenant?.id as string },
segment as Core.LoanApplicationEntities.ISectionSegment,
    ];
setSegment(updatedSegment);
setLoading(false);
};

  useEffect(() => {
updateSegment();
}, []);

  useEffect(() => {
if (updateFieldGroups) {
setSegment({
fieldGroups: updateFieldGroups(formData),
      }];
}
}, [formData]);

  return {
errors,
fieldGroups: filteredGroups,
formData,
loading,
setErrors,
setFormData,
setLoading,
setValidateAllFields,
validate,
validateAllFields,
};
};

export { useDynamicSegment };

```typescript
import { DateTime } from 'luxon';
import * as yup from 'yup';

export const isEmptyString = yup.string().length(0);
export const isNumericOnly = yup.string().matches(/^(0|[1-9][0-9]*)$/);
export const isYearNumericOnly = yup.string().matches(/^\d{4}$/);
export const isTwoDecmialNumber = yup.string().matches(/^\d+(?:\.\d{2})$/);
export const isInteger = yup.number().integer();
export const isArrayEmpty = yup.array().min(1);
export const maxLengthChecker: (
maxLength: number,
) => yup.StringSchema<string | undefined> = (maxLength: number) =>
yup.string().max(maxLength);

export const isValidTwoDecimalNumber: (
input: string,
) => Promise<boolean> = async (input: string) => {
const result = await isTwoDecmialNumber.isValid(input);
// if input is an empty string, return valid
return !input ? true : result;
};

export const isValidDate: (input: string) => Promise<boolean> = async (
input: string,
) =>
(await isEmptyString.isValid(input))
? true
: DateTime.fromFormat(input, 'MM/dd/yyyy').isValid;

export const isValidYear: (input: string) => Promise<boolean> = async (
input: string,
) =>
(await isEmptyString.isValid(input))
? true
: await isYearNumericOnly.isValid(input);

export const isEndDateInPast: (input: string) => Promise<boolean> = async (
input: string,
) => {
const date = DateTime.fromFormat(input, 'M/d/yyyy');

  return DateTime.now().startOf('day') <= date.startOf('day');
};

export const zipNumericOnlyNonEmpty: (
input: string,
) => Promise<boolean> = async (input: string) =>
(await isEmptyString.isValid(input))
? true
: await isNumericOnly.isValid(input);

export const isEmptyStringCheck: (input: string) => Promise<boolean> = async (
input: string,
) => await isEmptyString.isValid(input);

export const maxLengthCheck: (
input: string,
maxLength: number,
) => Promise<boolean> = async (input: string, maxLength: number) =>
await maxLengthChecker(maxLength).isValid(input);

export const emptyArrayCheck: (
input: string,
optionalMultiValuesArg: string[],
) => Promise<boolean> = async (
input: string,
optionalMultiValuesArg: string[],
) => {
const result = await isArrayEmpty.isValid(optionalMultiValuesArg);
return result;
};

[>>
* Copied from https://urlregex.com/
>[
const URL_REGEX =
/((([A-Za-z]3,9}:(?:\/\/)?)(?:[1-; :&=\+\$, Iw]+@)?[A-Za-z0-9\.1-]+I(?:www\.I[\-;:&=\+\$, Iw]+@)[A-Za-z0-9\.1-]+)((?:\/[\+~%\/\.Iw\-_]*)?\??(?:[\-\+=&;%@1. Iw_]*)#?(?:[1.1!\/\1lw]*))?)/;

export const isValidURL = (value: any) => {
if (typeof value !== 'string') return false;
return URL_REGEX.test(value);
};

### 595. `C:\DevCode\AstroUI\src\i18n\index.ts`

<!-- CODESCAN_FILE_START path="C:\DevCode\AstroUI\src\i18n\index.ts" type=".ts" size="1045" lines="42" chars="1045" -->
```typescript
/* eslint-disable @typescript-eslint/ban-types */
import { useCallback } from 'react';
import i18n from 'i18next';
import { initReactI18next, useTranslation } from 'react-i18next';
import { i18n as commonUIi18n } from 'commonui';
import { translation } from './en';

const translations = { ...commonUIi18n.translation, ...translation };
export type TranslationType = keyof typeof translations;

i18n.use(initReactI18next).init({
resources: {
en: {
translation: translations,
},
},
lng: 'en',
keySeparator: false,
}};

interface IUseTypedTranslation {
t: (ns: TranslationType, options?: object) => string;
}

const useTypedTranslation = (): IUseTypedTranslation => {
const { t } = useTranslation();
const newT = useCallback(
(ns: TranslationType, options?: object): string => t(ns, options),
[t],
  ];
return {
t: newT,
};
};

const nonHookTypedTranslation = (
ns: TranslationType,
options?: object,
): string => i18n.t(ns, options);

export { i18n, useTypedTranslation, nonHookTypedTranslation };

```

```typescript
/* eslint react/display-name:0 */
import { render } from '@testing-library/react';
import React from 'react';
import { MemoryRouter } from 'react-router-dom';
import { useRoutes } from '../routes';
import { AppContext } from 'context/app-context';

jest.mock('utils/launchDarkly');

const TestContainer: React.FC = ({}) => {
const routes = useRoutes();
return <div>{JSON.stringify(routes, null, 2)}</div>;
};

describe('routes hook', () => {
const mockTenant = {
recordState: 'Active',
id: 'testId',
};

  const mockAppContext = {
setTenant: jest.fn(),
};

  const wrapper =
(tenant: Record<string, any> | undefined) =>
({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }): JSX.Element => {
const tenantContext = {
...mockAppContext,
tenant: tenant || null,
};

      return (
<AppContext.Provider value={tenantContext as any}>
<MemoryRouter
initialEntries={[`/admin/tenant/testId/map-loan-templates`]}
>
{children}
</MemoryRouter>
</AppContext.Provider>
      ];
};

  beforeEach(() => {
jest.spyOn(window.sessionStorage.__proto__, 'setItem').mockImplementation();
  }};

  it('Should return tenant routes enabled', () => {
const { container } = render(<TestContainer />, {
wrapper: wrapper(mockTenant),
    }};
expect(container).toMatchSnapshot();
  }};
it('Should return tenant routes hidden', () => {
const { container } = render(<TestContainer />, {
wrapper: wrapper(undefined),
    }};
expect(container).toMatchSnapshot();
  }};
}};

```

```typescript
import React from 'react';
import AdminLayout from 'layouts/Admin.jsx';
import {
Route,
RouterProvider,
Routes,
createBrowserRouter,
} from 'react-router-dom';
import { useRoutes } from './routes';

import LoginPage from 'views/Pages/LoginPage';
import Home from 'components/Home/Home';
import { PingAuthHandler } from 'views/Pages/PingAuthHandler';
import 'assets/scss/material-dashboard-pro-react.scss?v=1.8.0';
import { TenantContainer } from 'layouts/TenantContainer';
import { HomeContainer } from 'layouts/HomeContainer';

[>>
>
* @todo Update react router and adopt modern nested routes
>[
const RouterSwitch = (): JSX.Element => {
const { routes, decisionRoutes } = useRoutes();
const allRoutes = [...routes, ...decisionRoutes];

  const homeRoutes = allRoutes.filter(({ isHomeRoute }) => isHomeRoute);
const otherRoutes = allRoutes.filter(({ isHomeRoute }) => !isHomeRoute);

  return (
<Routes>
<Route path="/" Component={LoginPage} />
{/* callback path for Ping Identity authentication */}
<Route path="/auth" Component={PingAuthHandler} />
<Route path="/admin" Component={AdminLayout}>
<Route path="/admin/home" Component={Home} />
{homeRoutes.map(({ path, component: Component, props }) => (
<Route
path={path}
Component={() => (
<HomeContainer>
<Component {...props} />
</HomeContainer>
            }}
key={path}
          [>
        }]}
{otherRoutes.map(
({ path, component: Component, useTenantContainer, props }) => (
<Route
path={path}
Component={() => (
<TenantContainer useTenantContainer={useTenantContainer}>
<Component {...props} />
</TenantContainer>
              }}
key={path}
/>
          },
        }}
</Route>
</Routes>
  ];
};

export { RouterSwitch };

```

icon: '',
component: Decisioning,
hidden: false,
isTenantRoute: true,
useTenantContainer: true,
},
];

export const useRoutes = (): {
routes: DashRoutes[];
decisionRoutes: DashRoutes[];
} => {
const decisioningActive = useGetFeatureFlags('DECISIONING');
const [decisionRoutes, setDecisionRoutes] = useState<DashRoutes[]>([]);

  useEffect(() => {
if (decisioningActive) {
setDecisionRoutes(decisioningRoutes);
}
}, [decisioningActive]);

  return {
routes: baseDashRoutes,
decisionRoutes,
};
};

```typescript
import React from 'react';
import { AppContext } from 'context/app-context';
import { render, screen, waitFor } from '@testing-library/react';
import { TenantContainer } from '../TenantContainer';

jest.mock('react-router-dom', () => ({
...jest.requireActual('react-router-dom'),
useParams: () => ({ tenantId: 'tenantId' }),
}}];

describe('TenantContainer', () => {
const tenantId = 'tenantId';
const props = {
useTenantContainer: true,
};
it('should render children directly if not enabled', () => {
render(<TenantContainer useTenantContainer={false}>Test</TenantContainer>);
expect(screen.queryByText('Test')).toBeInTheDocument();
  }};
it('should not load tenant if already in context', () => {
const loadTenant = jest.fn();
const tenant = { id: tenantId };
const setExternalApiAuthorizationLoaded = jest.fn();

    render(
<AppContext.Provider
value={{ tenant, loadTenant, setExternalApiAuthorizationLoaded } as any}
>
<TenantContainer {...props}>Test</TenantContainer>
</AppContext.Provider>,
    ];

    expect(loadTenant).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
expect(screen.queryByText('Test')).toBeInTheDocument();
  }};
it('should load tenant', async () => {
const loadTenant = jest.fn().mockResolvedValue({ id: tenantId });
const tenant = undefined;

import React, { useEffect } from 'react';
import { makeStyles } from '@material-ui/core/styles';
import logo from 'assets/img/AstroIcon.svg';
import styles from 'assets/jss/material-dashboard-pro-react/layouts/adminStyle.js';
import AdminNavbar from 'components/Navbars/AdminNavbar';
import { Sidebar } from 'components/Sidebar/Sidebar';
import { useAuth } from 'context/auth-context';
import { useUser } from 'context/user-context';
import { Outlet, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { SCContainer } from './styles';

const useStyles = makeStyles(styles);

const Dashboard = () => {
// states and functions
const { login } = useAuth();
const userState = useUser();
const push = useNavigate();
// styles
const classes = useStyles();
const mainPanelClasses = classes.mainPanel;

  useEffect(() => {
const userCached = checkForCachedUser(login, userState);
if (!userCached) {
push('/');
}
}, []);

  const isHomeRoute = () => window.location.pathname === '/admin/home';

  return (
<SCContainer>
<Sidebar logoText={''} logo={logo} />
<div
className={mainPanelClasses}
style={{ height: '100vh', overflowY: 'scroll' }}
>
<AdminNavbar />
<div className={classes.content}>
<div className={isHomeRoute() ? classes.home : classes.container}>
            <0utlet />
</div>
</div>
</div>
</SCContainer>
  ];
};

// TODO -> this approach does not account for expiring access tokens
function checkForCachedUser(login, reactUser) {
const cachedUser = localStorage.getItem('astro_user');
const isCached = cachedUser ? true : false;

  if (isCached && !reactUser) {
cachedUser && login(JSON.parse(cachedUser));
}

  return isCached;
}

export default Dashboard;

```typescript
import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { useAppContext } from 'context/app-context';
import FullPageStatus from 'components/FullPageStatus/FullPageStatus';
import { Spinner } from 'commonui';
import { SCLoading } from 'layouts/styles';
import { useParams } from 'react-router-dom';

const TenantContainer = ({
children,
useTenantContainer,
}: {
children: React.ReactNode;
useTenantContainer?: boolean;
}): JSX.Element => {
if (!useTenantContainer) {
return <>{children}</>;
}

  const { tenant, loadTenant, setExternalApiAuthorizationLoaded } =
useAppContext();
const [isLoading, setLoading] = useState<boolean>(false);
const [error, setError] = useState<string>('');
const { tenantId } = useParams();
const isTenantLoaded = tenantId?.toLowerCase() === tenant?.id.toLowerCase();

  const getTenant = async (): Promise<void> => {
if (!isLoading && !isTenantLoaded && tenantId) {
setLoading(true);
try {
await loadTenant(tenantId);
} catch (e) {
const error = e as Error;
setError(error.message);
}
setLoading(false);
setExternalApiAuthorizationLoaded(false);
}
};

  useEffect(() => {
if (!isTenantLoaded) {
getTenant();
}
    ,[};

  if (!tenant && error) {
return <FullPageStatus errorResponse={new Error(error)} />;
}

  if (isLoading || !tenant?.id) {
return (
<SCLoading>
<Spinner />
</SCLoading>
    ];
}

  return <>{children}</>;
};

export { TenantContainer };

import { getManager } from './oidc-client';

[>>
* HttpMethod
* @summary
* Temporary enum that can be replaced by something from the common repo (if it exists) later
* Values are used for client2 http method params - don't change them
>[
export enum HttpMethod {
GET = 'GET',
POST = 'POST',
PUT = 'PUT',
DELETE = 'DELETE',
}

export interface Config {
headers: {
'content-type'?: string;
'X-TENANT-ID': string;

    /** Body should not be nested in headers: */
body?: any;
};
body?: any;
}

function client2(
endpoint: string,
method = 'GET',
{ body, ...customConfig } : any = { },
isJson = true,
Promise<any> {
return window
    .fetch(endpoint, buildConfig(method, { body, isJson, ...customConfig ))
    .then(async (res) =>
      customConfig.errorHandler
        ? customConfig.errorHandler(res)
          await handleErrors2(res),
    ]
    .then((r) => r.json())
    .catch((e) => {
      console.log(e);
      throw e;
    }};
}

function client(
  endpoint: string.
method = 'GET',
{ body, ...customConfig }: any = {},
isJson = true,
): Promise<void | {
ok: any;
status: any;
json: any;
}> {
return window
.fetch(endpoint, buildConfig(method, { body, isJson, ...customConfig }))
.then(async (res) => await handleErrors(res))
.then((r) => {
return {
ok: r.ok,
status: r.status,
json: r.json(),
};
    }}
.catch((error) => console.log(error));
}

function buildConfig(
method = 'GET',
{ body, isJson, ...customConfig }: any = {},
): any {
const defaultHeaders: {
'x-api-key': string | undefined;
'content-type'?: string | undefined;
Authorization?: string | undefined;
} = {
'x-api-key': process.env.REACT_APP_ASTRO_API_KEY,
};

  if (isJson === true && !customConfig['config-type']) {
defaultHeaders['content-type'] = 'application/json';
}

  const cachedToken = localStorage.getItem('astro_token');
if (cachedToken) {
defaultHeaders.Authorization = `Bearer ${cachedToken}`;
}

  const config = {
method: method,
...customConfig,
headers: {
...defaultHeaders,
...customConfig.headers,
},
};
if (body) {
config.body = isJson ? JSON.stringify(body) : body;
}
return config;
}

function generalFetch(
endpoint: string,
method = 'GET',
{ body, ...customConfig }: any = {},
): Promise<any> {
return window
.fetch(
endpoint,
buildConfig(method, { body, isJson: false, ...customConfig }),
    ]
.then(handleErrors)
.catch((error) => {
console.log(error);
    }};
}

const handleErrors = async (response: any): Promise<any> => {
if (!response.ok) {
if (response.status === 401 && !window.location.href.includes('?timeout')) {
client2(
`${process.env.REACT_APP_TOKEN_API_BASE_URL}oauth2/token`,
'DELETE',
).catch((err: Error) => console.log(err));
localStorage.removeItem('astro_user');
localStorage.removeItem('astro_token');
localStorage.setItem('401_redirect', 'true');

      await getManager().signoutRedirect({
post_logout_redirect_uri: window.location.origin,
      }];

      // window.location.replace('/?timeout');
return Promise.reject('401 response');
}

    return response;
}

  return response;
};

const handleErrors2 = async (response: Response): Promise<any> => {
if (!response.ok) {
if (response.status === 401 && !window.location.href.includes('?timeout')) {
client2(
`${process.env.REACT_APP_TOKEN_API_BASE_URL}oauth2/token`,
'DELETE',
).catch((err: Error) => console.log(err));
localStorage.removeItem('astro_user');
localStorage.removeItem('astro_token');
localStorage.setItem('401_redirect', 'true');

      await getManager().signoutRedirect({
post_logout_redirect_uri: window.location.origin,
      }];

      // window.location.replace('/?timeout');

      return;
}

    return response.text().then((e: any) => {
throw Error(e);
    }};
}

  return response;
};

export const handleJsonResponseError = async (response: any): Promise<any> => {
if (!response.ok) {
if (response.status === 401 && !window.location.href.includes('?timeout')) {
client2(
`${process.env.REACT_APP_TOKEN_API_BASE_URL}oauth2/token`,
'DELETE',
).catch((err: Error) => console.log(err));
localStorage.removeItem('astro_user');
localStorage.removeItem('astro_token');
localStorage.setItem('401_redirect', 'true');

      await getManager().signoutRedirect({
post_logout_redirect_uri: window.location.origin,
      }];

      // window.location.replace('/?timeout');

      return;
}
throw await response.json();
}
return response;
};

export { client2, client, generalFetch };

### 617. `C:\DevCode\AstroUI\src\utils\base64-utils.js`

<!-- CODESCAN_FILE_START path="C:\DevCode\AstroUI\src\utils\base64-utils.js" type=".js" size="851" lines="29" chars="851" -->
```javascript
export function fileToBase64(file) {
return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
const reader = new FileReader();
reader.readAsDataURL(file);
reader.onload = () => resolve(reader.result);
reader.onerror = (error) => reject(error);
  }};
}

export function b64toBlob(b64Data, contentType = '', sliceSize = 512) {
const byteCharacters = atob(b64Data);
const byteArrays = [];

  for (let offset = 0; offset < byteCharacters.length; offset += sliceSize) {
const slice = byteCharacters.slice(offset, offset + sliceSize);

    const byteNumbers = new Array(slice.length);
for (let i = 0; i < slice.length; i++) {
byteNumbers[i] = slice.charCodeAt(i);
}

    const byteArray = new Uint8Array(byteNumbers);
byteArrays.push(byteArray);
}

  const blob = new Blob(byteArrays, { type: contentType });
return blob;
}

```

```javascript
class FormValidator {
constructor(validations) {
// validations is an array of validation rules specific to a form
this.validations = validations;
}

  async validateField(field, value) {
// for each validation rule, get everything that doesn't need 'required'.
const rules = this.validations.filter(
(rule) => rule.field === field && !rule.hasOwnProperty('required'),
    ];
let rule;
for (rule of rules) {
const field_value = value.toString();
const validation_method = rule.method;

      // call the validation_method with the current field value as the first
// argument, any additional arguments, and the whole state as a final
// argument.  If the result doesn't match the rule.validWhen property,
// then modify the validation object for the field and set the isValid
// field to false
// console.log(field_value);
if ((await validation_method(field_value, rule.arg)) !== rule.validWhen) {
return { isInvalid: true, message: rule.message };
}
}

    return { isInvalid: false, message: '' };
}

  async validate(state) {
// start out assuming valid
const validation = this.valid();
// for each validation rule
for (const validationRule of this.validations) {
// if the field hasn't already been marked invalid by an earlier rule
if (!validation[validationRule.field].isInvalid) {
// determine the field value, the method to invoke and optional args from
// the rule definition
const field_value = state[validationRule.field].toString();
const validation_method = validationRule.method;

        // call the validation_method with the current field value as the first
// argument, any additional arguments, and the whole state as a final
// argument.  If the result doesn't match the rule.validWhen property,
// then modify the validation object for the field and set the isValid
// field to false
if (
(await validation_method(field_value, validationRule.arg, state)) !==
validationRule.validWhen
        } {
validation[validationRule.field] = {
isInvalid: true,
message: validationRule.message,
};
validation.isValid = false;
}
}
}

    return validation;
}

  async validateForDraft(state) {
// start out assuming valid
const validation = this.valid();

    // for each validation rule
this.validations = this.validations.filter((rule) =>
rule.hasOwnProperty('required') ? rule.required !== 'Active' : true,
    ];
for (const validationRule of this.validations) {
// if the field hasn't already been marked invalid by an earlier rule
if (!validation[validationRule.field].isInvalid) {
// determine the field value, the method to invoke and optional args from
// the rule definition
const field_value = state[validationRule.field].toString();
const validation_method = validationRule.method;

        // call the validation_method with the current field value as the first
// argument, any additional arguments, and the whole state as a final
// argument.  If the result doesn't match the rule.validWhen property,
// then modify the validation object for the field and set the isValid
// field to false
if (
(await validation_method(field_value, validationRule.arg, state)) !==
validationRule.validWhen
        } {
validation[validationRule.field] = {
isInvalid: true,
message: validationRule.message,
};
validation.isValid = false;
}
}
}

    return validation;
}

  valid() {
const validation = {};

    this.validations.map(
(rule) => (validation[rule.field] = { isInvalid: false, message: '' }),
    ];

    return { isValid: true, ...validation };
}
}

export default FormValidator;

public numberToCurrencyOrPercentageString = (
value: number | string,
): string =>
`${(typeof value === 'string'
? parseFloat(this.stripNumberString(value || '0'))
: value
).toLocaleString('en-US', {
minimumFractionDigits: 2,
maximumFractionDigits: 2,
})}`;

  [>>
* genericStringToNumber
* @summary
* Same as above function, but reversed. Returns number value from formatted string
* Will work on integers and formatted strings (e.g. $1.23, 5.38%) too
   >[
public genericStringToNumber = (value: string): number =>
Number(this.stripNumberString(value)) || 0;
}

export const numberUtils = new NumberUtils();

```typescript
import * as Oidc from 'oidc-client';

///////////////////////////////
// OidcClient config
///////////////////////////////
Oidc.Log.logger = console;
Oidc.Log.level = Oidc.Log.INFO;

const settings = {
authority: process.env.REACT_APP_PING_AUTHORITY,
client_id: process.env.REACT_APP_CLIENT_ID,
redirect_uri: `${process.env.REACT_APP_REDIRECT_URL}/auth`,
post_logout_redirect_uri: `${process.env.REACT_APP_REDIRECT_URL}/?logout=true`,
response_type: 'id_token token',
scope: 'openid email profile groups',

  automaticSilentRenew: false,
filterProtocolClaims: true,
loadUserInfo: true,
};

const mgr = new Oidc.UserManager(settings);

function getManager(): Oidc.UserManager {
return mgr;
}

export { getManager };

class RegexUtils {
public isNotApplicableString = (value: string): boolean => {
return /^\s*[Nn]\s*\/?\s*[Aa]\s*$/.test(value);
};
}

export const regexUtils = new RegexUtils();

const PROTOCOL_REGEX = /(^\w+:|^)\/\//;

[>>
>
* @summary
* In production environments the url protocol in a payload will trigger a 403 response.
* Strip it out before saving.
>[
const sanitizeURL = (url: string): string => {
try {
const validURL = new URL(url);
return validURL.host;
} catch (error) {
return url ? url.replace(PROTOCOL_REGEX, '') : url;
}
};

export { sanitizeURL, PROTOCOL_REGEX };

```

}

export default function StateMenu(props: StateMenuProps): JSX.Element {

['Utah', 'UT'],
['Vermont', 'VT'],
['Virginia', 'VA'],
['Washington', 'WA'],
['West Virginia', 'WV'],

];

  const listItems = states.map((ar) => {
return (

// core components
import GridContainer from 'components/Grid/GridContainer';
import GridItem from 'components/Grid/GridItem';
import {
Address,
BaseTenant,
Tenant,
} from 'components/ManageTenant/base-tenant';
import Danger from 'components/Typography/Danger';
import ViewMode from 'context/view-mode';
import CustomFormLabel from '../../../components/CustomFormLabel/CustomFormLabel';
import * as validation from '../validate-create-tenant';
import { ICreateTenantProps } from '../types';

const useStyles = makeStyles(styles);

interface ICreateTenantPropsObject {
tenantProps: ICreateTenantProps;
}

const APIClientCreate: React.FunctionComponent<ICreateTenantPropsObject> = ({
tenantProps,
) = (
const {
viewMode,
newTenant,
setNewTenant,
handleCreateNewTenant,
reformatTenantData,
discardChangesWarning,
validationErrors,
setValidationErrors,
duplicateEntryError,
setDuplicateEntryError,
serviceRequestError,
setServiceRequestError,
} = tenantProps;

  const [openSchemaResponseDialog, setOpenSchemaResponseDialog] =
React.useState<{
isDialogOpen: boolean;
responseMessage: string;
activatingTenant: boolean;
}>({ isDialogOpen: false, responseMessage: '', activatingTenant: false });

  const setField: (k: string, v: string | boolean | null | Address) => void = (
k: string,
v: string | boolean | null | Address,
) => {
setNewTenant((prev: BaseTenant | Tenant) => {
return {
...prev,
[k]: v,
};
    }};
};

  const [errors, setErrors] = React.useState({
legalName: '',
contractNumber: '',
tenantSchema: '',
cnxApiKey: '',
apiSubscriptionKey: '',
apiServiceAccountName: '',
apiServiceAccountPassword: '',
apiContactName: '',
apiContactEmail: '',
  }};

  const setErrorField: (k: string, v: Error) => void = (
k: string,
v: Error,
) => {
setErrors((prev) => {
return {
...prev,
[k]: v,
};
    }};
};

  const [openErrorDialog, setOpenErrorDialog] = React.useState(false);

  const validateTenantAndSchema: (activatingTenant: boolean) => void = async (
activatingTenant,
) => {
const saveData = reformatTenantData();
const schemaIsValid = validation.validateTenantSchema(
saveData.tenantSchema,
setErrorField,
    ];

    if (!schemaIsValid) {
setOpenErrorDialog(true);
return;
}

    const tenantIsValid = validation.validateAllApiClientFields(
activatingTenant,
newTenant,
setErrorField,
    ];

    if (tenantIsValid) {
saveTenant(activatingTenant);
}
};

  const saveTenant: (activatingTenant: boolean) => void = (
activatingTenant: boolean,
) => {
const tenantIsValid = validation.validateAllApiClientFields(
activatingTenant,
newTenant,
setErrorField,
    ];
const { ...saveFicusApiData } = newTenant;
if (tenantIsValid) {
saveFicusApiData.recordState = activatingTenant ? 'Active' : 'Draft';
if (viewMode === ViewMode.Create) {
saveFicusApiData.tenantSchema = `T${saveFicusApiData.contractNumber}`;
}
handleCreateNewTenant(
saveFicusApiData,
setValidationErrors,
setDuplicateEntryError,
setServiceRequestError,
      ];
    else
setOpenErrorDialog(true);
}
};

  const classes = useStyles();

  return (
    >>
<GridContainer>
{validationErrors.length !== 0 && (
<GridItem xs={12} sm={12} md={12}>
<Card>
<CardBody
className={classes.justifyContentCenter}
style={{

>

</ul>
</Danger>

<GridItem xs={12} sm={12} md={6}>
<GridContainer>

<h4 className={classes.cardTitle}>Tenant Information</h4>
</CardText>
</CardHeader>
<CardBody>
<GridContainer>

error={errors.legalName.length !== 0}
className={classes.labelHorizontal}
required={true}
styleType="createTenant"
label="Legal Name"
                      [>
</GridItem>
<GridItem xs={12} sm={10} md={7}>
<CustomInput
id="legalName"
error={errors.legalName.length !== 0}
formControlProps={{
fullWidth: true,
}}
inputProps={{
value: newTenant.legalName,
type: 'text',

                          onChange: (
event: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>,
) => {
// update postal name if it doesn't differ already from legal name
if (newTenant.legalName === newTenant.postalName) {
setField('postalName', event.target.value);
validation.validatePostalName(
event.target.value,
setErrorField,
                               };
}

// update abbreviated name if it doesn't differ already from legal name
if (
newTenant.legalName === newTenant.abbreviatedName
                             } {
setField('abbreviatedName', event.target.value);
validation.validateAbbreviatedName(
event.target.value,
setErrorField,
                               };
}

                            setField('legalName', event.target.value);
validation.validateLegalName(
event.target.value,
setErrorField,
                             };
},
}}
helperText={
errors.legalName.length !== 0

}

                  >>
<GridContainer>

} from 'components/ManageTenant/base-tenant';

import AddressCard from 'views/Components/AddressCard';
import TenantType from 'views/CreateTenant/TenantType';
import CustomFormLabel from '../../../components/CustomFormLabel/CustomFormLabel';
import * as validation from '../validate-create-tenant';
import { ICreateTenantProps } from '../types';
import { DisplayMode } from 'commonui';
import { SCDynamicSegment, SCTenantIntegrationWrapper } from '../styles';
import { TenantIntegrations } from './TenantIntegrations';
import { RecordState } from 'components/LoanAppForm/types';

const useStyles = makeStyles(styles);

interface ICreateTenantPropsObject {
tenantProps: ICreateTenantProps;
}

const CreditUnionCreate: React.FunctionComponent<ICreateTenantPropsObject> = ({
tenantProps,
) = (
const {
viewMode,
newTenant,
setNewTenant,
merchantList,
handleCreateNewTenant,
reformatTenantData,
discardChangesWarning,
    validationErrors,
    setValidationErrors,
    duplicateEntryError,
    setDuplicateEntryError,
    serviceRequestError,
    setServiceRequestError,
= tenantProps;
const { tenant } = useAppContext();
  const [openSchemaResponseDialog, setopenSchemaResponseDialog] =
    React.usestate<l
      isDialogOpen: boolean;
      responseMessage: string:
      activatingTenant: boolean;
    }>({ isDialogopen: false, responseMessage: -', activatingTenant: false });

  const setField: (k: string, v: string | boolean l null | Address) => void = (
    k: string.
    v: string | boolean  null I Address,
) => {
setNewTenant((prev: BaseTenant | Tenant) => {
return {
...prev,
[k]: v,
};
    }};
};

  const [errors, setErrors] = React.useState({
legalName: '',
postalName: '',
abbreviatedName: '',
contractNumber: '',
cuicCustomerNumber: '',
tenantTypeId: '',
numberOfMembers: '',
membershipClassCode: '',
membershipClassDesc: '',
survivingContractNumber: '',
website: '',
phoneNumber: '',
assetRank: '',
assetSize: '',
systemStatus: '',
legalStatus: '',
operationalStatus: '',
homeBanking: '',
homeBankingFiId: '',
physicalAddressLine1: '',
physicalAddressLine2: '',
physicalAddressLine3: '',
physicalAddressCity: '',
physicalAddressCounty: '',
physicalAddressState: '',
physicalAddressZip: '',
physicalAddressCountry: '',
mailingAddressLine1: '',
mailingAddressLine2: '',
mailingAddressLine3: '',
mailingAddressCity: '',
mailingAddressCounty: '',
mailingAddressState: '',
mailingAddressZip: '',
mailingAddressCountry: '',
defaultZipCode: '',
losMembershipRedirectUrl: '',
losMembershipRedirectUrlRefId: '',
tenantSchema: '',
defaultMerchantId: '',
apiContactName: '',
apiContactEmail: '',
homeBankingStorefrontUrl: '',
  }};

  const setErrorField: (k: string, v: Error) => void = (
k: string,
v: Error,
) => {
setErrors((prev) => {
return {
...prev,
[k]: v,
};
    }};
};

  const handleAddressChange = (event: any, addressType: string): void => {
const { id, value, name } = event.target;
const key = id ? id : name;

    const newAddress = {
...newTenant[addressType],
[key]: value,
};

    setField(addressType, newAddress);
};

  const [openErrorDialog, setOpenErrorDialog] = React.useState(false);

  const validateTenantAndSchema: (activatingTenant: boolean) => void = async (
activatingTenant,
) => {
const saveData = reformatTenantData();

    const schemaIsValid = validation.validateTenantSchema(
saveData.tenantSchema,
setErrorField,
    ];

    if (!schemaIsValid) {
setOpenErrorDialog(true);
return;
}

    const tenantIsValid = validation.validateAllFields(
activatingTenant,
newTenant,
setErrorField,
    ];

    if (tenantIsValid) {
const tenantSchemaResponse = await generalFetch(
`${process.env.REACT_APP_TENANT_API_BASE_URL}schema/${saveData.tenantSchema}`,
'GET',
      ];
if (tenantSchemaResponse && tenantSchemaResponse.ok) {
switch (tenantSchemaResponse.status.toString()) {
case '200':
setOpenSchemaResponseDialog({
isDialogOpen: true,
responseMessage:
'The declared schema is already in use. Are you sure you want to declare a schema for this tenant that is already in use?',
activatingTenant,
            }];
break;

          case '204':
saveTenant(activatingTenant);
break;

          default:
setOpenSchemaResponseDialog({
isDialogOpen: true,
responseMessage: 'Something went wrong with the schema service.',
activatingTenant,
            }];
}
}
}
};

  const isStatusDraft = newTenant?.recordState === RecordState.Draft;

  const saveTenant: (activatingTenant: boolean) => void = (
activatingTenant: boolean,
) => {
const tenantIsValid = validation.validateAllFields(
activatingTenant,
newTenant,
setErrorField,
    ];
const saveData = reformatTenantData();
if (tenantIsValid) {
if (activatingTenant && isStatusDraft) {
saveData.recordState = 'Active';
}
handleCreateNewTenant(
saveData,
setValidationErrors,
setDuplicateEntryError,
setServiceRequestError,
      ];
} else {
setOpenErrorDialog(true);
}
};
const tenantStates = isStatusDraft
? [RecordState.Draft, RecordState.New]
: [RecordState.Active, RecordState.Inactive];

  const classes = useStyles();

  return (
    >>
<GridContainer>
{validationErrors.length !== 0 && (
<GridItem xs={12} sm={12} md={12}>
<Card>
<CardBody
className={classes.justifyContentCenter}
style={{
display: 'flex',
}}
>
<Danger>
<Warning
style={{
display: 'inline-block',
marginRight: '10px',
height: '1em',
width: '1em',
top: '.125em',
position: 'relative',
}}
                  [>

                  <p
style={{
display: 'inline-block',
paddingLeft: '0px',
}}
>
<b>There are errors in the form:</b>
</p>

                  <ul data-testid="validation-errors-list">
{validationErrors.map((errorText: string, i: number) => (
<li key={i}>{errorText}</li>
                    ]}}
</ul>
</Danger>
</CardBody>
</Card>
</GridItem>
        }}
<GridItem xs={12} sm={12} md={6}>
<GridContainer>
<GridItem xs={12} sm={12} md={12}>
<Card>

</CardText>

<GridContainer>
<GridItem xs={12} sm={2} md={4}>
<CustomFormLabel
error={errors.legalName.length !== 0}
className={classes.labelHorizontal}
required={true}
styleType="createTenant"
label="Legal Name"
                      [>
</GridItem>
<GridItem xs={12} sm={10} md={7}>
<CustomInput
id="legalName"
error={errors.legalName.length !== 0}
formControlProps={{
fullWidth: true,
}}
inputProps={{
value: newTenant.legalName,
type: 'text',

                          onChange: (
event: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>,
) => {
// update postal name if it doesn't differ already from legal name
if (newTenant.legalName === newTenant.postalName) {
setField('postalName', event.target.value);
validation.validatePostalName(
event.target.value,
setErrorField,
                               };
}

// update abbreviated name if it doesn't differ already from legal name
if (
newTenant.legalName === newTenant.abbreviatedName
                             } {
setField('abbreviatedName', event.target.value);
validation.validateAbbreviatedName(
event.target.value,
setErrorField,
                               };
}

                            setField('legalName', event.target.value);
validation.validateLegalName(
event.target.value,
setErrorField,
                             };
},
}}
helperText={
errors.legalName.length !== 0
? errors.legalName
: undefined
}
                      [>
</GridItem>

<SCTenantIntegrationWrapper>
<SCDynamicSegment>
                      fieldGroups={TenantIntegrations}
                      handleNavigation={()=> {}}
                      readonly={false}
                      contextData={{}}
                      formData={{ ...newTenant }}
                      mode={DisplayMode.LIGHT}
formLayoutColumns={3}

                    [>
</SCTenantIntegrationWrapper>

</GridItem>
</GridContainer>
<GridContainer style={{ textAlign: 'right' }}>
<GridItem xs={12} sm={12} md={11}>
<Button
id="create-tenant-discard-changes"
color="danger"
onClick={discardChangesWarning}
style={{
display: 'inline',
}}
simple
>
Discard Changes
</Button>
<Button
id="create-tenant-save-draft"
style={{
display: isStatusDraft ? 'inline' : 'none',
marginRight: '10px',
color: '#4caf50',
background: 'white',
borderColor: '#4caf50',
borderStyle: 'solid',
}}
color="transparent"
onClick={() => {
viewMode === ViewMode.Create && newTenant.tenantSchema
? validateTenantAndSchema(false)
: saveTenant(false);
}}
>
Save Draft
</Button>
<Button
id="create-tenant-save"
color="success"
onClick={() => {
viewMode === ViewMode.Create && newTenant.tenantSchema
? validateTenantAndSchema(true)
: saveTenant(true);
}}
style={{
display: 'inline',
}}
>
Save
</Button>
</GridItem>
</GridContainer>
<Dialog
onClose={() => {
setOpenSchemaResponseDialog({
isDialogOpen: false,
responseMessage: '',
activatingTenant: false,
          }];
}}
aria-labelledby="schema-warning-title"
open={openSchemaResponseDialog.isDialogOpen}
>
<DialogTitle
id="schema-warning-title"
style={{
marginBottom: '0px',
paddingBottom: '0px',
}}
>
<Danger>
<Warning
style={{
display: 'inline-block',
marginRight: '10px',
height: '1em',
width: '1em',
top: '.125em',
position: 'relative',
}}
/>
<p
style={{
display: 'inline-block',
fontSize: '20px',
}}
>
<b>Tenant Schema Warning</b>
</p>
</Danger>
</DialogTitle>
<DialogContent>
<DialogContentText>
{openSchemaResponseDialog.responseMessage}
</DialogContentText>
<div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between' }}>
<Button
id="create-tenant-discard-changes"
color="danger"
onClick={() =>
setOpenSchemaResponseDialog({
isDialogOpen: false,
responseMessage: '',
activatingTenant: false,
                }}
}
style={{
display: 'inline',
color: '#f44336',
borderColor: '#f44336',
borderStyle: 'solid',
}}
simple
>
Cancel
</Button>
{openSchemaResponseDialog.responseMessage !==
'Something went wrong with the schema service.' && (
<Button
id="create-tenant-save-draft"
style={{
display:
newTenant.recordState !== 'Active' ? 'inline' : 'none',
marginRight: '10px',
color: '#4caf50',
background: 'white',
borderColor: '#4caf50',
borderStyle: 'solid',
}}
color="transparent"
onClick={() => {
saveTenant(openSchemaResponseDialog.activatingTenant);
setOpenSchemaResponseDialog({
isDialogOpen: false,
responseMessage: '',
activatingTenant: false,
                  }};
}}
>
Submit
</Button>
            }}
</div>
</DialogContent>
</Dialog>
<Dialog
onClose={() => {
setOpenErrorDialog(false);
}}
aria-labelledby="form-error-title"
open={openErrorDialog}
>
<DialogTitle
id="form-error-title"
style={{
marginBottom: '0px',
paddingBottom: '0px',
}}
>
<Danger>
<Warning
style={{
display: 'inline-block',
marginRight: '10px',
height: '1em',
width: '1em',
top: '.125em',
position: 'relative',
}}
/>
<p
style={{
display: 'inline-block',
fontSize: '20px',
}}
>
<b>Form Errors</b>
</p>
</Danger>
</DialogTitle>
<DialogContent>
<DialogContentText>
Please correct errors in the highlighted fields.
</DialogContentText>
</DialogContent>
</Dialog>
<Dialog
onClose={() => {
setDuplicateEntryError(false);
}}
aria-labelledby="form-duplicate-title"
open={duplicateEntryError}
>
<DialogTitle
id="duplicate-error-title"
style={{
marginBottom: '0px',
paddingBottom: '0px',
}}
>
<Danger>
<Warning
style={{
display: 'inline-block',
marginRight: '10px',
height: '1em',
width: '1em',
top: '.125em',
position: 'relative',
}}
/>
<p
style={{
display: 'inline-block',
fontSize: '20px',
}}
>
<b>Duplicate Tenant Exists</b>
</p>
</Danger>
</DialogTitle>
<DialogContent>
<DialogContentText>
A tenant with the same legal name and contract number already
exists.
</DialogContentText>
</DialogContent>
</Dialog>
<Dialog
onClose={() => {
setServiceRequestError(false);
}}
aria-labelledby="form-service-error-title"
open={serviceRequestError}
>
<DialogTitle
id="service-error-title"
style={{
marginBottom: '0px',
paddingBottom: '0px',
}}
>
<Danger>
<Warning
style={{
display: 'inline-block',
marginRight: '10px',
height: '1em',
width: '1em',
top: '.125em',
position: 'relative',
}}
/>
<p
style={{
display: 'inline-block',
fontSize: '20px',
}}
>
<b>Service Error</b>
</p>
</Danger>
</DialogTitle>
<DialogContent>
<DialogContentText>
An error occurred saving the tenant.
</DialogContentText>
</DialogContent>
</Dialog>
</>
  ];
};

export default CreditUnionCreate;

### 630. `C:\DevCode\AstroUI\src\views\CreateTenant\CreateTenant.tsx`

<!-- CODESCAN_FILE_START path="C:\DevCode\AstroUI\src\views\CreateTenant\CreateTenant.tsx" type=".tsx" size="14073" lines="446" chars="14073" -->
```typescript
import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
// @material-ui/core components
import { makeStyles } from '@material-ui/core/styles';
// @material-ui/icons
import sweetAlertStyle from 'assets/jss/material-dashboard-pro-react/views/sweetAlertStyle';
import FullPageStatus from 'components/FullPageStatus/FullPageStatus';
// core components
import GridContainer from 'components/Grid/GridContainer';
import GridItem from 'components/Grid/GridItem';
import {
baseTenant,
BaseTenant,
reformatFetchedTenant,
Tenant,
LOS_MEMBERSHIP_REDIRECT_BASE_URL_TEST,
LOS_MEMBERSHIP_REDIRECT_BASE_URL_PROD,
MembershipPreset,
} from 'components/ManageTenant/base-tenant';
import { Merchant } from 'components/MerchantList/types';
import SnackbarContent from 'components/Snackbar/SnackbarContent';
import { useAppContext } from 'context/app-context';
import ViewMode from 'context/view-mode';
import SweetAlert from 'react-bootstrap-sweetalert';
import { client, client2 } from 'utils/api-client';
import { sanitizeURL } from 'utils/sanitization';
import { TenantTypeCodes } from 'types';
import {
TENANT_FICUS_API,
tenantTypesList,
} from 'components/TenantsList/types';
import { ICreateTenantProps } from './types';
import CreditUnionCreate from './CreateTenantTypes/CreditUnionCreate';
import APIClientCreate from './CreateTenantTypes/ApiClientCreate';
import { getAuthorizationAPIRequest, updateAuthorizationAPIRequest } from 'API';
import { useLocation, useNavigate, useParams } from 'react-router-dom';

const useStyles2 = makeStyles(sweetAlertStyle);

const CreateTenant = () => {
const [isFicusApiClient, setIsFicusApiClient] = useState(false);
const [validationErrors, setValidationErrors] = useState<string[]>([]);
const [duplicateEntryError, setDuplicateEntryError] =
useState<boolean>(false);
const [serviceRequestError, setServiceRequestError] =
useState<boolean>(false);

  const params = useParams();
const location = useLocation();
const tenantId = params?.tenantId;
const viewMode: ViewMode =
location.pathname.includes('create') || !tenantId
? ViewMode.Create
: ViewMode.Edit;
const {
setTenant,
setDisplayCreateButton,
setPageTitle,
setExternalApiAuthorizationLoaded,
} = useAppContext();

  // for clarity purposes, creating a more descriptive setter for the tenant context
const setTenantContext: (tenant: Tenant) => void = (tenant: Tenant) =>
setTenant(tenant);

  const [tenantPending, setTenantPending] = useState<boolean | null>(null);
const [checkForUpdate, setCheckForUpdate] = useState<boolean | null>(false);

  const [alert, setAlert] = useState<boolean>(false);
const [newTenant, setNewTenant] = useState<BaseTenant | Tenant>(baseTenant());
const [merchantList, setMerchantList] = useState<Merchant[]>([]);

  const [loading, setLoading] = useState<boolean>(true);
const [errorResponse, setErrorResponse] = useState<Error | null>(null);
const navigate = useNavigate();
const titleList = new Map();

  const loadExitApiAuth = async (
tenantContractNumber: string,
): Promise<boolean> => {
if (tenantContractNumber === ``) return false;
const result = await getAuthorizationAPIRequest(tenantContractNumber);
return result;
};

  // Call the service and get the tenant/api data.
useEffect(() => {
if (!loading) return;

    setDisplayCreateButton(false); // remove navbar create button

    if (location.pathname.includes('create') || !tenantId) {
//This will be a create page.
setNewTenant(baseTenant());
const displayType =
tenantTypesList.find(({ id }) => id === params.tenantType)?.display ||
tenantTypesList[0].display; //defaults to 'credit union' if no variable.

      if (params.tenantType === TENANT_FICUS_API) {
setIsFicusApiClient(true);
setPageTitle(`New ${displayType}`);
setNewTenant((tenant) => ({
...tenant,
ficusApiClient: true,
tenantTypeName: 'API Client',
tenantTypeId: '3',
tenantTypeCode: 'APICLIENT',
        }]};
} else {
setIsFicusApiClient(false);
setPageTitle(`New ${displayType} Tenant`);
}
setLoading(false);
return;
}

    const tenantPromise = client2(
`${process.env.REACT_APP_TENANT_API_BASE_URL}public/tenants/${tenantId}`,
    ]
.then(async (data) => {
const { payload: tenantData }: Tenant = data;
const tenantName = tenantData.legalName;
titleList.set(`${tenantName} > `, `/admin/tenant/${tenantId}/view`);
titleList.set(`Edit `, `/admin/tenant/${tenantId}/edit`);
setPageTitle(titleList);
if (tenantData.tenantTypeCode === TenantTypeCodes.APIClient) {
setIsFicusApiClient(true);
}

        const authorizeToSubmitToLOS = await loadExitApiAuth(
tenantData.contractNumber,
        };

        setNewTenant({
...baseTenant(),
...reformatFetchedTenant(tenantData),
isMailingSameAddress: false,
isAuthorizeToSubmitToLOS: authorizeToSubmitToLOS,
        }];

        setTenantPending(tenantData.creationPending);

        if (tenantData.creationPending) {
setTimeout(() => setCheckForUpdate(true), 10000);
return;
}

        setTenantContext(tenantData); // create Merchants list in sidebar for this tenant
      }]
.catch((error) => {
setErrorResponse(error);
return;
      }];

    const merchantPromise = client2(
`${process.env.REACT_APP_MERCHANT_API_BASE_URL}/merchants?tenantId=${tenantId}`,
    ]
.then((data) => {
setMerchantList(data.payload);
      }]
.catch(() => {
setMerchantList([]);
      }];

    Promise.all([tenantPromise, merchantPromise]).then(() => {
setLoading(false);
    }};
}, [newTenant]);

  // effect hook to see if tenant pending status has changed
useEffect(() => {
if (!checkForUpdate) return;

    client2(
`${process.env.REACT_APP_TENANT_API_BASE_URL}public/tenants/${tenantId}`,
    ]
.then((data) => {
const { payload: tenantData }: Tenant = data;

        if (tenantData.creationPending) {
setCheckForUpdate(null);
setTimeout(() => setCheckForUpdate(true), 10000);
return;
}

        setTenantPending(tenantData.creationPending);
setTenantContext(tenantData); // display sidebar options for this tenant

        client2(
`${process.env.REACT_APP_MERCHANT_API_BASE_URL}/merchants?tenantId=${tenantId}`,
        }
.then((data) => {
setMerchantList(data.payload);
          }]
.catch(() => {
setMerchantList([]);
          }];
      }]
.catch((error) => {
setCheckForUpdate(null);
setErrorResponse(error);
return;
      }];
}, [checkForUpdate]);

  const discardChangesWarning: () => void = () => {
setAlert(true);
};

  const hideAlert: () => void = () => {
setAlert(false);
};

  const discardChanges: () => void = () => {
setAlert(false);
navigate(`/admin/tenants`);
};

  const reformatTenantData: () => Tenant | BaseTenant = () => {
newTenant.addresses = [];

    newTenant.addresses.push({
addressType: 'Physical',
line1: newTenant.physicalAddress.addLine1,
line2: newTenant.physicalAddress.addLine2,
line3: newTenant.physicalAddress.addLine3,
city: newTenant.physicalAddress.addCity,
state: newTenant.physicalAddress.addState,
postalCode: newTenant.physicalAddress.addZip,
county: newTenant.physicalAddress.addCounty,
country: newTenant.physicalAddress.addCountry,
    }};

    if (!newTenant.isMailingSameAddress) {
newTenant.addresses.push({
addressType: 'Mailing',
line1: newTenant.mailingAddress.addLine1,
line2: newTenant.mailingAddress.addLine2,
line3: newTenant.mailingAddress.addLine3,
city: newTenant.mailingAddress.addCity,
state: newTenant.mailingAddress.addState,
postalCode: newTenant.mailingAddress.addZip,
county: newTenant.mailingAddress.addCounty,
country: newTenant.mailingAddress.addCountry,
      }];
    else
newTenant.addresses.push(
JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(newTenant.addresses[0])),
      ];
newTenant.addresses[1].addressType = 'Mailing';
}

    // omit saving the internal format of the addresses
const { ...saveData } = newTenant;

    if (
saveData.numberOfMembers &&
typeof saveData.numberOfMembers !== 'number' &&
saveData.numberOfMembers.length === 0
    ] {
saveData.numberOfMembers = null;
}

    if (saveData.assetRank && saveData.assetRank.length === 0) {
saveData.assetRank = null;
}

    if (saveData.assetSize && saveData.assetSize.length === 0) {
saveData.assetSize = null;
}

    const URL_PROPERTIES_TO_SANITIZE = [
'website',
'vanityUrl',
'offersLandingPageVanityUrl',
'marketingSimRedirectUrl',
];

    for (const property of URL_PROPERTIES_TO_SANITIZE) {
const urlValue = saveData[property];
if (urlValue) {
saveData[property] = sanitizeURL(urlValue);
}
}

    switch (saveData.losMembershipRedirectUrlPreset) {
case MembershipPreset.TEST:
saveData.losMembershipRedirectUrl =
LOS_MEMBERSHIP_REDIRECT_BASE_URL_TEST +
saveData.losMembershipRedirectUrlRefId;
break;
case MembershipPreset.PROD:
saveData.losMembershipRedirectUrl =
LOS_MEMBERSHIP_REDIRECT_BASE_URL_PROD +
saveData.losMembershipRedirectUrlRefId;
break;
default:
saveData.losMembershipRedirectUrl = '';
saveData.memberizationRequired = false;
}

    delete saveData.losMembershipRedirectUrlPreset;
delete saveData.losMembershipRedirectUrlRefId;

    return saveData;
};

  const handleCreateNewTenant: (
saveData: Tenant | BaseTenant,
setStateAction1?: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<string[]>>,
setStateAction2?: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<boolean>>,
setStateAction3?: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<boolean>>,
) => Promise<void> = async (saveData: Tenant | BaseTenant) => {
const baseUrl = `${process.env.REACT_APP_TENANT_API_BASE_URL}tenants`;
let endpoint = baseUrl;
let method = '';
const stringifiedPayload = JSON.stringify({ ...saveData });
const config = {
body: btoa(stringifiedPayload),
headers: { 'content-type': 'text/plain' },
};
const isJson = false;
switch (viewMode) {
case ViewMode.Edit:
endpoint = `${baseUrl}/${saveData.id}`;
method = 'PUT';
break;
case ViewMode.Create:
default:
method = 'POST';
}
const response = await client(endpoint, method, config, isJson);

    if (response && response.ok) {
const responseJSON = await response.json;

      if (!responseJSON || !responseJSON.payload || !responseJSON.payload.id) {
navigate(`/admin/tenants`);
return;
}
const updatedTenant = responseJSON.payload as Tenant;
updatedTenant.isAuthorizeToSubmitToLOS =
saveData.isAuthorizeToSubmitToLOS;
setTenantContext(updatedTenant);

      await updateAuthorizationAPIRequest(
updatedTenant.contractNumber,
saveData.isAuthorizeToSubmitToLOS,
      ];
setExternalApiAuthorizationLoaded(false);

      navigate(`/admin/tenant/${responseJSON.payload.id}/view`);
}

    if (response) {
switch (response.status.toString()) {
case process.env.REACT_APP_HTTP_RESPONSE_VALIDATION_ERROR:
const valErrors = [];
const errorJson = await response.json;
const errorDescriptions = errorJson['result']['errors'];

          for (const i in errorDescriptions) {
valErrors.push(errorDescriptions[i]['message']);
}

          setValidationErrors(valErrors);
return;

        case process.env.REACT_APP_HTTP_RESPONSE_DUPLICATE_ERROR:
setDuplicateEntryError(true);
setValidationErrors([]);
return;

        // TODO -> need to address re-authentication error codes?
default:
setServiceRequestError(true);
setValidationErrors([]);
return;
}
}
};

  const classes2 = useStyles2();

  if (tenantPending) {
return (
<GridContainer>
<GridItem xs={12} sm={12} md={12}>
<SnackbarContent
message="This tenant is still in the process of being created, and cannot be edited."
close={false}
color="warning"
closeNotification={() => {}}
          [>
</GridItem>
</GridContainer>
    ];
}

  if (loading || errorResponse) {
return <FullPageStatus errorResponse={errorResponse} />;
}

  const tenantProps: ICreateTenantProps = {
viewMode,
newTenant,
setNewTenant,
merchantList,
handleCreateNewTenant,
reformatTenantData,
discardChangesWarning,
validationErrors,
setValidationErrors,
duplicateEntryError,
setDuplicateEntryError,
serviceRequestError,
setServiceRequestError,
};

  return (
<GridContainer>
{alert && (
<SweetAlert
// warning
style={{ display: 'block', marginTop: '-100px' }}
title=""
onConfirm={() => discardChanges()}
onCancel={() => hideAlert()}
confirmBtnCssClass={classes2.button + ' ' + classes2.success}
cancelBtnCssClass={classes2.button + ' ' + classes2.danger}
confirmBtnText="Yes, Discard!"
cancelBtnText="Cancel"
showCancel
>
<h3>Your changes will be lost!</h3>
</SweetAlert>
      ]}
<div style={{ width: '100%' }}>
{!isFicusApiClient && <CreditUnionCreate tenantProps={tenantProps} />}
{isFicusApiClient && <APIClientCreate tenantProps={tenantProps} />}
</div>
</GridContainer>
  ];
};

export default CreateTenant;

```

import React from 'react';

import Select from '@material-ui/core/Select';
import MenuItem from '@material-ui/core/MenuItem';

import { makeStyles } from '@material-ui/core/styles';
import styles from 'assets/jss/material-dashboard-pro-react/views/regularFormsStyle';

import * as validation from './validate-create-tenant';

const useStyles = makeStyles(styles);

export const tenantTypes = new Map([
['Credit Union', { id: 1, name: 'Credit Union', code: 'CU' }],
]};

export default function TenantType(props: any) {
const classes = useStyles();

  const listItems: Array<JSX.Element> = [];

  tenantTypes.forEach((tenantType) => {
listItems.push(
<MenuItem
key={tenantType.id}
classes={{
root: classes.selectMenuItem,
selected: classes.selectMenuItemSelected,
}}
value={tenantType.name}
>
{tenantType.name}
</MenuItem>,
    ];
  }};

  return (
<Select
MenuProps={{
className: classes.selectMenu,
}}
classes={{
select: classes.select,
}}
value={props.newTenant.tenantTypeName}
onChange={(event) => {
const value = (event as any)?.target?.value;
props.setField('tenantTypeName', value.toString());

        const tenantEntry = tenantTypes.get(value);
props.setField('tenantTypeId', tenantEntry?.id);
props.setField('tenantTypeCode', tenantEntry?.code);

        validation.validateTenantType(
false,
value.toString(),
props.setErrorField,
        };
}}
inputProps={{
name: 'tenantType',
id: 'tenant-type',
}}
>
<MenuItem
disabled
classes={{
root: classes.selectMenuItem,
}}
>
Choose Type
</MenuItem>
{listItems}
</Select>
  ];
}

export interface ICreateTenantProps {

) => Promise<void>;

### 634. `C:\DevCode\AstroUI\src\views\CreateTenant\validate-create-tenant.js`

```javascript
export function validateLegalName(value, setErrorField) {
if (!value || value.length == 0) {
setErrorField('legalName', 'A value must be provided.');
return false;
}

  if (value.length <= 100) {
setErrorField('legalName', '');
return true;
}

  setErrorField('legalName', 'Value must be less than 100 characters long.');
return false;
}

export function validatePostalName(value, setErrorField) {
if (!value) {
setErrorField('postalName', '');
return true;
}

  if (value.length <= 100) {
setErrorField('postalName', '');
return true;
}

if (!validateWebsite(newTenant.website, setErrorField)) {

  ] {
validationFailed = true;
}

  if (

  if (

  ] {
validationFailed = true;
}

  if (!validateDefaultZipCode(newTenant.defaultZipCode, setErrorField)) {

}

  return !validationFailed;
}

function isInt(value) {
if (!value) return false;

  if (typeof value === 'number') {
return Number.isInteger(value);
}

  if (typeof value === 'string') {
return !isNaN(value) && !value.includes('.');
}

  return false;
}

```typescript
import { useAuth } from 'context/auth-context';
import { useUser } from 'context/user-context';
import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { Helmet } from 'react-helmet';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { getManager } from 'utils/oidc-client';
import './LoginPage.css';
import SnackbarContent from 'components/Snackbar/SnackbarContent';

export const LoginPage = (): JSX.Element => {
const { login } = useAuth();
const userState = useUser();
const push = useNavigate();

  const urlParams: URLSearchParams = new URLSearchParams(
window.location.search,
  ];
const logout: boolean = urlParams.has('logout');

  const [showRedirectMessage, setShowRedirectMessage] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
if (userState) {
console.log('push home');
push('/admin/home');
return;
}

    const wasRedirected = localStorage.getItem('401_redirect');
if (wasRedirected) {
setShowRedirectMessage(true);
localStorage.removeItem('401_redirect');
}
    [;

  const handleLogin: (e: any) => void = (e: any) => {
e.preventDefault();
ping();
};

  const ping: () => void = () => {
const mgr = getManager();
mgr
.signinRedirect()
.then(function (user) {
console.log('sign in process started', user, login);
      }]
.catch(function (err: Error) {
console.log(err);
      }];
};

  return (
<div>
{showRedirectMessage && (
<SnackbarContent
message={
'Your session has expired. Please log back in to access the Astro site.'
}
close={true}
color="danger"
closeNotification={() => setShowRedirectMessage(false)}
        [>
      ]}
<Helmet>
<title>Login to Astro UI</title>
</Helmet>
{logout && (
<div className="loggedOut">You have successfully logged out.</div>
      ]}
<div className="loginWrapper">
<h2>ASTRO UI</h2>
<a className="loginButton" href="!#" onClick={handleLogin}>
SIGN IN
        >[>>
</div>
</div>
  ];
};

export default LoginPage;

```

### 639. `C:\DevCode\AstroUI\src\views\Pages\PingAuthHandler.tsx`

<!-- CODESCAN_FILE_START path="C:\DevCode\AstroUI\src\views\Pages\PingAuthHandler.tsx" type=".tsx" size="1642" lines="56" chars="1642" -->
```typescript
import { useAuth } from 'context/auth-context';
import { useUser } from 'context/user-context';
import React, { useEffect } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { getManager } from '../../utils/oidc-client';

export const PingAuthHandler = (): JSX.Element => {
const { login, logout } = useAuth();
const userState = useUser();
const push = useNavigate();

  useEffect(() => {
if (!userState) {
const mgr = getManager();
mgr
.signinCallback()
.then((result) => {
const memberOfGroups = result?.profile?.memberof?.toString() || '';

          const allowedGroups =
process.env.REACT_APP_MEMBER_GROUPS?.split(',') || [];
if (allowedGroups.length == 0) {
console.log(
'AstroUI must have configured REACT_APP_MEMBER_GROUPS setting',
            };
return;
}

          const isMember = allowedGroups.some((group) =>
memberOfGroups.toString().includes(group),
          };

          if (!isMember) {
console.log('user not a member of allowed AstroUI groups');
logout();
return;
}

          localStorage.setItem('astro_user', JSON.stringify(result));
if (result.access_token) {
localStorage.setItem('astro_token', result.access_token);
}
login(result);
console.log('push admin home');
push('/admin/home');
        }]
.catch((err) => {
console.log('error with authentication');
console.log(err);
        }];
}
}, []);

  return <div></div>;
};

```

        }],
    }};

    fetchSpy.mockResolvedValueOnce({
ok: true,
json: () => Promise.resolve(mockTenant),
    }};

ok: true,
json: () => Promise.resolve(mockTenant),
    }};

    jest.useFakeTimers();

    render(
<TenantContainer useTenantContainer>
<ViewTenant />
</TenantContainer>,
{ wrapper: AppCtxProvider },

jest.runOnlyPendingTimers();
    }};

    await screen.findByText(
'This tenant is still in the process of being created, and cannot be edited.',
    ];

    act(() => {
jest.runOnlyPendingTimers();
    }};

    await waitFor(() =>
expect(
screen.queryByText(
'This tenant is still in the process of being created, and cannot be edited.',
        },
).toBeInTheDocument(),
    ];

    await screen.findByText('legalName1');
  }};
}};

import React, { useEffect } from 'react';
import styled from 'styled-components';
import FullPageStatus from 'components/FullPageStatus/FullPageStatus';
import { AppCtxProvider } from 'context/app-context';
import { useAuth } from 'context/auth-context';

import {
ThemeWrapper,
NotificationProvider,
InjectModalContainer,
ModalProvider,
APIContext,
} from 'commonui';
import { RouterSwitch } from 'layouts/RouterSwitch';
import { useUser } from './context/user-context';
import 'assets/scss/material-dashboard-pro-react.scss?v=1.8.0';
import { withLDProvider } from 'launchdarkly-react-client-sdk';
import AppProviders from './context';
import { getTenantProductsByCategory } from './API';
import { RouterProvider, createBrowserRouter } from 'react-router-dom';

const themeOverrides = {
G1: '#05A493',
};

[>>
* Reset fonts to match material ui
* CommonUI ThemeWrapper will override with GTAmerica
>[
const FontReset = styled.div`
font-family: 'Roboto', 'Helvetica', 'Arial', sans-serif;
`;

const App = (): JSX.Element => {
const { login } = useAuth();
const userState = useUser();

  useEffect(() => {
const cachedUser = localStorage.getItem('astro_user');
const isCached = cachedUser ? true : false;

    if (isCached && !userState) {
cachedUser && login(JSON.parse(cachedUser));
}
}, []);

  const Root = () => (
<React.Suspense fallback={<FullPageStatus />}>
<APIContext.Provider value={{ getTenantProductsByCategory } as any}>
<AppProviders>
<AppCtxProvider>
<NotificationProvider>
<ThemeWrapper
themeOverrides={themeOverrides}
themeLoading={false}
>
<FontReset>



















//define a constant anonymous user to prevent wasting launchdarkly MAUs
user: {
key: 'ASTROUI',
},
})(App);

export default AppLDP;

### 658. `C:\DevCode\AstroUI\README.md`

<!-- CODESCAN_FILE_START path="C:\DevCode\AstroUI\README.md" type=".md" size="2395" lines="59" chars="2381" -->
```markdown
# Introduction

AstroUI is an implementor facing interface for configuring tenants and their product offerings.

# Getting Started

## Install

1. Clone the project from Azure Repo
`git clone https://cunamutual@dev.azure.com/cunamutual/H2IC/_git/AstroUI`
2. `npm install`

## Available Scripts

In the project directory, you can run:

### `npm start`

Runs the app in the development mode.<br />
Open [http://localhost:3000](http://localhost:3000) to view it in the browser.

The page will reload if you make edits.<br />
You will also see any lint errors in the console.

### `npm test`

Launches the test runner in the interactive watch mode.<br />
See the section about [running tests](https://facebook.github.io/create-react-app/docs/running-tests) for more information.

### `npm run build`

Builds the app for production to the `build` folder.<br />
It correctly bundles React in production mode and optimizes the build for the best performance.

The build is minified and the filenames include the hashes.<br />
Your app is ready to be deployed!

See the section about [deployment](https://facebook.github.io/create-react-app/docs/deployment) for more information.

### `npm run eject`

**Note: this is a one-way operation. Once you `eject`, you can’t go back!**

If you aren't satisfied with the build tool and configuration choices, you can 'eject' at anytime. This command will remove the single build dependency from your project.

Instead, it will copy all the configuration files and the transitive dependencies (webpack,Babel, EsLint, etc) right into your project so you have full control over them. All of thecommands except 'eject' will still work, but they will point to the copied scripts so you cantweak them. At this point you're on your own.

You don't have to ever use 'eject'. The curated feature set is suitable for small and middledeployments, and you shouldn't feel obligated to use this feature. However we understand thatthis tool wouldn't be useful if you couldn't customize it when you are ready for it.

You can learn more in the [Create React App documentation](https://facebook.github.io/create-react-app/docs/getting-started).

To learn React, check out the [React documentation](https://reactjs.org/).

Provides an implementor facing interface for configuring tenants and their product offerings.
